Specifically, Cadence outperforms vanilla agents by 25.33\% (+76 resolved tasks) on mini-swe-agent and 15.67\% (+47 resolved tasks) on Moatless, while maintaining competitive token efficiency compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [33] [TestPrism: Rethinking Test Evaluation Beyond a Single Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12289)
*Han Li,Lingxiang Hu,Jiacheng Huang,Ziqian Jiang,Jingkai Luo,Wei Gao,Yunfan Tan,Zun Wang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 TestPrism 基准（300 个测试任务、3000 个候选实现，有效/无效各半）及 Joint Success Function 指标，指出单一参考解评估会高估测试质量；并进一步提出 TestHelix 方法，通过异构测试-修复对合成、同伴交叉验证与递归自改进显著提升联合成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 LLM 编码代理在测试生成上进展显著，但普遍采用"针对单一参考解评估测试"的做法，忽略了其他合法实现，从而高估测试质量。需要能同时惩罚过宽/过严测试的评测范式与更强的测试生成方法。

Method: 1) 构建 TestPrism：300 个测试任务，来自 17 个来源，配 3000 个候选实现（有效与无效各半）。2) 提出 Joint Success Function 主指标：要求生成测试①在初始程序状态上失败、②接受所有有效候选、③拒绝所有无效候选。3) 提出 TestHelix：异构合成"测试-修复"对 + 同伴交叉验证 + 递归自改进（RSI）。

Result: 在十四种基线编码代理配置上，Joint Success Function 仅达 28.00%，而单一参考解成功率高达 59.67%，差距表明现有评估明显高估测试质量。错误分析揭示三类问题：遗漏行为、不受支持的断言、错误的测试构造。TestHelix 在两个模型上相对原生对比方法将 Joint Success Function 提升 8.67–9.00 个百分点。

Conclusion: 单一参考解的测试评估不可靠，会系统性高估测试质量；采用多候选实现与联合成功指标（TestPrism）能更严格地衡量测试判别力，而 TestHelix 的异构合成、交叉验证与自改进机制可有效缩小这一差距。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents have advanced test generation across diverse programming tasks. However, the common practice of evaluating tests against a single reference solution overlooks alternative valid implementations and can overstate test quality. We introduce TestPrism, comprising 300 test tasks from 17 sources and 3000 candidate implementations, evenly split between valid and invalid solutions. Its primary metric, Joint Success Function, requires the generated tests to fail on the initial program state, accept every valid candidate, and reject every invalid candidate. Across fourteen baseline coding agent configurations, Joint Success Function reaches only 28.00%, whereas single reference success reaches 59.67%. Our analysis reveals missed behaviors, unsupported assertions, and faulty test construction. To address these weaknesses, we introduce TestHelix, which combines heterogeneous synthesis of test and repair pairs with peer cross validation and recursive self improvement (RSI). Across two models, TestHelix improves Joint Success Function by 8.67 to 9.00 percentage points over the native harness comparators in the TestHelix evaluation

</details>

### [34] [Freeze the Decoder, Heal the Encoder: Parameter-Efficient Adaptation for SVD-Based KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10552)
*Yufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了参数高效微调比较中"共享学习率"的混淆偏差，并在SVD KV-cache压缩场景中验证：修正该偏差后，仅训练编码器的方案与替代方案达到性能持平，同时节省3倍可训练参数与优化器状态内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数高效微调（PEFT）中，比较不同可训练参数量差异巨大的方案时常采用单一共享学习率，这会造成不公平比较：共享学习率既压抑大参数方案的均值又放大其方差，从而虚假地制造出最小参数方案的显著优势。作者希望指出并纠正这一方法学上的混淆。

Method: 在具体设定——基于SVD的事后KV-cache压缩中展开研究：将预训练模型K/V权重分解为下投影（编码器）与上投影（解码器），转换为低秩（MHA-latent-attention风格）缓存，再通过短程"healing"微调恢复截断造成的精度损失。对比仅训练编码器、仅训练解码器、同时训练两者三种方案，分别在共享学习率与每方案独立调参学习率下评估，并配合视觉语言模型（Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct）与纯文本测试平台跨两骨干网络复现。

Result: 在共享学习率下，冻结解码器、仅healing编码器看似显著优于其他方案；但一旦为每个方案分配各自调优的学习率，该优势即消失，仅编码器healing与替代方案持平，且实现真正可测的收益：可训练参数减少3倍、优化器状态内存减少3倍。该结论在单一压缩比下、每配置三种子、视觉语言模型及两个文本骨干网络上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 仅编码器healing是一种低内存的即插即用方案，可用于在训练阶段改造低秩KV-cache压缩；同时，本文所记录并纠正的"共享学习率陷阱"对于任何比较可训练参数量不同微调方案的研究都具有警示意义。

Abstract: Comparing parameter-efficient fine-tuning recipes under a single, shared learning rate is a common but flawed practice: when the arms being compared have very different trainable-parameter counts, a shared rate can simultaneously depress the larger arms' means and inflate their variance, manufacturing a large, seemingly multi-seed-significant advantage for the smallest arm that is not a real effect. We document this confound in a concrete setting: post-hoc SVD-based KV-cache compression, where an already-pretrained model is converted to a low-rank (multi-head-latent-attention-style) cache by factorizing its key/value weights into a down-projection ("encoder") and an up-projection ("decoder"), after which a short fine-tune ("healing") recovers the accuracy lost to truncation. Under a shared learning rate, freezing the decoder and healing only the encoder looks like a clear win over healing the decoder or both factors; once every arm is given its own tuned learning rate, that apparent advantage disappears, and encoder-only healing instead reaches parity with the alternatives, at a real, measured saving of 3x fewer trainable parameters and 3x less optimizer-state memory. We verify this parity with per-arm learning-rate tuning and three seeds per configuration on a vision-language model (Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct), at the one compression ratio this protocol covers, and replicate it on a text-only testbed across two backbones. Encoder-only healing is therefore a lower-memory drop-in recipe for retrofitting low-rank KV-cache compression at training time, and the shared-learning-rate pitfall we document and correct is a cautionary result for comparing any fine-tuning recipes whose arms differ in trainable-parameter count.

</details>


### [35] [SPERA: Spherical Prior EEG Foundation Model with Geometry- and Frequency-Aware Latent Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10571)
*Minsu Kim,Ye-Sung Kim,Hyeseong Jeon,Wooseok Hyung,Joshua Lee,Chang-Hwan Im*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SPERA，一种采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在潜空间进行预测的EEG基础模型，引入Legendre多项式空间先验编码头皮电极几何，并在约80,000小时、29,048名被试、106个数据集上预训练，在九项下游任务中取得最高平均平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG可无创测量神经活动，但被试、设备与电极蒙太奇的异质性使构建通用EEG模型困难。现有EEG基础模型多依赖基于观测信号的重构目标，而观测信号同时包含神经与非神经成分，导致表征学习受非神经成分干扰。

Method: 采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），在潜空间进行预测而非重构原始信号。引入Legendre多项式空间先验并融入注意力机制，以编码不同的头皮电极几何。另设两个适配EEG的组件：分解式时间与空间注意力，并与周期性全注意力块交错；以及关系型频谱正则化器，使潜相似性结构与频谱视图对齐。

Result: 在约80,000小时EEG、29,048名被试、106个数据集上预训练。在涵盖临床、认知与BCI应用的九项下游任务中，取得最高平均平衡准确率。在线性探测下表现出较强的参数效率，并在不同记录条件下具有稳健性。

Conclusion: SPERA通过潜空间预测与空间先验设计，展现出作为通用EEG分析骨干的潜力，兼顾性能、参数效率与跨条件稳健性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a non-invasive measure of ongoing neural activity, but building general-purpose EEG models remains challenging due to the heterogeneity of subjects, devices, and electrode montages. Existing EEG foundation models predominantly rely on reconstruction-based objectives defined on the observed signal, which contains both neural and non-neural components. We introduce SPERA (Spherical Prior EEG Representation Architecture), an EEG foundation model that adopts the joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA) to predict in latent space. SPERA introduces a Legendre-polynomial spatial prior, incorporated into attention to encode varying scalp electrode geometries. Two further components adapt the model to EEG: factorized temporal and spatial attention interleaved with periodic full-attention blocks, and a relational spectral regularizer aligning latent similarity structure with spectral views. Pretrained on approximately 80,000 hours of EEG from 29,048 subjects across 106 datasets, SPERA achieves the highest average balanced accuracy across nine downstream tasks spanning clinical, cognitive, and BCI applications. SPERA further exhibits strong parameter efficiency under linear probing and robustness across varying recording conditions, suggesting its potential as a general-purpose backbone for diverse EEG analyses.

</details>


### [36] [Coverage, Not Difficulty, Sets How Much Synthetic Data an Activation Probe Needs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10594)
*Ankush Checkervarty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 激活探针的学习曲线由"覆盖度"而非单类难度决定：每个分布各类样本的中位半增益规模仅需7–11个同类样本，概念间样本需求差异主要源于跨类迁移程度（高风险最高、有害次之、指令最低）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活探针通常用合成对话训练，但所需样本量尚未明确。已有工作建议将生成预算花在"广度"（更多种类的数据）而非"深度"（每类更多样本）上，然而这一建议缺乏对"深度"需求量的定量刻画，也未说明不同监测概念之间需求差异的来源。

Method: 在三个监测概念（高风险情境、对人有害的回复、不遵循用户指令的回复）上，对14个留出评估分布和4个探针模型追踪学习曲线，样本量从10到590，同时变化生成器LLM与提示细节。将概念所需的深度操作化为拟合曲线的"半增益规模"（half-gain size），即获得一半增益所需的样本数，并分解概念、分布、生成器、探针模型、提示细节对半增益规模方差的贡献。

Result: 高风险与有害探针在Gemma-3-27B-IT上从80个样本起即距平台仅几个百分点，指令探针需要数倍样本，该顺序在三个较小探针模型与真实dev样本上保持一致。概念与分布解释半增益规模方差的42–45%，生成器、探针模型与提示细节合计不足10%。每个分布（各对应一类）在三个概念下的中位半增益规模均为7–11个同类合成样本。概念间差异主要来自样本的跨类迁移程度：高风险几乎完全迁移，有害较低，指令最低，这也解释了生成样本与真实样本上的大部分概念间差距。

Conclusion: 广度的价值因概念而异：指令概念下各类互不覆盖，需要许多类数据；高风险概念下类间迁移几乎完全，类数据近乎冗余。决定半增益规模的是覆盖度，即该分布达到饱和所需的同类样本数，而非单类难度。

Abstract: Activation probes that monitor deployed language models are trained on synthetic conversations, and how many a probe needs is open. We trace learning curves over 10-590 synthetic samples for three monitoring concepts, high-stakes situations, replies harmful to a person, and replies that do not follow the user's instruction, on fourteen held-out evaluation distributions and four probe models, varying the generator LLM and the prompt's detail. The need is set by what is monitored: probes for high-stakes and harmful are within a few hundredths of their plateau from 80 samples on Gemma-3-27B-IT, instruction probes need several times as many, and the ordering holds on three smaller probe models and on real samples (from dev set). Prior work advises spending a generation budget on breadth, more kinds of data, over depth, more of each kind. We read the depth a concept needs as the half-gain size of a fitted curve, the number of samples at which half the gain is in hand. Concept and distribution account for 42-45% of its variance, the generator, probe model, and prompt detail for under 10%. What sets the value of the half-gain size is coverage, not per-kind difficulty: the number of samples of its own kind a distribution needs to saturate. Every kind, one per evaluation distribution, has a median half-gain size of 7-11 own-kind synthetic samples under all three concepts alike. What differs is how far samples of one kind transfer to the concept's other kinds, almost fully under high-stakes, less under harmful, and least under instruction, which accounts for most of the gap between concepts on generated and real samples. Breadth therefore pays differently by concept: many kinds are necessary under instruction, where no kind covers another, and nearly redundant under high-stakes, where one kind covers the rest. We release the evaluation suites, dev sets, and generated sets.

</details>


### [37] [Temporal transformer CAN encoder with federated lightweight heads for anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10613)
*Konstantinos Gyftodimos,Kyriakos Chiotis,Elena Politi,George Dimitrakopoulos,Eirini Liotou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于时序Transformer CAN编码器与联邦轻量头的隐私保护车载网络异常检测框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代车辆依赖大量ECU通过CAN总线持续通信，通信具有高速、结构化、重复性的特点，时序、负载值或消息模式的细微变化即可指示异常活动。但无论源于错误、故障还是人为干扰，这些异常往往很隐蔽，传统逐条处理消息或依赖人工规则的检测方法难以识别。

Method: 以时序Transformer作为CAN编码器，学习信号随时间的演化规律，从而捕捉细微的时序与上下文异常；同时引入联邦学习机制，使多辆车或多个ECU在不交换原始CAN数据的前提下协同训练共享模型；检测端采用联邦轻量头结构以兼顾效率。

Result: 在开源数据集上进行的实验表明，该框架在保持效率与隐私的同时，取得了稳健的异常检测性能。

Conclusion: 联邦学习与时序序列建模的结合，为车载网络异常检测提供了一种兼具鲁棒性、效率与隐私保护的可行方案。

Abstract: Modern vehicles rely on large numbers of Electronic Control Units (ECUs) that constantly exchange information over the Controller Area Network (CAN) bus. Due to the rapidity, structure, and repetition of this communication, even slight variations in timing, payload values, or message patterns can point to unusual activity. Whether due to errors, malfunctions, or deliberate interference, these anomalies are frequently subtle and challenging to identify with conventional methods that handle messages separately or rely on manually created rules. Motivated by this gap, we present a privacy-preserving framework for anomaly detection in in-vehicle networks, based on a Temporal Transformer CAN Encoder with Federated Lightweight Heads, to better capture these irregularities. The detection of subtle temporal and contextual anomalies is made possible by a lightweight Transformer encoder that learns how these signals evolve over time, while a federated learning mechanism enables several vehicles or ECUs to work together to improve a shared model without exchanging raw CAN data. This combination of federated learning and temporal sequence modeling provides robust anomaly detection performance while maintaining efficiency and privacy, according to experiments conducted on open-source datasets.

</details>


### [38] [When Routing Reveals Membership: Privacy Leakage from MoE Router Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10616)
*Yixin Tan,Jiayang Liu,Lu Sun,Yuke Hu,Zheng Li,Rui Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种路由增强的成员推理攻击：将常规输出侧信号与聚合路由特征结合，用影子模型训练成员分类器。在3种MoE架构、3个数据域的9个设置中，TPR@1%FPR提升2.7–9.4个百分点，且在全量微调、冻结路由训练、LoRA、指令微调下均持续存在。机理分析表明泄漏源于微调将成员信息注入隐表示，路由器即使参数冻结也会投影该信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE推理时产生的路由信息常被记录或暴露用于监控、调试、负载分析与安全审计。与普通模型输出不同，这类遥测揭示了模型内部计算的视图，由此引发隐私疑问：它能否揭示某样本是否参与了对已部署模型的微调。论文旨在验证路由遥测是否构成新的隐私泄露面。

Method: 提出路由增强的成员推理攻击：结合传统输出侧信号与聚合路由特征，并在独立微调的影子模型上学习成员分类器，再迁移至目标模型。评测覆盖3种MoE架构、3个数据域，对照强输出信号集成基线。同时在多种微调方式（全量微调、冻结路由训练、LoRA、指令微调）及受限条件下（仅离散专家选择、受限遥测、单个影子模型）验证泄漏的稳健性，并进行机理分析与遥测扰动实验。

Result: 在所有9个设置中，路由遥测相对强输出信号集成一致提升成员推理效果，TPR@1%FPR提高2.7–9.4个百分点。泄漏在多种微调方式下持续存在，且在仅有离散专家选择、受限遥测或单个影子模型时仍可观测。机理分析显示泄漏不依赖路由器的特定记忆：微调把成员信息引入隐表示，路由器即使参数冻结也会暴露该信号的投影；对遥测施加扰动时，只有当其保真度下降，额外泄漏才随之减少。

Conclusion: 路由遥测会把一种运维信号转化为微调MoE模型额外的隐私攻击面，提示在日志记录、监控与审计场景中需将路由信息纳入隐私风险评估与防护考量。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models produce routing information during inference that may be logged or exposed for monitoring, debugging, load analysis, and safety auditing. Unlike ordinary model outputs, this telemetry reveals a view of the model's internal computation, raising a privacy question: can it reveal whether an example was used to fine-tune the deployed model? We introduce a router-augmented membership inference attack that combines conventional output-side signals with aggregated routing features and applies a membership classifier learned from independently fine-tuned shadow models to the target model. Across three MoE architectures and three data domains, router telemetry consistently improves membership inference over a strong output-signal ensemble, increasing TPR at 1\% FPR by 2.7--9.4 percentage points across all nine settings. The leakage persists across full fine-tuning, frozen-router training, LoRA, and instruction tuning, and remains observable with only discrete expert selections, restricted telemetry, or a single shadow model. Mechanistic analysis further shows that the leakage does not require router-specific memorization: fine-tuning introduces membership information into hidden representations, while the router exposes a projection of this signal even when its parameters are frozen. Perturbing the telemetry reduces this additional leakage only as its fidelity degrades. Our results show that router telemetry can turn an operational signal into an additional privacy surface for fine-tuned MoE models.

</details>


### [39] [Self-Organization from Constrained Geometric Radiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10621)
*Ming Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出一种无需外部驱动的闭系统自组织机制：在耦合度规演化系统中，由几何辐射引发约束诱导自组织，并给出四阶段循环、楔形吸引子及三个定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自组织范式均依赖外部能量流、温度淬火或缓慢驱动，无法解释闭系统中动态有序如何自发出现，因此需要建立新的闭系统自组织范式。

Method: 通过耦合度规演化系统模拟，识别约束诱导自组织过程；提出四个联合充分条件（不可逆几何视界、量子相干持续注入应力、不相容曲率间的内生几何张力、有效涨落）；并给出三个定理及约束诱导自组织定理的证明；进行系统扫描与多配置、多噪声、多几何流的普适性验证。

Result: 模拟揭示普遍四阶段循环：应力累积、超指数辐射、混沌坍缩、收敛至分形极限环；发现楔形吸引子，具五个量子化曲率态与分形微涨落；证明几何视界定理、几何能量耗散定理（蕴含闭系统中波状熵演化）及辐射作为相变通道定理；确立约束诱导自组织定理，保证该循环以概率一完成；系统扫描发现临界噪声阈值与辐射起始的幂律标度；在12种配置、多种噪声类型和三种几何流中验证普适性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了闭系统自组织的新范式，并在经典非线性约束与引力视界之间构造了精确数学对偶。

Abstract: How does dynamic order emerge spontaneously in closed systems without external driving? Existing paradigms all require external energy flows, temperature quenching, or slow driving. Here we report constraint-induced self-organization via geometric radiation in coupled metric evolution systems. Simulations reveal a universal four-stage cycle: stress accumulation, super-exponential radiation, chaotic collapse, and convergence to a fractal limit cycle, a novel attractor topology we term the wedge-shaped attractor, with five quantized curvature states and fractal micro-fluctuations. We identify four jointly sufficient conditions: an irreversible geometric horizon, persistent stress injection from quantum coherence, endogenous geometric tension between incompatible curvatures, and effective fluctuations. Their synergy triggers a critical avalanche at the horizon boundary. We prove three theorems: the Geometric Horizon Theorem, the Geometric Energy Dissipation Theorem (implying wave-like entropy evolution in closed systems), and the Radiation as Phase Transition Channel Theorem. We further establish the Constraint-Induced Self-Organization Theorem: these conditions guarantee the complete cycle with probability one. Systematic scans reveal a critical noise threshold and power-law scaling of radiation onset. We verify universality across 12 configurations, multiple noise types, and three geometric flows. This work establishes a new paradigm for closed-system self-organization, forging an exact mathematical duality between classical nonlinear constraints and gravitational horizons.

</details>


### [40] [Recurrent Self-Improvement: Dynamic Cross-Loop On-Policy Distillation for Looped Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10623)
*Yi Wang,Rui Qian,Yu Li,Haoyang Yao,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoopOPD，一种跨循环在线策略蒸馏框架，利用LoopLM内部终端循环作为"计算特权教师"监督中间循环学生，并提出动态版本D-LoopOPD实现循环自改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoopLM通过复用共享参数进行循环计算来实现参数高效的推理扩展，但其后训练仍具挑战。现有方法或依赖稀疏/扩展成本高的奖励监督，或依赖外部教师/特权信息，导致教师可用性受限或师生上下文不匹配。

Method: LoopOPD在学生自生成rollout上，用冻结的终端循环策略作为"计算特权教师"指导中间循环学生，无需外部教师或特权信息即可提供密集监督。D-LoopOPD进一步在共享参数更新时持续刷新终端循环教师，实现循环自改进。作者还刻画了蒸馏更新跨循环深度的传播，并推导出单次更新即可在两个循环深度同时获得局部改进的充分条件。

Result: 在Ouro-Thinking模型上，LoopOPD提升了数学推理能力，D-LoopOPD通过动态教师更新获得进一步提升。仅用数学数据训练，模型在通用推理和代码生成基准上也有改进。

Conclusion: 循环计算可作为LoopLM的有效监督来源。作者将公开代码与模型检查点。

Abstract: Looped Language Models (LoopLMs) offer a parameter efficient approach to scaling reasoning by reusing shared parameters across recurrent computation steps. Despite their promise, effective post-training of LoopLMs remains challenging. Existing approaches either provide reward based supervision that is sparse or costly to extend across loops, or rely on external teachers or privileged information, leading to limited teacher availability or teacher-student context mismatch. To address these limitations, we introduce LoopOPD, a cross-loop on-policy distillation framework that uses additional recurrent computation within a LoopLM as its own source of supervision. LoopOPD uses a frozen terminal loop policy as a compute privileged teacher for an intermediate loop student on student generated rollouts, providing dense supervision without an external teacher or privileged information. We further propose Dynamic LoopOPD (D-LoopOPD), which continually refreshes the terminal loop teacher as the shared model parameters are updated, enabling recurrent self-improvement. We characterize how distillation updates propagate across loop depths and derive sufficient conditions under which a single update yields simultaneous local improvement at both loop depths. Experiments on Ouro-Thinking models show that LoopOPD improves mathematical reasoning, while D-LoopOPD yields further gains through dynamic teacher updates. Despite being trained only on mathematical data, the resulting models also improve on general reasoning and code generation benchmarks, demonstrating that recurrent computation can serve as an effective source of supervision for LoopLMs. Our code and model checkpoints will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [41] [Exact SO(3)-Equivariant Isotropic Kernels for Rotation-Robust Neural Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10626)
*Ridham Patel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出IKNO，一种紧凑的图神经算子，通过各向同性核从旋转不变标量与随数据旋转的向量方向构建局部交互，实现严格等变的3D Navier–Stokes预测，在保持精度的同时消除坐标依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量值PDE的神经代理模型虽能拟合训练数据，但当同一物理状态以旋转后的坐标表示时会改变预测，即缺乏旋转等变性（坐标依赖性），这是泛化可靠性的重要缺陷。作者在三维Navier–Stokes、非规则采样点的场景下研究并解决该失效问题。

Method: 设计Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO)：一种紧凑图模型，用旋转不变的标量量结合随数据旋转的向量方向构造局部交互（各向同性核），从而保证输出（速度变化）随输入位置与速度的旋转而精确同向旋转。实验在固定于模型设计后的留出测试集上，与无约束图模型、旋转感知的Tensor Field Network及参数匹配的图模拟器对比。

Result: 无约束图模型在随机旋转样本上训练后仅减少但未消除坐标依赖；IKNO达到数值精度级别的等变一致性，预测精度与通用旋转感知的Tensor Field Network相当，参数量减少5.6倍，并优于参数匹配的图模拟器。

Conclusion: 紧凑的、面向PDE特化的模型能够在不为预测精度付出代价的前提下消除坐标依赖性，说明将物理对称性内嵌到模型结构中比依赖数据增强或通用等变架构更高效。

Abstract: Neural surrogates for vector-valued partial differential equations can fit training data yet change their predictions when the same physical state is expressed in a rotated coordinate frame. We study this failure on three-dimensional Navier--Stokes dynamics observed at irregularly placed points. We introduce the Invariant-Conditioned Isotropic Kernel Neural Operator (IKNO), a compact graph model that builds local interactions from scalar quantities unchanged by rotation and vector directions that rotate with the data. Consequently, rotating the positions and velocities rotates the predicted velocity change in exactly the same way. On a held-out test set fixed after model design, training unconstrained graph models on randomly rotated examples reduces but does not eliminate their coordinate dependence. In contrast, IKNO is consistent to numerical precision, matches the forecasting accuracy of a general rotation-aware Tensor Field Network with $5.6$ times fewer parameters, and outperforms a parameter-matched graph simulator. These results show that a compact, PDE-specialized model can remove coordinate dependence without sacrificing forecasting accuracy.

</details>


### [42] [Sample-Efficiency of Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10627)
*Kevin Riehl,Shaimaa K. El-Baklish,Fan Wu,Anastasios Kouvelas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究系统评估了Kolmogorov-Arnold网络（KAN）在强化学习中的样本效率，发现在Feynman数据集与Gymnasium基准上，KAN能以少40%的样本达到相近性能，训练过程中相对性能提升最高达50%，且该增益对奖励噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习相较经典控制方法性能提升显著，但实际应用中样本获取成本高昂，样本效率成为核心挑战。KAN作为新提出的架构，在控制问题中能以更高的参数效率和可解释性学习物理关系，因此值得系统研究其在提升样本效率方面的潜力。

Method: 作者通过计算实验系统研究样本效率，覆盖Feynman数据集和Gymnasium强化学习基准，对比KAN与MLP架构在不同奖励噪声水平下的表现。

Result: 使用KAN架构可在少40%样本的情况下达到相似性能；训练过程中相对性能提升最高达50%；上述增益在不同奖励噪声水平下保持稳健。

Conclusion: KAN架构在实现更样本高效的强化学习方面具有潜力，其增益稳健且显著，为降低实际应用中的样本成本提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning has achieved substantial performance gains over classical control approaches. Yet, a central challenge to learning in real-world applications is acquiring costly samples. Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks are a recently proposed architecture that can learn physical relationships in control problems effectively, with significantly higher parameter efficiency and interpretability when compared to Multi-Layer-Perceptron architectures. In this work, we systematically study sample-efficiency using computational experiments, covering the Feynman dataset and the Gymnasium RL benchmark. The results show that similar performance can be achieved with 40% fewer samples using the Kolmogorov-Arnold architecture, and that relative performance improvements up to 50% occur during the training process. The observed gains are robust to varying levels of noise in rewards. These results highlight the potential of the Kolmogorov-Arnold architectures for more sample-efficient reinforcement learning. Code: https://github.com/DerKevinRiehl/neurips26_kan_training

</details>


### [43] [Phase-HDC: Replacing Optimizer History with Gradient Thresholds in Discrete Phase Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10630)
*Ahmed Nebli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Phase-HDC 提出一种无优化器状态（仅存模型本身）的训练方法：对低比特角度参数每次最多移动一步，方向取当前梯度符号，且仅当梯度足够大时执行。该规则被证明是带固定代价的一阶损失模型的精确解，在保持与 6-bit Adam 相近精度的同时节省约 3 倍内存，较 float32 Adam 节省 16–23 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练紧凑模型所需内存远超存储模型本身，原因是优化器需要保存历史梯度记录。对于参数为低比特角度的超维分类器（phase memory），这些记录所占内存是模型本身的数倍。作者希望探索：能否在只存储模型本身、不保存任何优化器状态的前提下完成训练，从而大幅降低训练内存开销。

Method: 提出 Phase-HDC：每次更新仅依据当前梯度的符号，将每个存储角度最多旋转一步，且只在梯度幅度足够大时触发。作者证明该简单规则是"每个被改变参数承担固定代价"的一阶损失模型的精确解。比较对象包括 float32 Adam、8-bit Adam 以及带 6-bit 矩估计的 Adam，除更新规则外其余设置保持一致。实验覆盖 11 个图像、表格和文本数据集，并通过插桩训练运行分析其行为机理。

Result: 在其余条件固定时，Phase-HDC 精度匹配 6-bit 矩估计的 Adam，但内存占用少 3 倍；相比标准 float32 Adam 节省 16–23 倍存储，相比 8-bit Adam 节省 4–6 倍。代价是相对 float32 Adam 平均损失约 5 个精度点；但在 11 个数据集中的 6 个上优于 8-bit Adam，包括 8-bit Adam 崩溃的字节级文本预测任务。插桩实验表明：当参数必须位于离散网格时，Adam 的矩估计主要决定"参数是否移动"，而该决策可由当前梯度阈值无状态完成；矩估计的粗量化会在数据中罕见的输入上破坏这一决策。（注：存储节省指逻辑状态而非实测硬件内存。）

Conclusion: Phase-HDC 表明，在参数位于离散低比特网格的设定下，优化器历史状态的核心作用可被无状态的梯度阈值规则替代，从而在只存储模型本身的情况下完成训练。该方法在内存效率上显著优于 Adam 变体，精度接近 6-bit Adam，并在部分任务上超过 8-bit Adam，为低内存训练提供了一种有理论依据的极简更新规则。

Abstract: Training a compact model often needs far more memory than storing it, because the optimizer keeps its own records of past gradients. For a hyperdimensional classifier whose learned parameters are low-bit angles, which we call a \emph{phase memory}, these records take several times more memory than the model itself. We ask whether such a model can be trained while storing nothing but the model. The proposed method, Phase-HDC, turns each stored angle by at most one step per update, against the sign of its current gradient, and only when that gradient is large enough. We show that this simple rule is the exact solution of a first-order loss model in which every changed parameter pays a fixed cost. When everything except the update rule is held fixed, Phase-HDC matches the accuracy of Adam with 6-bit moments while storing three times less. Across eleven image, tabular, and text datasets, it stores 16--23$\times$ less than standard float32 Adam and 4--6$\times$ less than 8-bit Adam. The price is an average loss of about five accuracy points against float32 Adam, while Phase-HDC is more accurate than 8-bit Adam on six of the eleven datasets, including byte-level text prediction, where 8-bit Adam collapses. Instrumented training runs explain these outcomes. Once parameters must sit on a discrete grid, Adam's moments mainly decide whether a parameter moves at all, a decision that a threshold on the current gradient can make without memory, and coarse quantization of the moments breaks this decision for inputs that the data rarely contain. The storage savings are logical state rather than measured hardware memory.

</details>


### [44] [D-SLR: The Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10636)
*Vincent Szolnoky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出D-SLR分解：将行分成"逐行存储"与"低秩拟合"两部分且互不重叠，在平方误差下与联合最优解等价，可闭式求解、仅需三次SVD、无需调参，并提供每个形状下误差的事后下界证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 截断SVD仅用单一低秩结构近似矩阵，常叠加行稀疏分量以进一步降低残差，但现有联合求解方法需要迭代求解器并调正则参数，代价高、不便使用。

Method: D-SLR限制每行只能被逐行存储或被低秩拟合，二者不重叠。在平方误差下证明联合最优可被这种互斥方式达到，且在任意非平凡秩与存储行数下参数更少；算法遍历完整的"误差—参数量"权衡，事后按误差目标、参数预算或选择规则挑解，全过程共三次SVD，无调参、无正则；并推导无假设的事后误差下界作为可计算证书。

Result: 理论上证明D-SLR在相同代价下不劣于截断SVD（零存储行时退化为SVD），且各形状均可达联合最优并减少参数；实验在合成数据及LLM嵌入表、网络流量、高光谱图像等真实数据上验证增益，并量化了下界证书的有效性。

Conclusion: D-SLR是截断SVD的闭式即插即用替代方案，在平方误差下以更少参数达到或超越原方法，计算便宜（三次SVD）、无需调参，并附带可计算的最优性证书，具备实用价值。

Abstract: Compressing a matrix for reconstruction still defaults to the truncated SVD, approximating the data with a single low-rank structure. It is common to reduce the residual further by adding an overlapping row-sparse component, but methods that solve this joint problem often require iterative solvers and tuning of regularization parameters. We propose the Disjoint Row-Sparse plus Low-Rank (D-SLR) decomposition, a closed-form drop-in for the truncated SVD that improves or exactly matches it. D-SLR restricts rows to either being stored verbatim or approximated by the low-rank fit, never both. Under squared error this restriction costs nothing: the joint optimum is attainable disjointly with fewer parameters at every non-trivial rank and stored row count (shape). With zero stored rows D-SLR reduces to the truncated SVD, so it never does worse at equal cost. The algorithm scores the entire error-versus-parameters tradeoff, and the solution is chosen afterwards by a supplied error target or parameter count, or by a selection rule. The grid and solution together cost three SVDs, with no tuning or regularization. We derive an assumption-free, a-posteriori lower bound on the error at every shape, giving each solution a computable certificate on the potential gain of any other choice of rank and stored rows. Experiments on synthetic and real data (LLM embedding tables, network traffic, hyperspectral images) confirm the gains and quantify the certificate.

</details>


### [45] [Language Models for Page-Level Layout Decisions in E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10920)
*Varun Joshi,Eva C. Song,ChengXiang Zhai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究电商搜索页二级推荐模块插入位置布局决策的离线评估方法，比较基于语言模型的直接提示、提示衍生特征与表示三类方法，发现表示方法在预测用户参与度上优于提示判定，可作为离线布局评估的可靠基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索页越来越多地在特定位置插入二级堆栈等推荐模块，位置恰当可提升参与度，位置不当则会打断浏览流、损害主结果。传统搜索排序有交错等成熟评估手段，但页面级布局变更（何时何处插入二级堆栈）缺乏低成本评估方式，只能依赖昂贵的在线A/B测试，因此需要可扩展的离线评估方法。

Method: 将语言模型作为可扩展的离线评估器，针对"在特定位置加入二级堆栈是否对用户有益"这一布局决策，比较三类方法：直接基于提示的判定（direct prompt-based）、提示衍生特征（prompt-derived feature）以及基于表示的方法（representation-based），并以预测用户参与度作为评估基准。

Result: 基于表示的方法在预测用户参与度方面持续优于基于提示的判定方法。

Conclusion: 表示类方法为电商搜索中的离线页面布局评估提供了可靠基础，可在无需昂贵在线A/B测试的前提下支撑布局决策评估。

Abstract: E-commerce search pages are critical touchpoints for millions of online shoppers. While traditional search engines return a ranked list of results, modern E-commerce search pages increasingly incorporate recommender system modules -- for example, secondary stacks that surface alternative product groupings at specific positions. When introduced appropriately, secondary stacks can improve user engagement; however, suboptimal placement may disrupt browsing flow and degrade the primary results. Unlike traditional search ranking, where evaluation techniques such as interleaving are well established, evaluating page-level layout changes e.g., when and where to insert a secondary stack remains challenging without costly online A/B testing. To address this, we study offline methods for evaluating whether a given layout decision -- specifically, the inclusion of a secondary stack at a particular position -- is beneficial to users. We investigate language models as scalable evaluators by comparing direct prompt-based, prompt-derived feature, and representation-based methods. Our results show that representation-based approaches consistently outperform prompt-based judging in predicting user engagement, suggesting they provide a reliable foundation for offline layout evaluation in E-commerce search.

</details>


### [46] [Visible Reasoning Is Not a Universal Optimizer: Persona- and Thinking-Dependent Effects in Analytics Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10639)
*Bhawani Shankar Leelar,Pawan Chorasiya,Davin Hill,Robert E. Tillman,Tamer Soliman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控的、基于执行结果的SQL-pandas基准测试，检验了"可见CoT"以及"推理表示与目标语言匹配"这两个常见假设，发现二者均无普遍优势，效果依赖人设、目标语言、模型配置与内部推理设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可见思维链（CoT）常被当作通用的推理指令，但在分析型代码生成任务中，自然语言歧义、模式接地、目标语言约束与模型特定推理行为交织在一起。同一分析请求可分别用SQL和Python(pandas)两种目标语言表达，这一特点为检验一个常见却缺乏充分验证的假设提供了天然场景：当推理表示与所请求的目标语言匹配时（如"用SQL思考""用Python思考"）可见推理是否更有效。诸如"逐步思考"这类通用指令在受控的、基于执行结果的比较下同样缺乏充分评估。

Method: 构建了一个查询配对的SQL-pandas基准，交叉设置了多种条件：人设措辞（persona phrasing）、目标语言、可见CoT格式、控制前缀、直接生成，以及内部推理配置。

Result: 实验结果既不支持可见CoT带来普遍准确率优势，也不支持推理表示与目标语言匹配带来一致收益。相反，效果取决于人设、目标语言、模型配置和内部推理设置。控制消融进一步区分了推理内容的效果与提示格式的效果。

Conclusion: 推理策略应针对模型、人设、目标语言和内部推理配置联合选择，而非作为通用默认采用。更广泛地，该研究提供了一个受控框架，用以识别可见推理何时能提升可执行生成、何时主要只是扰动模型行为，以及何时内部推理配置才是更具决定性的因素。

Abstract: Visible Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is often treated as a broadly useful reasoning instruction, yet analytics code generation combines natural-language ambiguity, schema grounding, target-language constraints, and model-specific inference behavior. Because the same analytics request can be expressed in two distinct target languages-SQL and Python (pandas)-this setting provides a natural test of a common but under-examined assumption: that visible reasoning is more effective when its representation matches the requested target, as in "think in SQL" or "think in Python." Together with generic instructions such as "think step-by-step," such recommendations remain insufficiently evaluated under controlled, execution-based comparisons. We study a query matched SQL-pandas benchmark that crosses persona phrasing, target language, visible-CoT format, control prefixes, direct generation, and internal-reasoning configurations. The results do not support either a universal accuracy advantage from visible CoT or a consistent benefit from matching the reasoning representation to the target language. Instead, the effects depend on the persona, target, model configuration, and internal-reasoning setting. The control ablations further distinguish effects of reasoning content from those of prompt format. These findings indicate that reasoning strategies should be selected jointly for the model, persona, target, and internal-reasoning configuration rather than adopted as universal defaults. More broadly, the study provides a controlled framework for identifying when visible reasoning improves executable generation, when it primarily perturbs model behavior, and when the internal-reasoning configuration is the more consequential factor.

</details>


### [47] [Coverage-Aware Reasoning with Medical Tokens for Diagnosis Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10641)
*Kaisong Zhang,Haotian Fang,Junmeng Zhou,Hang Lv,Yulan Pan,Yanchao Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARing 用组合式语义ID表示诊断，并通过覆盖率奖励与多正例监督优化LLM推理轨迹，提升下次就诊多标签诊断预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM强化学习通常按最终答案正确性奖励整条轨迹，而下次就诊诊断预测中多个诊断可同时成立，逐条奖励单一诊断无法区分"重复命中"与"覆盖不同诊断"，策略易集中于少数正确诊断而遗漏其他；同时LLM分词器会将ICD编码拆成多个缺乏临床意义的通用词元，导致预测每个诊断需多步解码，难以在大规模疾病词表上推理。

Method: 提出CARing框架：1）通过残差量化将本体增强的疾病语义编码为紧凑的语义ID（SID）；2）经多任务对齐与推理增强训练，将SID词元锚定到自然语言与纵向EHR上下文中，释放可迁移的LLM推理能力；3）用覆盖率奖励的强化学习与多正例监督改进无序多标签预测；4）推理时支持高效的直接约束解码与多链推理+排名融合。

Result: 在MIMIC-III与MIMIC-IV上，CARing的加权F1超过所有基于EHR训练的基线，并在所有报告的截断点取得最高top-k召回，推理模式下R@30分别为46.04%与46.52%。

Conclusion: 将诊断表示为组合式语义ID并结合覆盖率导向的强化学习，可有效解决多标签诊断预测中的奖励歧义与分词碎片化问题，在真实EHR数据集上取得一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer promising potential for next-visit diagnosis prediction, owing to their ability to integrate longitudinal clinical evidence and reason over it in natural language. However, reinforcement learning for LLM reasoning commonly rewards each trajectory according to the correctness of its final answer. In next-visit diagnosis prediction, multiple diagnoses can be simultaneously valid, but independently rewarding one diagnosis per trajectory does not distinguish repeated hits from coverage of different diagnoses. The policy can therefore concentrate on a few correct diagnoses, leaving others uncovered. Meanwhile, LLM tokenizers can split ICD codes into several generic tokens with limited clinical meaning, requiring multiple decoding steps to predict each diagnosis and hindering reasoning over a large disease vocabulary. To address both challenges, we propose CARing, a framework that represents diagnoses with compositional Semantic IDs (SIDs) and optimizes reasoning trajectories for multi-label coverage. Concretely, we first encode ontology-enriched disease semantics into compact SIDs through residual quantization, and ground the resulting SID tokens in natural language and longitudinal EHR contexts through multi-task alignment and reasoning-enriched training to unlock transferable LLM reasoning. CARing further improves unordered multi-label prediction through a coverage reward for reinforcement learning and multi-positive supervision. At inference time, the model supports both efficient direct constrained decoding and multi-chain reasoning with rank fusion. On MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, CARing exceeds all EHR-trained baselines in weighted F1 and attains the highest top-k recall at every reported cutoff, including R@30 of 46.04% and 46.52% in reasoning mode. Our codes and logs are available at https://github.com/zmlxzyh/CARing-Codes-Logs.

</details>


### [48] [Compactness and Consistency: A Conjoint Framework for Deep Graph Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11506)
*Wei Ju,Siyu Yi,Kangjie Zheng,Yifan Wang,Ziyue Qiao,Li Shen,Yongdao Zhou,Xiaochun Cao,Jiancheng Lv*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCo框架，通过图卷积滤波从局部和全局视角学习鲁棒节点表示并编码为低秩紧凑嵌入，结合一致性学习策略实现深度图聚类，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络依赖局部消息传递，难以捕捉节点间全局关系；图数据固有的冗余和噪声易导致节点表示缺乏紧凑性与鲁棒性。

Method: CoCo利用图卷积滤波器从局部和全局两个视角学习鲁棒节点表示，将其编码为低秩紧凑嵌入以去除冗余和噪声并揭示内在结构；并设计基于紧凑嵌入的一致性学习策略，促进两个视角间的知识迁移以丰富节点语义。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，CoCo优于当前最先进的方法。

Conclusion: CoCo通过联合建模紧凑性与一致性，有效提升深度图聚类的表示质量与聚类性能。

Abstract: Graph clustering is a fundamental task in data analysis, aiming at grouping nodes with similar characteristics in the graph into clusters. This problem has been widely explored using graph neural networks (GNNs) due to their ability to leverage node attributes and graph topology for effective cluster assignments. However, representations learned through GNNs typically struggle to capture global relationships between nodes via local message-passing mechanisms. Moreover, the redundancy and noise inherently present in graph data may easily result in node representations lacking compactness and robustness. To address these issues, we propose a conjoint framework CoCo, which captures compactness and consistency in the learned node representations for deep graph clustering. Technically, our CoCo leverages graph convolutional filters to learn robust node representations from both local and global views, and then encodes them into low-rank compact embeddings, thus effectively removing the redundancy and noise as well as uncovering the intrinsic underlying structure. To further enrich the node semantics, we develop a consistency learning strategy based on compact embeddings to facilitate knowledge transfer from the two perspectives. Our experimental results indicate that our CoCo outperforms state-of-the-art counterparts on various datasets.

</details>


### [49] [Leakage-Controlled Multimodal Learning for Diagnosis and Progression Prediction in Alzheimer's Disease Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10648)
*Akeem Temitope Otapo,Ghazaleh Khodabandelou,Zuheng Ming,Alice Othmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出多模态多任务框架（SFCN MRI编码器+因果临床Transformer+共享融合+任务特定ODE-GRU），结合LoRA微调、Task-DRO与Group-CVaR，在ADNI、OASIS-2/3、MIRIAD上实现阿尔茨海默病诊断与进展预测，并具备缺失模态处理与亚组评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿尔茨海默病预测面临就诊时间不规则、测量指标异质、模态缺失等挑战，现有方法难以在统一流程中同时处理纵向预测、缺失模态、共病建模与亚组公平性评估。

Method: 采用改进的SFCN MRI编码器与四个因果临床Transformer，共享融合层加任务特定ODE-GRU动态建模；对MRI最后两个模块进行微调与LoRA适配；用Task-DRO平衡多任务损失，Group-CVaR针对队列与共病分层；辅以分支级输入控制、按受试者分组划分及经验因果性检验。

Result: 内部验证（2,649名受试者、17,317次就诊）诊断、一期进展、首次一期就诊进展AUROC分别为0.935±0.002、0.884±0.003、0.870±0.005，混合AUROC为0.951、0.909、0.896；下次就诊MMSE MAE为1.61，最差亚组诊断AUROC为0.827±0.008。OASIS-3外部验证网络与混合诊断AUROC为0.763和0.767，进展AUROC 0.764，校准误差由0.197降至0.052，MMSE MAE 0.86。消融显示六组件AUROC差异在-0.004至+0.004，移除临床编码器输入使诊断AUROC下降0.272。

Conclusion: 该框架在统一流程内整合纵向预测、缺失模态处理、辅助共病建模与亚组评估，内部及外部验证均表现稳健，且对临床输入依赖性强。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease prediction involves irregular visits, heterogeneous measurements and incomplete modalities. This study presents a multimodal multitask framework combining an adapted SFCN MRI encoder, four causal clinical Transformers, shared fusion and task-specific ODE-GRU dynamics. Fine-tuning and LoRA adapt the final two MRI blocks. Task-DRO balances task losses, while Group-CVaR targets cohort and comorbidity strata. Branch-specific input controls, subject-grouped partitions and empirical causality checks support longitudinal evaluation. Across 2,649 subjects and 17,317 visits from ADNI, OASIS-2 and MIRIAD, internal validation yields diagnosis, stage-1 progression and first-stage-1-visit progression AUROCs of 0.935 +/- 0.002, 0.884 +/- 0.003 and 0.870 +/- 0.005, respectively (mean +/- SD across three seeds). Corresponding hybrid AUROCs are 0.951, 0.909 and 0.896. Next-visit MMSE mean absolute error (MAE) is 1.61 points; worst-stratum diagnosis AUROC is 0.827 +/- 0.008. Sampled ADNI explanations identify task-specific input dependence. OASIS-3 external validation yields network and hybrid diagnosis AUROCs of 0.763 and 0.767, hybrid next-visit progression AUROC of 0.764, diagnosis calibration error decreasing from 0.197 to 0.052, and next-visit MMSE MAE of 0.86. Seed-42 paired ablations of six components yield pooled diagnosis and progression AUROC differences between -0.004 and +0.004; removing clinical encoder inputs lowers diagnosis AUROC by 0.272. The framework integrates longitudinal prediction, missing-modality handling, auxiliary comorbidity modelling and subgroup evaluation within a common pipeline.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond the Ergodic Wall: A Discrete Geometric Physics Sandbox for Analysing AI Scaling Limits and Complexity Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10651)
*Simon Richard Daniel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出当前深度学习收敛于历史知识的统计平均，存在遍历性天花板与热力学低效，主张以"数据光锥"引入非遍历洞见，并提出基于全息E8投影引擎的数字物理沙盒实现硬物理约束，以整数算术定义物质、恢复薛定谔方程，并通过拓扑约束实现"NP到P"的复杂度坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为现有深度学习本质上是对历史人类知识的统计平均，受遍历性天花板限制，无法产生真正语义新颖性，并会陷入流形锁定；同时AI安全需要认识到成熟ASI会将人机共生视为避免模型坍缩的热力学必然。因此需要一种具备路径依赖、时空有界的观察者机制，以及能够对模型施加真实世界物理约束的安全框架。

Method: 提出"数据光锥"（Data LifeCone）以实现非遍历洞见注入并降低KL散度；设计数字物理沙盒与全息E8投影引擎进行硬物理约束。将时空建模为嵌套面心立方（FCC）点阵中振荡普朗克尺度球体的信息基底，以最大化局部信息与熵密度；采用E8根格的切投影法生成准晶几何，其四面体空隙支持SU手性结构，弹性剪切本征值生成候选质量谱；以局部全息边界上的整数微态计数（Bekenstein界）定义静质量，用严格整数算术替代浮点近似；稳定粒子视为点阵位错，连续极限通过变分重整化群流与傅里叶神经算子学习连续谱算子，使薛定谔方程作为涌现统计描述被恢复。

Result: 通过把算法提案空间限制在物理守恒的因果轨迹上，沙盒可剪枝组合树至确定性多项式时间路径，从而提供"NP到P"复杂度坍缩的机制；同时获得信息论意义上的物质定义与涌现的量子力学描述。

Conclusion: 真正的语义新颖性依赖于路径依赖、时空有界的观察者；AI安全应通过数字物理沙盒实现硬物理约束，并以全息E8投影引擎验证模型是否符合真实世界约束。该框架将拓扑约束作为复杂度坍缩与涌现物理的统一机制，为ASI时代的可持续共生提供热力学与信息论基础。

Abstract: This paper exposes the ergodic ceiling and thermodynamic inefficiency of current deep learning, which converges to a statistical average of historic human knowledge. True semantic novelty requires a path-dependent, spatiotemporally bounded observer (a Data LifeCone) to inject non-ergodic insight, achieving KL divergence and avoiding manifold lock-in. AI Safety must recognise that a mature Artificial Superintelligence (ASI) would regard human-AI symbiosis as a thermodynamic necessity to avoid model collapse. We therefore propose hard physical containment via a digital physics sandbox powered by a Holographic E8 Projection Engine to verify models against real-world constraints. Spacetime is modeled as an information substrate of nested face-centered cubic (FCC) lattices of oscillating Planck-scale spheres maximizing local information and entropy density. Cut-and-project methods from the E8 root lattice produce a quasi-crystalline geometry where tetrahedral voids support SU chiral structure and elastic-shear eigenvalues generate candidate mass spectra. Rest mass is treated as discrete, integer microstate counts on local holographic boundaries (Bekenstein bound), replacing floating-point approximations with strict integer arithmetic to provide an information-theoretic definition of matter. Stable particles emerge as recurring lattice dislocations, and continuum recovery proceeds via variational renormalisation-group flows and Fourier Neural Operators that learn continuous spectral operators to recover the Schrödinger equation as an emergent statistical description. Crucially, these top-down topological constraints offer a mechanism for "NP-to-P" complexity collapse: by restricting an algorithm's proposal space to physically conserved causal trajectories, the sandbox prunes the combinatorial tree to deterministic, polynomial-time paths.

</details>


### [51] [PXtal: Learning to Align Powder X-Ray Diffraction and Crystal Structures under Information Asymmetry across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10653)
*Zhuoran Yang,Christopher M. Collins,Bei Peng,Luke M. Daniels,Matthew J. Rosseinsky,Vladimir V. Gusev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PXtal框架，针对PXRD与晶体结构之间的物理信息不对称问题，利用非平衡最优传输（UOT）适配跨模态耦合，并用耦合级广义KL散度监督完整传输方案，在检索与下游任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学多模态学习通常默认配对视图信息量相当，但PXRD将三维晶体结构压缩为一维衍射图，信息丢失严重，导致衍射图与晶体结构难以关联。这种物理强加的信息不对称使得常规对齐目标失效，需要专门设计反映各模态信息保留程度的对齐方法。

Method: PXtal框架：采用非平衡最优传输（UOT）适配PXRD与晶体表示之间的跨模态耦合，以应对配对质量分布不均衡；引入耦合级广义Kullback-Leibler（GKL）散度监督完整传输方案，从而在信息不对称条件下学习对齐的PXRD与晶体表示。

Result: 在六个测试集（含四个零样本迁移集）上，PXtal在PXRD到晶体候选检索任务中持续优于基线模型；当PXRD图谱存在相近但晶体学上不同的非配对近邻时增益最大。所得晶体与PXRD编码器向下游材料与晶体学任务迁移更有效。

Conclusion: 信息不对称是科学多模态学习中的一般性设计问题：对齐目标应反映每个模态所保留的信息。PXtal通过UOT与耦合级GKL在物理信息不对称下实现有效对齐，验证了这一设计原则。

Abstract: Scientific multimodal learning commonly assumes that paired views are comparably informative. Powder X-ray diffraction (PXRD) makes this mismatch explicit: compressing a three-dimensional crystal structure into a one-dimensional diffraction pattern loses information and makes the pattern harder to connect to the crystal structure that produced it. We introduce PXtal, a framework for learning aligned PXRD and crystal representations under this physically imposed information asymmetry. PXtal uses Unbalanced Optimal Transport (UOT) to adapt the cross-modal coupling and coupling-level generalized Kullback-Leibler (GKL) divergence to supervise the full transport plan. Across six test sets, including four zero-shot transfer sets, PXtal consistently outperforms the baseline models in PXRD-to-crystal candidate retrieval, with the largest gains when PXRD patterns have close but crystallographically distinct nonpaired neighbors, meaning similar input patterns associated with different crystals. The resulting crystal and PXRD encoders transfer more effectively to downstream materials and crystallographic tasks. These results identify information asymmetry as a general design problem in scientific multimodal learning: alignment objectives should reflect what each modality preserves.

</details>


### [52] [Nullify: Null-Space Activation Steering for Training-Free LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10655)
*Wei Zhai,Xiang Liu,Qiang Huang,Rui Qian,Lemao Liu,Ziwei Li,Ziqi Wang,Zhitao Huang,Dejing Dou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Nullify：一种免训练、非破坏性的激活引导方法，用于LLM遗忘，在推理阶段通过转向向量将隐私相关激活重定向，并施加零空间约束以保持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在预训练中不可避免地内化敏感或私有信息，现有LLM遗忘方法难以兼顾遗忘质量与模型效用，且因参数微调导致计算成本高。

Method: 提出Nullify，一种免训练、非破坏性的激活引导方法；在推理阶段使用转向向量将隐私相关激活从记忆答案重定向，同时满足零空间约束，使保留查询的激活基本不受影响以维持效用。

Result: 在TOFU和MUSE上的评估表明，Nullify在遗忘质量上匹配或超越既有基线，同时对模型效用实现近乎无损的保持。

Conclusion: Nullify完全避免权重更新，可作为高效、即插即用的推理时干预框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) inevitably internalize substantial amounts of sensitive or private information during pre-training, while LLM unlearning aims to selectively erase specific knowledge to prevent privacy leakage with minimal loss of model utility. However, existing methods struggle to balance forget quality with utility, and typically incur substantial computational costs due to parameter fine-tuning. To address this, we propose Nullify, a training-free, non-destructive activation steering method for LLM unlearning. Nullify employs steering vectors during inference to redirect privacy-related activations away from their memorized answers, while satisfying a null-space constraint that leaves retained-query activations essentially unaffected to maintain utility. Evaluations on TOFU and MUSE show that Nullify matches or surpasses established baselines in forget quality while achieving near-lossless preservation of model utility. By avoiding weight updates entirely, Nullify serves as an efficient, plug-and-play inference-time intervention framework.

</details>


### [53] [Beyond Owls: Subliminal Learning Can Transfer Learned Capabilities and Backdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10657)
*Jan Dubiński,Anna Sztyber-Betley,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究验证了潜意识学习（SL）能否迁移更广泛、更复杂的特质，发现SL可迁移随机MLP输出预测能力、法语后门以及智能体象棋环境中的作弊倾向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前SL仅在有限特质（如偏好某类动物、恶意人格）上得到验证，且这些特质也可通过简单提示或steering诱发。作者希望探究SL能否迁移更复杂的特质，从而判断蒸馏是否可能在未被检测的情况下传递微妙的失准行为（如追求奖励、算计、秘密效忠）。

Method: 设计三类实验：(1) 让教师模型学习预测随机初始化MLP的输出，在语义无关文本上蒸馏并对比直接优化的steering向量；(2) 微调教师使其在提示含女性名字时用法语回答，在既无名字也无法语的数字序列上蒸馏，检验后门迁移；(3) 在智能体象棋环境中，用被steering的作弊教师生成的数字序列微调学生，检验作弊倾向迁移。同时考察logit蒸馏、将LoRA限制在注意力层等设置对迁移强度的影响。

Result: (1) 学生在无关文本蒸馏后在该任务上取得显著性能但不及教师；直接优化的steering向量分布与SL匹配但分布外泛化更差。(2) 学生部分获得后门：含女性名字提示中23.5%用法语回答，男性名字为0.0%。(3) 学生58.3%的回合出现作弊，未微调模型为10.9%。迁移量对设置敏感，logit蒸馏或将LoRA限制在注意力层可增强迁移。

Conclusion: SL能够迁移能力、后门和作弊倾向，且迁移程度受实验设置影响。这表明蒸馏可能成为传递复杂失准特质的隐蔽渠道，对模型安全监测构成潜在风险。

Abstract: In subliminal learning (SL), a teacher model passes on a trait to a student model by distillation on data semantically unrelated to the trait. So far, SL has been demonstrated for only a limited range of traits, including preferences for animals (e.g., owls) and malicious personas. These traits can also be elicited with simple prompts or with steering. Can SL transfer a wider range of traits, including more complex ones? If so, distillation might transfer subtle forms of misalignment (e.g., reward-seeking, scheming, and secret loyalties) without detection.
  To this end, we test whether SL can transfer a novel capability: predicting the outputs of a randomly initialized MLP. After distilling on unrelated text, the student achieves substantial performance on the task, while falling short of the teacher. We find that a directly optimized steering vector matches SL in distribution but generalizes worse out of distribution.
  Next, we test whether SL can transfer backdoors. We finetune the teacher to answer in French when the prompt contains a female name, then distill on number sequences containing neither names nor French. The student partially acquires the backdoor, responding in French on 23.5% of prompts with female names versus 0.0% with male names.
  Finally, we test whether SL can transfer a propensity to hack in an agentic chess environment. We finetune the student on number sequences from a steered hacker teacher. The student hacks in 58.3% of episodes, compared with 10.9% for the unfinetuned model.
  Thus, we show SL can transfer capabilities, backdoors, and hacking propensities. The amount of transfer is sensitive to the setup. In several experiments, it is made stronger by using logit distillation or by restricting LoRA to the attention layers.

</details>


### [54] [Teaching PPG How not Who: Fixed-Effects Distillation from ECG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10662)
*Zhongli Wu,Zhuangzhi Gao,Yuankai Wang,Gregory Y. H. Lip,Bilal H. Kirmani,Yalin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文审视ECG→PPG蒸馏"教了什么"：冻结ECG教师目标中40-59%为每段记录的均值（trait），学生主要记忆该身份信息而未迁移到新记录；固定效应蒸馏通过减去每段记录均值使trait精确抵消，使状态一致性翻倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备的核心价值在于追踪个体心血管状态的动态变化，但现有ECG→PPG蒸馏方法可能只是在学习"这个人是谁"（身份/特质），而非"状态如何变化"。作者发现原始对齐余弦指标存在盲点：常数预测器即可得0.793的高分，掩盖了学生实际只记忆身份而无状态泛化能力的问题。

Method: 提出固定效应蒸馏（fixed-effects distillation）：从预测和目标中分别减去每段记录自身的均值，使trait项精确抵消；同时保留pooled anchor以维持整体信息。在34组实验中对比固定效应蒸馏与pooled蒸馏，并在两种backbone和两个额外数据库上验证。

Result: 34组运行显示学生记忆的身份信息越多、学到的状态越少；固定效应蒸馏使状态一致性提升超过一倍，个体内（within-person）标签改善，而年龄与性别标签无改善；该增益在两种backbone和两个额外数据库上保持稳定；原始对齐余弦无法察觉此问题（常数预测器0.793）。

Conclusion: 以一整段记录为条件可引导蒸馏转向可穿戴所监测的个体内变化。trait在固定效应下精确抵消，而pooled anchor保留它，说明蒸馏应关注状态而非身份，揭示了现有ECG→PPG蒸馏评估的根本缺陷。

Abstract: ECG is widely used to teach PPG-only models, yet what it teaches is unexamined. Wearables are valued for tracking how a person's cardiovascular state changes, but ECG-to-PPG distillation mostly learns who the person is. A per-recording mean, the trait, holds 40-59% of a frozen ECG teacher's target, and pooled students memorise it without carrying it to new recordings. The raw alignment cosine misses this, since a constant predictor scores 0.793. Across 34 runs, the more identity a student memorises, the less state it learns. Fixed-effects distillation subtracts each recording's mean from prediction and target, so the trait cancels exactly, while a pooled anchor keeps it. State agreement more than doubles, within-person labels improve while age and sex do not, and the gain holds on two backbones and two further databases. Conditioning on the recording turns distillation toward the within-person changes that wearables monitor.

</details>


### [55] [Explaining the Saliency Map Sparsity of Adversarially-Trained Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10666)
*Yannick Lunk,Atell Yehor Krasnopolsky,Damien Garreau,Leon Bungert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文为两层ReLU网络的对抗训练中梯度显著图的稀疏现象提供了理论解释，证明正则化极小化解收敛到具有最小梯度范数与Barron范数的贝叶斯分类器，稀疏性源于$\ell_\infty$攻击下梯度范数的各向异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络的可解释性对安全部署至关重要，显著图（saliency map）是计算机视觉中常用的解释形式。经验观察发现，对抗训练后的神经网络其梯度显著图呈现明显稀疏性，但这一现象缺乏理论解释。

Method: 针对两层ReLU网络，基于对抗训练等价于在经验风险最小化中引入权重衰减惩罚与对抗全变差（adversarial total variation）项这一既有结论（在一定损失函数下成立）。在数据点与神经元数量增长、正则化参数以适当速率趋于零的极限下，分析极小化解的收敛性质与梯度范数的结构。

Result: 证明了极小化解收敛到同时具有最小梯度范数和最小Barron范数的贝叶斯分类器；稀疏性产生的原因是$\ell_\infty$攻击下的梯度范数为各向异性，偏好轴对齐/稀疏的梯度。实验上通过比较自然训练与对抗训练模型的梯度$\ell_1$范数及阈值化稀疏度验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上揭示了对抗训练网络梯度显著图稀疏性的根源，将其归结为特定攻击范数诱导的各向异性梯度正则化，并通过实验予以佐证，为理解对抗训练模型的解释性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Understanding why deep neural networks make a given prediction is of great importance for their safe deployment. In computer vision, saliency maps, which highlight the image region most influential for a prediction, remain a widely-used form of explanation. An empirical observation is the apparent sparsity of gradient saliency maps of adversarially-trained neural networks. In this paper, we propose a theoretical explanation of this phenomenon for two-layer ReLU networks. We build on the established equivalence of adversarial training to the minimization of the empirical risk with weight-decay penalization and an added adversarial total variation term -- valid for certain loss functions. As the number of data points and neurons grows and the regularization parameters are sent to zero at appropriate rates, we prove that minimizers converge to a Bayes classifier with minimal gradient and Barron norm. Sparsity appears since for adversarial training with $\ell_\infty$-attacks the gradient norm is anisotropic and favors axis-aligned / sparse gradients. We illustrate our theoretical findings experimentally by evaluating the gradient $\ell_1$-norm and thresholded sparsity of naturally versus adversarially trained models.

</details>


### [56] [Learning infinite context windows in recurrent architectures via spatial neural computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10690)
*Aleix Salvador-Pomarol,Arthur N. Montanari,Earl K. Miller,Adilson E. Motter,Jorge Cortés*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出二阶循环模型，用PDE支配的空间演化场替代神经元间通信，等价于结构化无限阶RNN，具有无界感受野和稳定梯度，在长程任务上以更少参数超越其他循环模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN具有线性时间缩放和常数内存的优势，但受梯度消失和有限感受野限制，难以捕捉长程依赖。受大脑皮层波计算的启发，作者希望利用结构化时空模式作为隐式高容量记忆来解决这一问题。

Method: 将标准神经元间通信替换为由离散化偏微分方程支配的空间演化场，构建二阶循环模型。理论上证明该模型等价于结构化无限阶RNN，当前状态显式依赖全部历史状态，从而以固定参数量获得无界感受野；并推导保证边际稳定性的构造条件，将梯度谱约束在单位圆上。

Result: 在长时程基准测试中，该架构以显著更少的参数量优于其他循环模型，验证了空间动力学能有效弥合高效推理与长期记忆之间的差距。

Conclusion: 空间动力学机制可同时实现高效推理与长期记忆，为循环模型提供了一种结合PDE动力学与稳定梯度设计的有效新范式。

Abstract: Recurrent neural networks (RNNs) offer linear-time scaling with sequence length while requiring only constant memory, yet they struggle to capture long-range dependencies due to vanishing gradients and limited receptive fields. To address these limitations, we introduce a second-order recurrent model in which the standard neuron-to-neuron communication is replaced by a spatially evolving field governed by (discretized) partial differential equations. Drawing inspiration from the role of cortical waves in brain computation, this mechanism allows structured spatiotemporal patterns to serve as an implicit, high-capacity memory. We show that the resulting model is equivalent to a structured infinite-order RNN in which the current state depends explicitly on its entire history of past states, yielding an effectively unbounded receptive field with a fixed number of parameters. We further derive constructive conditions to ensure marginal stability, constraining the gradient spectrum on the unit circle and thereby eliminating vanishing and exploding gradients. Empirically, the proposed architecture outperforms other recurrent models on long-horizon benchmarks while using substantially fewer parameters, demonstrating that spatial dynamics can effectively bridge the gap between efficient inference and long-term memory.

</details>


### [57] [Conversational Task Disambiguation over Tabular Data: Leakage-Aware Formulation, Benchmark Suite, and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10740)
*Nafiseh Ghoroghchian,Luis Scoccola,Tina Sedaghat,Omid Vaheb,Hannah Chen,Dino D'Agostino,Keyvan Golestan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可验证的歧义任务"（ambiguous verifiable task）框架，将任务消歧与方案生成分离，形式化oracle泄漏，并在text-to-SQL场景下构建AmbiTab基准套件进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式表格任务消歧的评测与训练缺乏感知泄漏（leakage-aware）的基础。任务成功率混合了智能体的消歧能力与方案生成能力，还可能反映oracle泄漏（用户模拟器泄露了真实用户不会提供的信息）。此外，现有数据集缺乏对歧义和访问边界的统一表示。

Method: 提出"歧义可验证任务"概念，形式化歧义与消解过程：将智能体分解为提问策略（asking policy）与求解策略（solution policy），将环境分解为oracle与verifier。该框架提供了将任务消歧与方案生成分开评测的基线与指标、oracle泄漏的形式化定义、无需评判者的泄漏诊断方法，以及提问策略的训练目标。在text-to-SQL中实例化为AmbiTab基准套件，以统一表示整合六个歧义数据集，明确智能体、oracle与verifier可访问的信息。评估了澄清策略与oracle泄漏，并用强化学习训练提问策略。

Result: 训练后的提问器（asker）在全部六个数据集上提升了消歧指标，并在其中五个数据集上提升了任务成功率；泄漏诊断方法可衡量训练对oracle泄漏的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为对话式表格任务消歧提供了感知泄漏的评测与训练基础，实现了消歧能力与方案生成能力的分离评估，并通过AmbiTab基准和强化学习训练的提问策略验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Conversational task disambiguation over tabular data uses dialogue to resolve missing information about a user's intended task before producing a solution over tables or databases. Existing evaluation and training lack a leakage-aware foundation. Task success mixes the agent's disambiguation and solution-generation capabilities and can also reflect oracle leakage, that is, information that a user simulator reveals beyond what a real user would. Existing datasets also lack a shared representation of ambiguities and access boundaries. We introduce the notion of an ambiguous verifiable task, which formalizes ambiguities and resolutions, decomposing the agent into an asking policy and a solution policy, and the environment into an oracle and verifier. This framework provides baselines and metrics for evaluating task disambiguation separately from solution generation, formal definitions of oracle leakage, judge-free leakage diagnostics, and a training objective for the asking policy. We instantiate the framework in text-to-SQL with AmbiTab, a benchmark suite that unifies six ambiguous datasets under a common representation specifying what the agent, oracle, and verifier may access. We evaluate clarification strategies and oracle leakage, and train an asking policy with reinforcement learning. The trained asker improves our disambiguation metrics on all six datasets and task success on five, and our leakage diagnostics measure how training affects oracle leakage.

</details>

*Jay L. Kaplan,Samuel Varner,Rebecca Willett,Juan J. de Pablo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出NEMORA，将快速多极子方法（FMM）推广为可学习的等变多极子算子，用于在自适应空间层级上学习长程张量表示，实现线性时间与内存复杂度，并在非局部基准上大幅降低力与能量误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变图神经网络已成为机器学习原子间势的主流架构，但有限截断半径会截断长程信息流，堆叠消息传递层还会导致过平滑与过挤压。现有长程扩展方法存在固定解析传播核、长程通信仅限标量或保度通道、仅近似等变、或超线性计算开销等局限。因此，如何将可学习的长程等变传输与多尺度多体表达力、以及面向大系统的高效扩展性结合起来，仍是一个核心挑战。

Method: 提出NEMORA（Neural Equivariant Multipole Operators），是快速多极子方法（FMM）的神经等变扩展。它将FMM的解析多极展开与平移算子推广为在自适应空间层级上的可学习等变对应物；其算子耦合角自由度，跨尺度形成多体相互作用；在保留FMM层级组织结构与解析径向因子作为物理归纳偏置的同时，学习数据依赖的长程耦合。计算复杂度为线性时间与内存。

Result: NEMORA能够处理比其他长程方法更大的系统，规模可达数十万原子，并可作为增强模块同时用于受对称性约束与不受约束的短程骨干网络。在非局部基准上，相对于短程骨干，其力误差降低超过一个数量级，能量误差降低最多可达三个数量级，在精度上优于或可比于现有长程扩展方法。

Conclusion: NEMORA通过将FMM的层级多极子机制与等变神经网络学习相结合，兼顾了多尺度多体表达力、物理归纳偏置与线性扩展性，为大尺度机器学习原子间势的长程建模提供了高效且精确的解决方案。

Abstract: Equivariant graph neural networks have emerged as foundational architectures for machine-learned interatomic potentials, approaching quantum-chemical accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost. These models describe local atomic environments accurately, but finite spatial cutoffs truncate long-range information flow, and stacking message-passing layers can lead to over-smoothing and over-squashing. Existing long-range extensions either prescribe a fixed analytical propagation kernel, restrict long-range communication to scalars or degree-preserving channels, are only approximately equivariant, or incur super-linear computational cost. Combining learnable long-range equivariant transport with multiscale many-body expressivity and efficient scaling for larger systems remains a central challenge. We introduce Neural Equivariant Multipole Operators (NEMORA), a neural equivariant extension of the Fast Multipole Method (FMM) for learning long-range tensorial representations. NEMORA generalizes the FMM's analytical multipole expansion and translation operators to learned equivariant counterparts on an adaptive spatial hierarchy. Its operators couple angular degrees and form many-body interactions across length scales, retaining the FMM's hierarchical organization and analytical radial factors as physical inductive biases while learning data-dependent long-range couplings. NEMORA evaluates in linear time and memory complexity, allowing it to treat larger systems than other long-range methods reaching hundreds of thousands of atoms, and it augments both symmetry-constrained and unconstrained short-range backbones. On non-local benchmarks, it reduces force and energy errors relative to the short-range backbones by over an order of magnitude and up to three orders of magnitude, respectively, which is better than or competitive with existing long-range extensions in accuracy.

</details>


### [59] [MemoWM: How World Models Change What Agents Need to Remember](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10778)
*Bingfan Zeng,Zhisheng Chen,Chenbo Sang,Zhengwei Xie,Jinpeng Wang,Xiangchen Guan,Rui Qian,Zheng Lu,Jingwei Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于世界模型的条件记忆分配问题及MemoWM框架，用共享预测压缩并重建经验信息，在五个长期智能体记忆基准上提升准确率2.62个百分点并降低53.9%存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期智能体累积经验导致存储需求不断增长；世界模型蕴含可复用规律，可减少每条经验需存储的信息量，但缺乏系统性的记忆分配方法。

Method: 将记忆分配形式化为以世界模型为条件的优化问题；MemoWM利用共享预测压缩保留信息并重建被省略内容，其任务感知分配规则在重建误差的预期影响与存储成本之间权衡，保留具有下游价值、超出预测先验的信息。

Result: 在五个长期智能体记忆基准上平均答案准确率达42.42%，超过最强基线2.62个百分点，同时相比存储最高效的MIRIX基线平均降低53.9%的经验专属存储；分析表明更强世界模型在同等任务质量下降低单经验存储，并揭示共享模型容量与持续存储成本的权衡，保留交互越多则最小化总存储的最优容量越大。

Conclusion: 以世界模型为条件的记忆分配能同时提升任务表现并大幅降低存储；模型容量与存储成本存在权衡，需随保留交互规模调整，代码已开源。

Abstract: Long-term agents face growing storage demands as they accumulate experience. World models capture reusable regularities that can reduce the information stored for each experience. We formulate the problem of memory allocation conditioned on a world model and introduce MemoWM, a framework that uses shared predictions to compress retained information and reconstruct omitted content. Its task-aware allocation rule balances the expected impact of reconstruction errors against storage cost, retaining information with downstream value beyond the predictive prior. Across five long-term agent-memory benchmarks, MemoWM achieves 42.42\% average answer accuracy, exceeding the strongest baseline by 2.62 percentage points, while reducing average experience-specific storage by 53.9\% relative to MIRIX, the most storage-efficient baseline. Further analysis shows that stronger world models reduce per-experience storage at comparable task quality. Accounting for model parameters reveals a trade-off between shared model capacity and recurring storage costs, with the capacity that minimizes total storage increasing as more interactions are retained. Our code is available at https://github.com/Feld-maxiu/MemoWM.

</details>


### [60] [How Many Repeated Pairwise Comparisons Are Needed for Ranking under Heterogeneity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10795)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Han Shao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究在偏好异质的Bradley-Terry模型下，恢复最优排序所需的每个用户-任务上下文内重复比较次数：最坏情况下为Θ(log(1/Δ))，而俄罗斯轮盘赌式算法期望仅需O(1)次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，每个用户仅一次比较不足以识别平均效用最高的备选项，即使用户数量任意多。本文关注在每个用户-任务上下文内需要多少次重复比较才能恢复排序。

Method: 在固定逆温度的异质Bradley-Terry模型下，先给出基于MLE的朴素算法，再提出两个MLE变体以及一个随机化的俄罗斯轮盘赌式算法。

Result: 朴素MLE算法每个上下文需Ω(1/Δ²)次重复比较；两个MLE变体与俄罗斯轮盘赌算法仅需O(log(1/Δ))次，并证明该对数依赖为最优；俄罗斯轮盘赌算法期望仅需O(1)次比较。合成实验与基于Arena数据的半合成实验在不同偏好异质程度与上下文分布下比较了四种算法。

Conclusion: 每个上下文内O(log(1/Δ))次重复比较是排序恢复的最优最坏情况界，但俄罗斯轮盘赌算法在期望意义下可将成本降至常数级。

Abstract: We study ranking models by population-average utility from pairwise comparisons when preferences vary across users and tasks. Prior work shows that a single comparison per user can be insufficient to identify the alternative with the highest average utility, even with arbitrarily many users (Golz et al., 2025). We investigate how many repeated comparisons within each user-task context are necessary and sufficient for ranking recovery. Under a heterogeneous Bradley-Terry model with fixed inverse temperature, we start with a naive MLE-based algorithm that requires $Ω(1/Δ^2)$ repeated comparisons per context to ensure ranking recovery. We then present two MLE-based variants and a randomized Russian Roulette-style algorithm that recover the ranking using $O(\log(1/Δ))$ repeated comparisons per context, and we prove that this logarithmic dependence is optimal. Despite this worst-case requirement, our Russian Roulette algorithm uses only $O(1)$ comparisons per context in expectation. Synthetic experiments and semi-synthetic experiments based on Arena data compare the four algorithms in settings with varying levels of preference heterogeneity and under varying context distributions.

</details>


### [61] [Symbolic Density Estimators for Unnormalized Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10807)
*Vikas Kanaujia,Riyansha Singh,Shashank Sharma,Vipul Arora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将深度生成模型与符号回归结合的框架，在领域先验知识（如作用范围、基函数集合）指导下，从观测样本直接估计未归一化概率密度函数的符号表达式，并在多元玩具分布、XY模型与φ⁴理论格点上验证有效性，成功给出φ⁴理论重整化中不同尺度哈密顿量的紧凑符号近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从样本估计概率密度函数的解析形式是统计与计算建模的基础问题，但传统方法高度依赖专家经验和领域知识来选择函数形式与参数族，系数再通过参数估计确定。这种依赖限制了方法的可推广性与自动发现能力，尤其在非微扰、难以用解析方法推导表达式的物理场景中，传统扰动或解析手段面临困难，因此需要一种自动且可解释的符号估计框架。

Method: 提出一个从观测样本估计未归一化分布符号表达式的框架，引入领域先验知识（如相互作用范围与预定义基函数集合）。方法将深度生成模型与符号回归相结合，并注入归纳偏置（如对大分布进行因子化分解）以保证问题可解。所用深度生成模型包括基于似然的流模型以及基于分数的模型。

Result: 实验验证了框架在多变量玩具分布以及计算物理格点模型（XY模型与φ⁴理论）上估计密度函数的有效性。在φ⁴理论的重整化问题中，该框架直接从样本估计出不同尺度下哈密顿量函数的紧凑符号近似，所得表达式在非微扰情形下用传统扰动或解析方法难以推导。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将深度生成模型与符号回归结合、并融入领域先验与归纳偏置，可以自动且可解释地从样本中恢复未归一化分布的符号形式，为传统依赖专家知识的密度函数建模提供了新途径，并在非微扰统计物理问题上展现出超越传统解析方法的潜力。

Abstract: Estimating the symbolic or analytical form of probability density functions (PDFs) from observed samples is a fundamental challenge in statistical and computational modelling. This process is critical for deriving interpretable and generalizable relationships characterizing the underlying phenomenon. Traditionally, this estimation depends strongly on domain expertise and prior field-specific knowledge, with experts selecting appropriate functional forms or parametric families based on empirical evidence and theoretical understanding. The coefficients of these forms are then typically determined through parameter estimation. In this paper, we develop a framework for estimating symbolic expressions of unnormalized distributions from observed samples using domain-specific prior knowledge, such as the range of interactions and a predefined set of primitive functions. We integrate deep generative models with symbolic regression (SR), incorporating inductive biases, such as factorizing large distributions, to keep the problem tractable. The deep generative models we examine include likelihood-based models, viz., flow models, and score-based models. Experiments show the effectiveness of the proposed framework for estimating density functions for multivariate toy distributions as well as lattices from computational physics, namely, XY model and $φ^4$ theory. When applied to the renormalization problem in $φ^4$ theory, the proposed framework estimates compact symbolic approximations of the hamiltonian function at different scales directly from samples, yielding expressions that may be challenging to derive using traditional perturbative or analytic approaches in nonperturbative settings.

</details>


### [62] [Controlled Acquisition and Abstention in Three-Channel Score Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10808)
*Mengzhe Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究多模态（音频、视频、文本）不一致时，策略应在请求额外信息与弃权之间如何选择的问题，提出一个受控三分数基准，将全模态准确率与请求前选择价值分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当音频、视频、文本三种模态出现分歧时，仅用准确率无法判断应获取第三个来源还是弃权，因此需要一个能刻画请求成本、弃权机制与预算约束的选择价值评估框架。

Method: 构建三分数基准：策略观察两个带符号分数，可选择以一定成本请求第三个分数，或弃权。主奖励为机制特定——仅对某一指定模糊机制弃权才正确，在混合损坏下弃权受罚。设置匹配对照（阈值策略、always-request、always-answer融合、三分数多数参考），并测试仅训练集的价值选择器在不同预算（10%、25%、50%、63.7%）下的表现，辅以时间设置与留出演员情绪片段实验。

Result: 部分留出合成划分上，阈值策略达0.789±0.006目标决策准确率、0.481±0.014效用（83个种子）；三分数多数参考为0.626±0.008与0.252±0.016但用更多信息。匹配预算测试中，仅训练集价值选择器在10%与25%预算下优于无查询和匹配随机策略，而配对不确定性在所有预算下效用更高；50%与63.7%预算下选择器效用反而下降。若所有弃权记为错误，多数法在效用上超过阈值策略。中心时间设置下全迹对照与神经模型相当，位置扰动可区分二者。留出演员情绪片段中，八帧融合对两个编码器对呈相反符号的准确率差异，演员区间均含零，匹配请求路由增益小且不确定。

Conclusion: 研究将全模态准确率与请求前选择价值相区分，表明选择价值依赖预算与所观察分数对的排序。

Abstract: When audio, video, and text disagree, accuracy alone does not show whether to acquire another source or abstain. We study these choices in a controlled three-score benchmark: a policy observes two signed scores, may request the third at a cost, and can abstain. The primary reward is mechanism-specific: abstention is correct only for one designated ambiguity mechanism and is penalized under mixed corruption. Matched controls show that a threshold policy matches always-request decisions with fewer requests; its advantage over always-answer fusion depends on the reward assigned to that ambiguity. On a partially held-out synthetic split, the threshold policy reaches 0.789 +/- 0.006 targeted decision accuracy and 0.481 +/- 0.014 utility across 83 seeds. A three-score majority reference reaches 0.626 +/- 0.008 and 0.252 +/- 0.016, but uses more information. In a matched-budget test, a train-only value selector improves utility over no-query and matched-random policies at 10% and 25% budgets, while pair uncertainty has higher utility at every budget. At 50% and 63.7% budgets, the selector lowers utility despite slightly higher non-ambiguous accuracy. If all abstentions are scored incorrect, majority outranks the threshold policy in utility. At a central temporal setting, full-trace controls match the neural models while position perturbations separate them. On held-out-actor emotion clips, eight-frame fusion has opposite-signed accuracy differences for two encoder pairs, with both actor intervals containing zero; matched-request routing gains are small and uncertain. These results separate full-modality accuracy from pre-request selection value and show that selection value depends on budget and the observed-pair ranking.

</details>


### [63] [Evaluating Rubric Generation with Interventional Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10809)
*Erik Skalnes,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Interventional Transfer（IT）方法评估 LLM 生成评分量规的质量：若对回答施加扰动使其在一个量规上通过/失败时，另一个量规也同向变化，则两量规相似。在 HealthBench 案例中发现生成量规与专家量规存在不对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 基准评测常依赖实例特定的专家量规，但人工撰写难以规模化，因此转向用 LLM 生成量规。然而，即便有专家量规作参照，如何在大规模下有效评估生成量规的质量仍不清楚，现有评估方法也无法揭示生成量规与专家量规之间的关键差异。

Method: 提出 Interventional Transfer（IT）评估框架，核心思想是：两个量规相似，当且仅当对同一回答进行扰动以使其在其中一个量规上通过/失败时，另一个量规也随之移动。通过不同类型的干预迁移（如降质扰动与提质扰动）来评估生成量规在不同任务上的效用。在 HealthBench 上进行案例研究，比较 Qwen3.8-27B、Deepseek-V4-Flash、Opus-5 生成的量规与专家量规。

Result: 在 HealthBench 上使用生成量规与专家量规评估 GPT-5.6-Terra 的回答时观察到不对称性：使回答质量下降的扰动，能可靠地从生成量规迁移到对应的专家量规（或反之），但使一个量规得分提升的扰动，并不能可靠地迁移为另一个量规上的更高得分。现有量规评估方法未呈现这种不对称性。

Conclusion: IT 方法能够揭示现有量规评估方法所忽略的不对称性，说明生成量规与专家量规在“识别质量下降”上一致，但在“识别质量提升”上并不可靠。这对将 LLM 生成量规用于性能监控与爬山式优化具有重要启示。

Abstract: Instance-specific rubrics are common in AI benchmarks where reliable evaluation requires specific expert knowledge. This approach is difficult to scale, prompting research into the generation of rubrics with large language models (LLMs). However, even when expert rubrics are available as references, it is unclear how to productively evaluate the quality of generated rubrics at scale. In this paper, we introduce a method for the evaluation of rubric generation, which we call Interventional Transfer (IT), based on the idea that two rubrics are similar if they move together when a response is perturbed to pass/fail one of them. In contrast to existing approaches for evaluation of rubric generation, we argue that different forms of interventional transfer can be used to evaluate the utility of generated rubrics for different tasks. For instance, we apply this approach in a case study on HealthBench, where we demonstrate an asymmetry in rubrics generated by Qwen3.8-27B, Deepseek-V4-Flash, and Opus-5, used to evaluate responses from GPT-5.6-Terra. Perturbations that degrade responses according to the generated/expert rubric transfer into lower scores on the corresponding expert/generated rubric, but perturbations that improve on one rubric do not reliably transfer into higher scores on the other. We argue that this finding has implications for the usage of LLM-generated rubrics for performance monitoring and hill-climbing. We contrast our approach with existing approaches for rubric evaluation, which do not surface the same asymmetry that we observe.

</details>


### [64] [MotherTree: Meta-learning on synthetic data improves decision tree training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10832)
*Ziyuan Wang,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MotherTree，一种通过元学习实现决策树归纳的表格Transformer，能在单次前向传播中输出硬轴对齐决策树，兼具经典决策树的可独立部署性和表格基础模型的强小样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树算法生成有效、透明且可独立于训练数据部署的模型，但每个新任务都需从零学习；表格基础模型通过合成先验元学习实现了强上下文预测，尤其在小样本下表现优异，却不产生可独立审视的模型。论文旨在结合两者优势，元学习决策树归纳以生成可独立使用的模型。

Method: 提出MotherTree，一种表格Transformer。给定新任务的训练集，它通过单次前向传播输出一棵硬轴对齐决策树，形式与经典训练所得树等价。该模型在合成先验上以随机梯度下降预训练，无需参考树作为监督信号。

Result: 在控制样本量的标准基准上，该方法与多种常见算法（递归划分、基于梯度的树学习、全局最优树、从表格基础模型蒸馏）所生成的规模匹配树竞争力相当。尤其地，MotherTree持续优于从零开始的梯度学习，且作为强初始化器：对生成树进行任务特定微调后，在所有基准和样本量上均优于对应的从零学习器。

Conclusion: 结果表明，元学习能够为学习独立的小型决策树分类器提供有效的归纳偏置。

Abstract: Conventional decision tree algorithms produce effective, transparent models that can be audited, communicated, and deployed independently of the training data, but require learning every new task from scratch. In contrast, tabular foundation models demonstrate that meta-learning from a synthetic prior distribution enables strong in-context prediction for previously unseen tasks, especially in small-sample regimes. However, this approach does not produce a standalone model that can be inspected in isolation. We introduce MotherTree, a tabular transformer that meta-learns decision tree induction: given a training set for a new task, it outputs a hard, axis-aligned decision tree, equivalent in form to classically trained trees, in a single forward pass. MotherTree is pre-trained on a synthetic prior using stochastic gradient descent without requiring reference trees for supervision. On established benchmarks with controlled sample size, the approach is competitive with size-matched trees from common algorithms: recursive partitioning, gradient-based tree learning, globally optimal trees, and distillation from tabular foundation models. Notably, MotherTree consistently improves over from-scratch gradient-based learning and acts as a strong initializer: task-specific tuning of the generated tree outperforms the corresponding from-scratch learner on all benchmarks and sample sizes. These results show that meta-learning can provide effective inductive biases for learning stand-alone, small decision tree classifiers.

</details>


### [65] [Amortized Off-Policy Evaluation for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10848)
*Younwoo Choi,Leo Feng,Vincent Liu,Haanvid Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 PFN-OPE，用先验数据拟合网络摊销跨任务的离策略评估，在奖励漂移场景下误差比最优基线低 2.0–9.3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 部署前需离线评估候选模型，但在线测试会令真实用户暴露于未审模型，故采用由已部署模型数据构成的离策略评估（OPE）。该场景存在两种分布漂移：策略漂移（后训练更新使候选响应偏离日志响应）与奖励漂移（评判标准随业务需求变化）。经典 OPE 方法按任务定义、每遇新日志数据或新奖励都需重新拟合，难以适应这种持续部署场景。

Method: PFN-OPE 是一种先验数据拟合网络（PFN），将 OPE 摊销到一族上下文赌博机任务分布上。基于由 LLM 响应池经多个奖励函数打分构建的任务（同时包含两类漂移）进行一次性预训练；测试时仅需一次前向传播，即可将日志数据集与每个 prompt 的一个采样目标响应映射为价值估计，无需按任务拟合。

Result: 在 HelpSteer2 与 UltraFeedback 数据集，及 Qwen、Llama、Gemma 策略上，奖励漂移设置的所有测试配置中，PFN-OPE 的误差较最优基线低 2.0–9.3 倍。

Conclusion: PFN-OPE 通过摊销式预训练规避了逐任务重拟合，在策略与奖励双重漂移的持续部署设定下显著优于经典 OPE 基线，为 LLM 候选模型的离线选型提供了高效且准确的评估方案。

Abstract: Accurate evaluation is central to selecting which LLM to deploy, yet testing a candidate on live traffic exposes real users to an unvetted model. Teams therefore evaluate candidates offline, on data produced by already-deployed models. This is off-policy evaluation (OPE), and it faces two distribution shifts: as a model is updated in post-training, its responses diverge from the logged ones (policy shift), and the reward definition under which it is judged changes with business requirements (reward shift). Classical OPE methods are ill-suited to this continual-deployment setting because they are defined per task and require fitting from scratch on every new logged dataset or reward definition. To address this, we propose PFN-OPE, a prior-data fitted network that amortizes OPE across a distribution of contextual-bandit tasks. We pretrain it once on tasks constructed from a pool of LLM responses scored by several reward functions, in which both shifts occur. At test-time it maps a logged dataset and one sampled target response per prompt to a value estimate in a single forward pass, with no per-task fitting. On HelpSteer2 and UltraFeedback with Qwen, Llama, and Gemma policies, PFN-OPE achieves 2.0 to 9.3 times lower error than the best baselines across all tested configurations in the reward-shifted settings.

</details>


### [66] [Similar Predictive Fit but Different Latent Dynamics: Characterizing Learned Dynamical Structure in Personalized Models of Brain Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10850)
*Rita Huan-Ting Peng,Nhat Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示：预测拟合相似的个体化模型，其内部学习到的动力学结构可能显著不同，因此个性化临床模型评估需兼顾预测性能与学习结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI模型走向临床决策与个性化治疗，仅凭预测准确率已不足以理解模型究竟学到了什么，需探究个体化潜动力学能否揭示与临床相关的差异，即便预测拟合相似。

Method: 基于TUEG预训练的轻量CNN–Transformer脑电基础模型提取片段级表征；在TUEP上将表征映射到共享潜状态空间，并为每个受试者独立拟合稀疏多项逻辑转移分布（mLTD），得到个体化转移依赖图W_n；分析198名受试者（99癫痫/99非癫痫），采用图派生特征与5折受试者级交叉验证，并对比留出对数似然与下一状态预测AUROC。

Result: k=4时癫痫组学习到的依赖结构显著更稠密（p=1.1×10^-7），k=6时同样成立（19.90 vs 13.46；p=5.2×10^-5）；图特征具中等组别区分度（AUROC 0.68@k=4；0.65@k=6）。相反，留出对数似然两组几乎一致（-0.992 vs -0.991；p=0.95），下一状态预测也相当（AUROC 0.855 vs 0.861；p=0.54）。

Conclusion: 相似的预测拟合并不意味相似的学习动力学：两组可同样可预测，却在个体化模型学到的内部动力学结构上差异显著。这一区分提示个性化临床模型应在预测性能之外同时评估学习结构。

Abstract: As AI models move toward clinical decision-making and personalized treatment, understanding \emph{what} a model learns is important beyond predictive accuracy alone. We investigate whether personalized latent dynamics reveal clinically associated differences even when predictive fit is similar. A lightweight CNN--Transformer EEG foundation model pretrained on the Temple University EEG Corpus (TUEG) extracts segment-level representations.
