Using the Temple University Epilepsy Corpus (TUEP), representations are mapped to a shared latent-state space, and sparse multinomial logistic transition distributions (mLTD) are fit independently to each subject to obtain personalized transition-dependency graphs $W_n$. Analyses include $n{=}198$ subjects (99 epilepsy / 99 non-epilepsy). At $k{=}4$, epilepsy subjects exhibit substantially denser learned dependency structure ($p{=}1.1\times10^{-7}$), with the same pattern at $k{=}6$ (19.90 vs. 13.46; $p{=}5.2\times10^{-5}$). Graph-derived features provide moderate group discrimination under 5-fold subject-wise cross-validation (AUROC 0.68 at $k{=}4$; 0.65 at $k{=}6$). In contrast, held-out log-likelihood is nearly identical between groups at $k{=}4$ ($-0.992$ vs. $-0.991$; $p{=}0.95$), with similarly matched next-state prediction (AUROC 0.855 vs. 0.861; $p{=}0.54$). Thus, similar predictive fit does not imply similar learned dynamics: groups can be comparably predictable while differing substantially in the internal dynamical structure learned by personalized models. This distinction motivates evaluating learned structure alongside predictive performance in personalized clinical models.

</details>


### [67] [KDFP: A first-principles approach to knowledge distillation in large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10854)
*Ryan Swift,Konstantinos Psounis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出KDFP方法，用于大语言模型的白盒通用知识蒸馏，在9个基准上优于现有方法1.6%–4.9%，并通过临时参数减少将训练效率最高提升99.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LLM蒸馏研究多聚焦于后训练阶段习得的能力（如指令遵循、思维链推理和工具使用），而面向LLM的通用知识蒸馏存在较大研究空白。通用知识蒸馏对于开发可部署于边缘设备的高效、隐私友好系统至关重要。

Method: 采用第一性原理方法，重新评估既有工作的经验，并开展新探索，提出KDFP——一种面向LLM白盒通用知识蒸馏的新型方法论，核心在于通过临时参数减少（ephemeral parameter reduction）提升训练效率。

Result: 在9个基准上，KDFP相比现有方法提升1.6%–4.9%，同时训练效率最高提升99.1%。

Conclusion: KDFP为LLM通用知识蒸馏提供了有效且高效的解决方案，填补了该领域的研究空白，有利于边缘设备上的高效、隐私保护部署。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is an established technique for improving the capabilities of small, efficient student models by training them with the representations of larger, more capable teacher models. Much of the recent work in the distillation of large language models (LLMs) has focused on distilling abilities learned during post-training, such as instruction following, chain-of-thought reasoning, and tool usage. This has left a large research gap in general knowledge distillation for LLMs, which is essential for developing efficient and private systems suitable for deployment on edge devices. We take a first-principles approach, evaluating previous lessons from prior works and conducting new explorations to develop a distillation methodology suitable for modern LLMs. We present KDFP, a novel methodology for white-box general knowledge distillation in LLMs. We demonstrate that KDFP outperforms existing methods by 1.6% $-$ 4.9% across 9 benchmarks while increasing training efficiency by up to 99.1% through ephemeral parameter reduction.

</details>


### [68] [Shape irregularity of Life-Like Network Automaton rules as an indicator of classification performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10867)
*Lucas C. S. Oliveira,Michiel Rollier,Jan Baetens,Odemir M. Bruno*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"jaggedness"指标来衡量Life-Like Network Automata转移规则与锯齿波形的相似度，证明其可作为混沌性与敏感性的理论代理，并据此设计启发式规则搜索策略，在精度损失小于5%的情况下将计算开销降低90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂网络分类需要兼具尺度不变性与计算效率的高层结构刻画。基于LLNA的方法无需提供特征即可从涌现的时序模式中提取网络描述符，但其效果受制于一个高代价的组合优化问题：自动机转移规则的选择。现有文献依赖穷举搜索，在大规模应用中不可行，因此亟需高效的规则选择方法。

Method: 作者提出"jaggedness"这一属性，用于量化LLNA转移函数与锯齿形状的相似程度，揭示规则空间具有内在结构；从理论上论证该指标是混沌性与敏感性的代理；并基于jaggedness设计启发式搜索策略来引导规则选择。

Result: 实验表明，所提方法在分类精度上达到全局最优解的5%以内，同时相比穷举方法将计算开销降低90%。

Conclusion: 研究为优化基于自动机的模式识别方法提供了一个新颖且高效的框架，说明规则空间的jaggedness结构可有效替代昂贵的穷举搜索。

Abstract: Complex Network (CN) classification requires high-level structural characterizations that are both scale-invariant and computationally efficient. Methods based on Life-Like Network Automata (LLNA) offer an interesting way to extract network descriptors by leveraging emergent temporal patterns without requiring provided features, but their efficacy is bottlenecked by a high-cost combinatorial optimization problem: the selection of the automaton transition rule. While current literature relies on exhaustive searches that are unfeasible for large-scale applications, this work reveals that the rule space is fundamentally structured by a property we term ``jaggedness'', that quantifies the resemblance of a LLNA transition function with a sawtooth shape. We demonstrate that this metric acts as a theoretical proxy for chaoticity and sensitivity -- properties essential for generating discriminative dynamic behaviors among network categories. Moreover, we introduce a heuristic search strategy that uses jaggedness to guide the rule selection. Experimental results show that our approach achieves classification accuracies within 5% of the global optimum while reducing computational overhead by 90% compared to exhaustive approach. Our findings provide a novel, efficient, framework for optimizing automata-based methods for pattern recognition.

</details>


### [69] [Barron Optimal Transport I: Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10875)
*Evan Dogariu,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出以Barron能量替代经典动能L²能量的最优测度输运框架，用以刻画神经网络表示复杂度，并定义新的概率测度度量，分析其在生成建模中的性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成建模与采样中，沿常/随机微分方程将参考分布映射到目标分布，其离散化对应深度残差网络，每层近似瞬时速度。因此自然地希望找到实现该输运的最有效神经网络表示，需要一种能衡量网络复杂度的代价度量。

Method: 以Benamou–Brenier的OT动力学表述为起点，将平均动能L²能量替换为Barron能量（衡量用单隐层表示给定向量的复杂度并反映特征学习的自适应性质），从而在概率测度空间上定义一个新的度量，与Wasserstein、Stein几何互补。

Result: 在该度量下量化了扩散生成建模的次优性：对由高斯经神经网络推前生成的数据，建立了超多项式的分数函数逼近下界；并进一步探讨自适应性的收益，以研究替代生成模型。

Conclusion: Barron输运几何为理解生成建模的表达效率与自适应特征学习提供新视角；配套论文将该几何用于采样，通过特征自适应扩展Stein变分梯度方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Motivated by recent applications in generative modeling and sampling, we introduce a framework for optimal measure transport where cost captures the notion of neural network complexity. In transport-based generative models, samples from a reference distribution (e.g. Gaussian) are mapped to samples of a target distribution along ordinary or stochastic differential equations. These are implemented as deep residual networks when discretized in time, where each hidden layer approximates the associated instantaneous velocity. Thus, given a pair of target and reference measures, a natural question is to search for the most efficient neural representation that implements this transport.
  Our starting point is the kinetic formulation of OT, due to Benamou and Brenier. We replace the average kinetic $L^2$ energy by the \emph{Barron} energy \cite{bach2017breaking, ma2022barron}, a natural norm which measures the complexity of representing a given vector field with a neural hidden layer, and which captures the adaptive properties of feature learning. This defines a metric on the space of probability measures, complementing existing Wasserstein and Stein geometries.
  In this work we examine the properties of this metric in the context of generative modeling. As a first application, we quantify the suboptimality of diffusion generative modeling in the Barron geometry by establishing super-polynomial score approximation lower bounds for data generated by neural network pushforwards of the Gaussian. We then investigate the benefit of adaptivity as a way to study alternative generative models. In a companion paper \cite{companionpaper} we leverage the Barron transport geometry for sampling applications, extending the scope of Stein variational gradient methods via feature adaptation.

</details>


### [70] [Gen-PINNs: Generative Adversarial Physics Informed Neural Networks for solving partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10897)
*Muhammad M. Akmal,Kamy Sepehrnoori,Michael J. Pyrcz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Gen-PINNs，一种确定性残差-对抗框架，结合生成器与判别器及傅里叶表示、谱诊断和动态权重，显著提升含激波/锐利前沿的非线性及高阶PDE求解精度与收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINNs在求解PDE时存在谱偏置、损失失衡与优化器停滞等局限；尽管GAN的对抗学习在数据驱动任务中表现优异，但其在确定性物理信息PDE求解中应用有限。

Method: 提出Gen-PINNs统一确定性残差-对抗框架：生成器用动态加权的物理信息损失学习PDE解；独立判别器针对理想零残差状态评估互补的PDE残差特征。并引入傅里叶表示以解析高频空间内容、正交谱诊断以量化频率相关误差，以及改进的基于梯度的动态权重系统，对物理、初始条件、边界条件与对抗损失目标进行加权。

Result: 在Burgers、Allen-Cahn和Kuramoto-Sivashinsky等非线性与高阶PDE上，相较于标准PINNs，在锐利前沿、刚性和高阶PDE求解中精度与收敛性均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 确定性残差-对抗学习是求解 challenging 非线性PDE的有效途径，Gen-PINNs展现出解决锐利/激波前沿行为的潜力。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are a widely used data-free method for solving Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) using machine learning. With recent advances in Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), adversarial learning has shown strong capabilities for modeling complex data-driven problems; however, the use of GANs in deterministic physics-informed PDE solutions remains limited. In this work, we first identify limitations of standard PINNs for solving PDEs, including spectral bias, loss imbalance, and optimizer stagnation. We then propose Generative Adversarial Physics-Informed Neural Networks (Gen-PINNs), a unified deterministic residual-adversarial framework designed to improve data-free solutions of PDEs with sharp or shock-front behavior. The generator learns the underlying PDE solution using dynamically weighted physics-informed loss components, while separate discriminators evaluate complementary PDE residual features against ideal zero-residual states. The framework further develops and adapts several methodological components, including a Fourier representation for resolving high-frequency spatial content, an orthonormal spectral diagnostic for quantifying frequency-dependent solution errors, and a modified gradient-based dynamic weighting system for physics, initial-condition, boundary-condition, and adversarial loss objectives. Gen-PINNs is tested against standard PINNs on nonlinear and higher-order PDEs, including the Burgers, Allen-Cahn, and Kuramoto-Sivashinsky equations. The results demonstrate substantial improvements in accuracy and convergence across sharp-front, stiff, and higher-order PDE solutions, highlighting the potential of deterministic residual-adversarial learning as an effective approach for solving challenging nonlinear PDEs.

</details>


### [71] [How Hackable Is Your Speech Quality Metric? A Corrected Protocol, a Benchmark, and What Patching Buys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10899)
*Ali Alavi,Donald S. Williamson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出语音质量预测器作为奖励时的"可攻破性(hackability)"缺乏统一度量，提出应以编码器往返后的未扰动分数为参照、并以多个随机种子攻击者的最坏情况作为防御指标，据此审计四种预测器及闭环攻防修补流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音质量预测器越来越多被用作奖励信号，但其"易被攻破程度"没有公认度量方式。现有测量存在两个缺陷：一是扰动经由神经编解码处理链传导，链条本身会改变分数，若以原始输入为基准会把这一偏移错误地归因于攻击；二是单一攻击者只是抽样而非测量，随机种子不同即导致结果大幅波动，因此防御声明必须基于多个攻击者的最坏情况。

Method: 将可攻破性测量的参照从原始输入改为"未扰动的编解码往返"输出；对同一固定预测器训练五个仅随机种子不同的攻击者，取成功率最坏情况作为指标；在该协议下比较四种已发表预测器(NISQA、SSL-MOS、DNSMOS、UTMOS)；并审计一个"攻击-检测-修补"闭环流程：检验其是否只在自身攻击空间内生效、与随机扰动基线比较、评估跨域SRCC代价，以及针对修补后预测器后训练增强器(enhancer)的迁移攻破程度(以PESQ为例)。

Result: 参照未扰动往返可将测得可攻破性改变最多四倍(某防御从0.31降至0.08)；五个仅种子不同的攻击者对同一预测器成功率在0.00至0.38之间。四种预测器差异显著：NISQA在90%语句上被攻破，SSL-MOS为21%，DNSMOS为14%，UTMOS为6%。闭环攻防修补仅在自身攻击空间内加固预测器，加固幅度小于攻击者间离散度；随机扰动基线即可与之持平；且跨域损失最高达0.30系统SRCC。针对修补后预测器后训练的增强器攻破程度显著更低(PESQ -0.03 对 -0.23)。

Conclusion: 语音质量预测器的可攻破性度量需纠正处理链偏移归因并以多攻击者最坏情况为准，否则估计不可靠；按此协议，现有预测器抗攻破能力差距悬殊，闭环"攻击-检测-修补"难以提供稳健且跨域有效的防御。代码、预注册与运行输出均已公开。

Abstract: Speech quality predictors are increasingly used as rewards, yet no agreed measure of their hackability exists. The usual measurement has two flaws. First, the perturbation reaches the predictor through a processing chain -- here a neural codec -- that shifts the score on its own, which scoring against the raw input charges to the attack. Referencing the unperturbed round trip instead changes measured hackability by up to a factor of four (0.31 to 0.08 for one defence). Second, one trained attacker is a sample, not a measurement: five attackers differing only in random seed reach success rates from 0.00 to 0.38 against one fixed predictor, so a defence claim needs the worst case over several. Under this protocol, four published predictors differ widely: NISQA is hacked on 90% of utterances, SSL-MOS on 21%, DNSMOS on 14% and UTMOS on 6%. We then audit a closed attack-detect-patch loop. It hardens the predictor only in its own attack space, by less than the spread between attackers; a random-perturbation baseline matches it; and it costs up to 0.30 system SRCC out of domain. Enhancers post-trained against patched predictors hack them far less (PESQ -0.03 versus -0.23). Code, preregistration and run outputs are released.

</details>


### [72] [Adaptive Multi-Discriminator WGAN Framework for Resource-Constrained Internet of Vehicles Using Reinforcement Learning and Game Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10926)
*Farhoud Jafari Kaleibar,Amr M. Zaki,Marin Litoiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应多判别器Wasserstein GAN（MD-WGAN）框架，结合强化学习与博弈论协调，在车联网环境下联合优化GAN训练精度、资源效率、通信开销与时延，在NGSIM数据上取得最低RMSE与MAE并显著降低CPU与内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将机器学习负载作为网络服务带来资源编排难题：需决定任务节点分配、通信与计算预算划分，并在移动性导致的连接性与节点可用性变化下维持服务质量。GAN在车联网（IoV）中部署是该问题的典型困难场景：资源受限、拓扑动态、优化目标相互竞争，传统GAN架构难以同时实现高精度、高效资源利用、低时延与低通信开销。

Method: 提出自适应多判别器Wasserstein GAN（MD-WGAN）框架，融合强化学习与博弈论协调。路边单元托管生成器并配以DQN智能体，用于选择判别器子集并在移动车载节点间管理分布式训练；博弈论协调步骤在生成器与判别器之间分配训练轮次。构建统一优化目标，将对抗学习质量与车辆约束下的资源效率、通信开销和时延绑定，使框架随网络状况变化持续自适应调整训练行为。

Result: 基于真实NGSIM轨迹数据的评估表明：预测精度与先进GAN基线相当，取得所有对比方法中最低的RMSE（1.029）与MAE（0.894）；同时资源效率显著提升，平均CPU利用率约降低28%、平均内存占用约降低6%，通信开销与时延具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在车联网动态约束下同时兼顾生成质量与系统效率，验证了强化学习与博弈论协同在多判别器WGAN资源编排中的有效性，为移动网络服务场景下的GAN部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Managing machine learning workloads as a network service introduces a resource-orchestration problem distinct from conventional model training; which nodes should be allocated to a task, how communication and computation budgets should be divided among them, and how service quality should be sustained as connectivity and node availability change with mobility. Deploying Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs) in Internet of Vehicles (IoV) environments is a demanding instance of this problem; resource constraints, dynamic network topologies, and competing optimization objectives mean that traditional GAN architectures cannot simultaneously achieve high accuracy, efficient resource use, low delay, and low communication overhead. This paper introduces an adaptive multi-discriminator Wasserstein GAN (MD-WGAN) framework that integrates reinforcement learning with game-theoretic coordination to address these challenges jointly. In our framework, roadside units host generators paired with Deep Q-Network (DQN) agents that select discriminator subsets and manage distributed training across mobile vehicular nodes, while a game-theoretic coordination step allocates training epochs between generators and discriminators. A unified optimization objective ties adversarial learning quality to resource efficiency, communication overhead, and latency under vehicular constraints, allowing the framework to continuously adapt its training behavior as network conditions change. Evaluation on real-world NGSIM trajectory data shows that the framework attains prediction accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art GAN baselines - the lowest RMSE (1.029) and MAE (0.894) among all evaluated methods - while markedly improving resource efficiency: average CPU utilization is reduced by roughly 28% and mean memory usage by roughly 6%, at competitive communication overhead and latency.

</details>


### [73] [Coefficient Calibration as Selection Pressure in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10931)
*Mattia Billa,Veronica Guidetti,Federica Mandreoli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文针对模因符号回归中系数校准影响结构选择的问题，提出 Dirichlet-Sinkhorn 常数平均（DSCA）校准策略：将数据划分为协变量分布不同但等大小的子集，分别校准后在参数均值处评估，从而在选择中引入对协变量偏移稳健的压力，并在合成与真实数据上优于集中式 BFGS 与 LM 校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模因符号回归中，候选结构需在校准系数后进行比较，因此校准协议本身参与进化选择。传统集中式校准在合并样本的最优点评估每个结构，忽略了该最优解在协变量偏移下的稳定性，可能偏好依赖样本特异系数的拟合结构，导致过拟合与泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出 Dirichlet-Sinkhorn 常数平均（DSCA）校准策略：将优化数据划分为大小相等但协变量分布不同的子集；对每个候选表达式在每个子集上独立校准；以这些分区校准所得参数的均值作为最终评估点。理论上分析 DSCA 相对于集中式校准的额外损失：对正确设定且可识别的表达式，该额外损失在总体层面趋于消失；在模型误设定时，若分区校准不能聚合到合并最优解，则额外损失持续存在。

Result: 在合成基准与十个真实数据集上，以负对数似然及 AIC、BIC 为选择准则时，DSCA 相比集中式 BFGS 和 Levenberg-Marquardt 校准提升了函数形式恢复效果并改善了精度-复杂度权衡。机制分析表明 DSCA 的额外损失与泛化差距相关，且该效应无法通过重复集中式拟合复现。

Conclusion: 受控的异质校准可为符号回归搜索提供一种互补的选择压力，即通过分区间的校准不一致性抑制对样本特异系数的依赖，从而改善结构选择与泛化表现。

Abstract: In memetic symbolic regression, candidate structures are compared after coefficient calibration, so the calibration protocol itself contributes to evolutionary selection. Standard centralized calibration evaluates each structure at its pooled-sample optimum, ignoring how stable this calibration is under covariate shifts, and can thus favor structures whose fit relies on sample-specific coefficients.
  We propose Dirichlet-Sinkhorn Constant Averaging (DSCA), a calibration strategy that partitions the optimization data into equally sized subsets with different covariate distributions, calibrates each candidate independently on every partition, and evaluates it at the mean of the resulting parameters. We show that the excess loss of DSCA relative to centralized calibration vanishes at the population level for correctly specified, identifiable expressions, whereas under misspecification it persists when partition-specific calibrations do not aggregate to the pooled optimum.
  On synthetic benchmarks and ten real-world datasets, DSCA improves functional recovery and the accuracy-complexity trade-off over centralized Broyden-Fletcher-Goldfarb-Shanno and Levenberg-Marquardt calibration, under selection by negative log-likelihood and by the Akaike and Bayesian information criteria. Mechanism analyses associate the DSCA excess loss with the generalization gap and show that the effect is not reproduced by repeated centralized fitting. These results indicate that controlled heterogeneous calibration provides a complementary source of selection pressure in symbolic-regression search.

</details>


### [74] [World-Model Policy Arbiter for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10932)
*Junwei Quan,Evgenii Opryshko,Nicholas Rhinehart,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WMPA 是一种测试时框架，将一组冻结的目标条件策略通过世界模型想象 rollout 与共享价值函数评估组成策略组合，并在短承诺区间内执行最优策略，从而在 OGBench 18 个数据集上把宏平均成功率从 44% 提升到 58%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线目标条件强化学习已有多种目标到达算法，但没有任何单一算法能在不同环境、目标乃至同一任务的不同阶段都表现最佳。因此作者希望不再只部署最优策略，而是把一组冻结的目标条件策略作为策略组合集体使用，在每个状态决定由哪个策略行动。

Method: 在每个状态选择策略并不直接：各策略的价值函数尺度可能不同甚至不存在，无法直接比较；需要在一次只能执行一个策略的前提下判断各策略可能到达的状态；还需避免频繁切换导致控制不稳定。为此提出 World-Model Policy Arbiter（WMPA）：给定一组冻结策略，在学到的状态空间世界模型中分别 rollout 每个冻结策略，用共享的目标条件价值函数评估想象出的未来，并执行得分最高的策略一个短承诺区间，再进行下一轮仲裁。该方法只需一批冻结的目标条件策略库，无需重新训练策略，也不需要特权任务知识。

Result: 在 OGBench 官方评估协议下，覆盖迷宫导航以及立方体、场景和拼图操作共 18 个基于状态的数据集，WMPA 将宏平均成功率从每个数据集所选最佳策略的 44% 提升到 58%，并在 12 个数据集上取得统计显著提升，包括 cube-double-play 上 +33 个百分点和 scene-play 上 +36 个百分点。

Conclusion: WMPA 表明冻结的目标条件策略组合可以通过世界模型想象与共享价值评估进行测试时仲裁，在不重训练、不依赖特权知识的情况下显著且稳定地优于逐数据集最佳单一策略。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) has produced a diverse set of goal-reaching algorithms, yet no single algorithm performs best across environments, goals, and even different phases of the same task. Rather than deploying only the best-performing policy, we ask whether a set of frozen goal-conditioned policies can be used collectively as a portfolio, deciding at every state which policy should act. Choosing a policy at each state is not straightforward. The policies' own value functions cannot be compared directly: they may use different scales, and some policies have no value function. We need to judge each policy by the states it is likely to reach, even though we can execute only one policy at a time. We also need to avoid switching so often that control becomes unstable. To address these challenges, we introduce World-Model Policy Arbiter (WMPA), a test-time framework that, given a set of frozen policies as input, rolls out each frozen policy in a learned state-space world model, evaluates the imagined futures with a shared goal-conditioned value function, and executes the highest-scoring policy for a short commitment interval before the next round of arbitration (policy selection). WMPA assumes access to a bank of frozen goal-conditioned policies and requires neither policy retraining nor privileged task-specific knowledge. Under the official OGBench evaluation protocol on 18 state-based datasets spanning maze navigation as well as cube, scene, and puzzle manipulation, WMPA improves the macro-average success rate from the 44% achieved by the best policy selected per dataset to 58%, with statistically significant gains on 12 datasets. These gains include +33 percentage points on cube-double-play and +36 percentage points on scene-play.

</details>


### [75] [Rethinking the Tradeoff Between Temporal Encoding and Nonlinear Computation in Spiking Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10933)
*Hanfei Liu,Shuchang Feng,Yanxia Chen,Changzeng Fu,Shiqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Spora，通过联合设计脉冲编码与注意力算子，缓解脉冲语言模型在短时间窗内表征连续语义特征与保留非线性注意力运算之间的权衡。Spora 用二元时间权重让 T 个脉冲承载最高 T bit 的组合值，并提出 UBS/BBS 两种编码以支持累加-移位点积与整数指数映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲语言模型面临一个核心矛盾：一方面需要在较短时间窗内表征连续的语义特征，另一方面又要保留代价较高的非线性注意力操作。现有脉冲计数读出方式的信息容量仅为 O(log2 T) bit，难以高效承载连续语义信息，因此需要在脉冲编码与注意力算子层面进行协同设计。

Method: 提出 Spora，联合设计脉冲编码与注意力算子。采用二元时间权重，使 T 个脉冲可表示容量最高达 T bit 的组合值（相比脉冲计数读出的 O(log2 T) bit）。设计两种编码：UBS（单极二元脉冲）利用阈值与脉冲触发的残差衰减产生非负整数码；BBS（双极二元脉冲）分离符号与幅值并为有符号激活学习尺度。这些表示支持注意力中的累加-移位点积与整数指数映射。

Result: 在四时间步设置下，Spora 取得 76.6 的平均 GLUE 分数与 44.1 的 CoLA MCC，分别较 SpikeLM 提升 1.2 与 6.2 分。将 BBS 扩展至六步后，两项分数进一步提升至 78.2 与 47.4。

Conclusion: 通过条件衰减分析、等预算激活量化对比、事件负载统计与定点评估，进一步刻画了编码保真度与计算代价之间的联系，表明 Spora 的编码-注意力协同设计可在提升性能的同时明确其计算开销特性。

Abstract: Spiking language models face a tradeoff between representing continuous semantic features over short temporal windows and retaining costly nonlinear attention operations. We introduce Spora, which jointly designs spike encodings and attention operators. Binary temporal weights let $T$ spikes represent compositional values with up to $T$ bits of capacity, compared with $O(\log_2 T)$ bits for spike-count readout. Unipolar Binary Spiking (UBS) uses thresholds and spike-triggered residual decay to produce non-negative integer codes; Bipolar Binary Spiking (BBS) separates sign and magnitude and learns a scale for signed activations. These representations support accumulation-and-shift dot products and integer-exponent mappings in attention. With four time steps, Spora achieves 76.6 average GLUE score and 44.1 CoLA MCC, improving over SpikeLM by 1.2 and 6.2 points, respectively. Extending BBS to six steps raises these scores to 78.2 and 47.4. Conditional-decay analysis, matched-budget activation-quantization comparisons, event-workload statistics, and fixed-point evaluation further characterize the connection between encoding fidelity and computational cost.

</details>


### [76] [RH-Detect: A Unified Benchmark for Reward Hacking Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10947)
*Junwei Quan,Evgenii Opryshko,Rohan Subramani,Igor Gilitschenski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RH-Detect 基准，整合 11 个公开数据集中与奖励劫持相关的子集（92,761 行、6 类行为），在统一 schema 下评测 6 个现成语言模型作为检测器，最优模型池化 AUROC 达 0.962，并揭示了多轮工具使用场景下的检测性能缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励劫持（reward hacking）指模型利用评估信号而非真正完成任务，威胁已部署语言模型系统的可靠性。现有数据集在标签、响应格式和元数据规范上各不相同，导致检测器结果难以比较，缺乏统一、可复现的评测基准。

Method: 构建 RH-Detect 基准：从 11 个公开数据集中抽取奖励劫持相关子集，统一为共同 schema，含 92,761 行、6 类行为。在 5,021 个开放式评测单元（任务提示 + 自由形式模型续写，含多轮工具使用轨迹）上，以零训练方式评测 5 个家族共 6 个现成语言模型作为检测器。同时将基准作为训练数据集，采用留一源（hold-out each source）策略，测试单 token SFT 及 GRPO 后续训练对检测性能的影响。

Result: 最优模型池化 AUROC 为 0.962，准确率超 93%。对于四个最强模型，在两个多轮工具使用数据集 MALT 和 TRACE 上，相同决策阈值下准确率比其他来源低 10.7–15.9 个百分点。不同输入格式对各模型影响不同：移除 thinking 使 Qwen3.5-4B 的 AUROC 从 0.779 升至 0.849，却使 Qwen Flash 从 0.977 降至 0.950。作为训练数据时，留一源设置下单 token SFT 在六个留出源中的五个上提升平均 AUROC；针对失败源的 GRPO 后续训练带来检测性能的小幅提升。

Conclusion: 单一池化分数会掩盖数据来源、检测器输入和训练流程之间的差异；RH-Detect 为奖励劫持检测提供了统一评测框架，并指出多轮工具使用场景是部署时监控的关键缺口。

Abstract: Reward hacking, where a model exploits an evaluation signal without completing the intended task, threatens the reliability of deployed language model systems. Existing datasets use different labels, response formats, and metadata conventions, making detector results difficult to compare. We present RH-Detect, a benchmark that combines reward-hacking-relevant subsets from eleven public datasets, comprising 92,761 rows and six behavior categories, into a common schema. On 5,021 open-ended evaluation units, each comprising a task prompt and a free-form model continuation, including multi-turn tool-use trajectories, we evaluate six off-the-shelf language models from five families as reward hacking detectors without additional training. The best model achieves a pooled AUROC of 0.962, with accuracy above 93%. For the four strongest models, however, accuracy on the two multi-turn tool-use datasets, MALT and TRACE, is 10.7-15.9 percentage points lower than on the other sources at a common decision threshold, highlighting a key gap for deployment-time monitoring. We find that different input formats have different effects across models. Removing thinking raises Qwen3.5-4B AUROC from 0.779 to 0.849, but lowers Qwen Flash from 0.977 to 0.950. We also evaluate the benchmark as a training dataset for detectors. Holding out each source in turn, single-token SFT improves average AUROC on five of six held-out sources. A GRPO follow-up on that failure case yields a slight improvement in detection performance. Our results show that a single pooled score can conceal variation across data sources, detector inputs, and training procedures.

</details>


### [77] [SPD-MetaFormer is what you need for small-data brain decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10952)
*Zhida Wang,Wei Lyu,Guo Yu,Sui Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文发现SPD流形注意力模型的注意力权重训练后接近均匀，替换为均匀权重对性能几乎无影响，据此提出免注意力架构SPD-MetaFormer，在三个EEG基准上取得竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑信号解码面临神经记录噪声大、个体差异大且标注数据有限的挑战。近期基于对称正定（SPD）流形的注意力模型利用协方差与连接性表示取得良好性能，但学习到的token加权（注意力权重）的实际贡献尚不明确，需要澄清其作用。

Method: 分析两种代表性架构MAtt（基于对数欧氏几何）与GBWAtt（基于广义Bures–Wasserstein几何），考察其学到的注意力权重分布；指出在原始softmax缩放下有界相似度参数化限制了注意力权重对比度；在训练与评估全程用均匀权重替代学习权重进行对照实验。进而提出SPD-MetaFormer：基于对数欧氏几何的均匀加权Fréchet聚合的免注意力架构，主干使用测地残差更新摘要token，并用共享谱前馈映射变换所有token，最后经学习加权读出；token在进入切空间分类前始终保持SPD取值。

Result: MAtt与GBWAtt训练后注意力权重接近均匀；将学习权重替换为均匀权重后，平均预测性能几乎不变，且保持各自原有的聚合几何。SPD-MetaFormer在三个EEG基准上相对已发表的欧氏与流形基线取得竞争性结果；另通过匹配复现实验对比了MAtt与GBWAtt中学习加权与均匀加权的效果。

Conclusion: 在所研究的短序列、有限数据场景下，精心设计的SPD架构可作为自适应流形注意力的更简单且有效的替代方案。

Abstract: Brain signal decoding is challenging because neural recordings are noisy and vary across individuals, while labeled data are often limited. Recent attention-based models on the symmetric positive definite (SPD) manifold have nevertheless achieved strong performance using covariance and connectivity representations, yet the contribution of learned token weighting remains unclear. We examine two representative architectures, MAtt (based on log-Euclidean geometry) and GBWAtt (based on generalized Bures--Wasserstein geometry), and find that their learned attention weights remain close to uniform after training. We relate this behavior to bounded similarity parameterizations that, under the original softmax scaling, limit attention-weight contrast. Moreover, replacing learned weights with uniform weights, throughout training and evaluation, has little effect on mean predictive performance while preserving each model's original aggregation geometry. Motivated by these findings, we introduce SPD-MetaFormer, an attention-free architecture built on uniformly weighted Fréchet aggregation under log-Euclidean geometry. Its backbone uses a geodesic residual to update a summary token and a shared spectral feedforward map to transform all tokens, followed by a learned weighted readout. Token states remain SPD-valued until tangent-space classification. Across three EEG benchmarks, SPD-MetaFormer achieves competitive results relative to published Euclidean and manifold baselines. Separate matched reproductions test learned versus uniform weighting within MAtt and GBWAtt. These results suggest that, in the short-sequence and limited-data regimes studied, carefully designed SPD architectures can provide a simpler and effective alternative to adaptive manifold attention.

</details>


### [78] [TRACE: A Governance Framework for Measuring Explainability Debt in Production AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10957)
*Harish Kant Pathak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出TRACE七工具治理框架，用于量化、追踪和修复生产AI系统中的“可解释性债务”，并以一个欺诈检测系统的12个月纵向案例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的高风险AI系统会累积一种现有监控框架无法检测的治理负债：当监管者、审计者或受影响个体要求问责时，系统逐渐丧失对单个决策进行解释的能力。且现有系统级指标无法揭示这种风险的不对称分布。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，包含七个工具：基础工具EDS（可解释性债务分数）量化低于治理设定置信阈值的生产决策比例；辅以DART（债务累积速率追踪与违约预测）、SHIV（场景健康与完整性验证）、FDE（特征漂移与因果归因）、HVE（人工验证引擎）、AIDE（审计干预决策引擎）和ZERO（零可解释性风险优化器）。通过对处理每日5万笔金融交易的生产欺诈检测系统进行12个月纵向案例研究来验证。

Result: 该系统准确率98.46%、ROC-AUC为0.9990；审计日EDS为0.23，可通过DART轨迹分析提前六个月统计预测（beta=0.008/周，R²=0.94，95% CI [0.006,0.010]）；78%的可解释性债务集中在最高监管风险类别（金额超过1万美元的交易），这种风险不对称对系统级指标完全不可见。

Conclusion: TRACE为生产AI部署中的欧盟《人工智能法案》第13条合规提供了首个量化运营架构，并确立了独立于“解释生成”的“解释治理”新子学科。

Abstract: Production AI systems deployed in high-stakes domains accumulate a governance liability that existing monitoring frameworks fail to detect: the progressive inability to explain individual decisions when regulators, auditors, or affected individuals demand accountability. We introduce TRACE (Transparency, Risk, Accountability, Compliance, and Explainability), a seven-instrument governance framework for measuring, tracking, and remediating Explainability Debt in production AI systems. The foundational instrument, the Explainability Debt Score (EDS), quantifies the proportion of production decisions falling below a governance-defined explainability confidence threshold at any point in time. Complementary instruments include DART (Debt Accumulation Rate Tracker for breach forecasting), SHIV (Scenario Health and Integrity Validator for daily governance), FDE (Feature Drift Evaluator for causal attribution), HVE (Human Validation Engine), AIDE (Audit Intervention Decision Engine), and ZERO (Zero Explainability Risk Optimiser for remediation). Through a twelve-month longitudinal case study of a production fraud detection system processing 50,000 daily financial transactions, achieving 98.46% accuracy and ROC-AUC of 0.9990, we demonstrate that an EDS of 0.23 on audit day was statistically predictable six months in advance using DART trajectory analysis (beta = 0.008/week, R-squared = 0.94, 95% CI: [0.006, 0.010]), and that 78% of Explainability Debt was concentrated in the highest-regulatory-risk decision category (transactions above $10,000), a risk asymmetry completely invisible to system-level metrics. TRACE provides the first quantitative operational architecture for EU AI Act Article 13 compliance in production AI deployment, establishing a new subdiscipline of explanation governance distinct from explanation generation.

</details>


### [79] [Spectrally Targeted Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10965)
*Vishrut Goyal,Rohan Ramkumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Spectrally Targeted Muon (STM)，通过仅在奇异值高于/低于阈值 τ 的谱上做正交化来插值 normalized SGD 与 Muon；实验表明 Muon 的有效性主要来自放大过小、原始尺度下无法训练的方向，而非抑制大奇异方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 通过把每次更新的奇异值全部置为 1 来正交化更新矩阵，在 LLM 训练中效果显著。但其成功机制尚不明确：究竟是放大了梯度下降所忽视的小奇异方向，还是抑制了破坏训练的大而退化的方向？需要一种可解耦这两类效应的实验手段。

Method: 提出 Spectrally Targeted Muon (STM)：只对高于或低于阈值 τ 的奇异值做正交化，使 τ 变化时在 normalized SGD 与 Muon 之间连续插值。为隔离相关奇异子空间，对移位后的 Gram 矩阵用 Newton-Schulz 迭代求投影，避免 SVD 开销。在 CIFAR-10 与 NanoGPT speedrun 上评测变体，并追踪梯度、更新、权重矩阵的有效秩，以及新指标——更新与权重矩阵等谱流形切空间的对齐度。

Result: 1) 动量的最小奇异值并非噪声：对每个矩阵除最大的少数奇异值外全部正交化，几乎可匹敌 Muon，却只触及动量的一小部分；仅正交化前几个大奇异值则明显不足，尽管其几乎占据全部动量。在语言模型上，动量所有奇异值都远小于 1，故定向正交化只能起放大作用，Muon 的优势在于让原始尺度下过小、无法训练的方向变得可训练。2) 压缩最大奇异值负责保持参数谱平坦，成本低，但不是驱动 loss 下降的原因。3) AdamW 与 Muon 的差异主要在于其构建结构的速率缓慢，这解释了其起步更慢，以及 warmup 有利于 AdamW 却只损害 Muon。

Conclusion: Muon 的增益源于对过小奇异方向的放大而非对大奇异方向的抑制；谱整形（压平参数谱）与 loss 优化目标可解耦；AdamW 与 Muon 的核心区别在于结构形成速度，从而统一解释了 warmup 对二者相反的作用。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer orthogonalizes each update matrix, setting all of its singular values to one, and has proven highly effective for training large language models. It remains unclear, however, whether this success comes from amplifying small singular directions that gradient descent neglects or from suppressing large, degenerate directions that disrupt training. We introduce Spectrally Targeted Muon, which orthogonalizes only the singular values above or below a threshold $τ$, so that varying $τ$ interpolates between normalized SGD and Muon. It isolates the relevant singular subspaces with projections computed by Newton-Schulz iteration on a shifted Gram matrix, so no SVD is needed. We evaluate these variants on the CIFAR-10 and NanoGPT speedruns, tracking the effective rank of gradient, update, and weight matrices and a new metric, the alignment of updates with the tangent space of the weight matrix's isospectral manifold. We find three things. First, the small singular values of the momentum are not noise. Orthogonalizing everything except the few largest singular values of each matrix nearly matches Muon while touching only a small fraction of the momentum, whereas orthogonalizing only the top falls well short even though it holds almost all of it. On language models every momentum singular value is far below one, so targeted orthogonalization can only amplify, and Muon wins by making directions that are too small to train on at their raw scale trainable. Second, shrinking the largest singular values is what keeps the parameter spectrum flat; this is cheap, and it is not what drives the loss. Third, AdamW differs from Muon mainly in how slowly it builds structure, which explains its slower start and why warmup helps AdamW but only hurts Muon.

</details>


### [80] [ASPIRE: Saddle-Point Discovery through Set Prediction and Physical Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10969)
*Yucheng Zhao,Quanyou Zhang,Shaoxiang Qin,Haixuan Xu,Xiongye Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ASPIRE 框架，用等变集合预测器 Ev-Quiformer 单次预测多个鞍点候选并结合 Dimer 精修，加速热激活扩散与缺陷演化模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件驱动模型预测热激活扩散与缺陷演化需识别原子重排机制及其激活势垒，而鞍点搜索是主要计算瓶颈：一个亚稳态可能对应多种重排，局部搜索成本高且易失败或重复收敛到同一鞍点。

Method: ASPIRE（Atomistic Saddle-Point Inference with Refinement for Events）从单一初始原子环境预测一组鞍点候选，并在原始原子间势上通过 Dimer 搜索精修；其等变集合预测器 Ev-Quiformer 包含：(i) 几何条件化的标量-向量事件槽以生成多个鞍点提议；(ii) 解码器，将每个槽映射为完整原子位移场，通过结合原子、槽与锚点相对向量及不变系数实现。同时贡献两个数据集 BCCFE4VACAV-4000 与 BCCFE-1TO4VAC。

Result: 理论上建立提议等变性的条件；实验上 ASPIRE 在基准上取得 77.20% 参考事件覆盖率，高于传统 Dimer 基线的 75.73%，且所需 Dimer 力评估次数约为其一半；50 个构型计时评测中，单构型墙钟时间由 478.8 s 降至 176.3 s。

Conclusion: ASPIRE 通过一次性预测多鞍点候选并精修，有效提升鞍点发现覆盖率并显著降低计算开销，为事件驱动的扩散与缺陷演化模拟提供高效方案。

Abstract: Predicting thermally activated diffusion and defect evolution with event-driven models requires identifying atomic rearrangement mechanisms and their activation barriers. Discovering the associated saddle points is a major computational bottleneck: multiple rearrangements may originate from one metastable state, while costly local searches can fail or repeatedly converge to the same saddle. To address this challenge, we introduce ASPIRE (Atomistic Saddle-Point Inference with Refinement for Events), a framework that predicts a set of saddle candidates from a single initial atomic environment and refines them through Dimer searches on the original interatomic potential. The framework's equivariant set predictor, Ev-Quiformer, integrates (i) geometry-conditioned scalar-vector event slots for generating multiple saddle-point proposals and (ii) a decoder that maps each slot to a full atomic displacement field by combining atom, slot, and anchor-relative vectors with invariant coefficients. We also contribute two datasets: (i) BCCFE4VACAV-4000, comprising 4,000 four-vacancy body-centered cubic iron configurations and 65,450 reference events grouped by initial state for set supervision and post-refinement evaluation; and (ii) BCCFE-1TO4VAC, comprising 5,372 configurations with one to four vacancies each. Theoretically, we establish conditions for proposal equivariance. Experimentally, ASPIRE achieves 77.20% reference-event coverage on this benchmark, compared with 75.73% for a conventional Dimer baseline, while requiring approximately half as many Dimer force evaluations. In a timing evaluation on 50 configurations, ASPIRE reduces wall time per configuration from 478.8 s to 176.3 s under the stated hardware settings.

</details>


### [81] [Transferability of Learned States in Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10972)
*Shunye Wang,Haochen Wen,Shuo Li Liu,Xuanyi Wang,Lihao Liu,Zhongying Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出“复用契约”框架，通过配对状态比较、匹配目标信息与预算、成本核算，将神经PDE求解器的最终精度拆分为解的精度、学习贡献与数值效用，并用文献审计与实验验证复用价值的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估神经PDE求解器中的“有效复用”存在困难：最终精度同时反映源域学习与目标时刻计算，二者混淆，难以判断性能提升究竟来自复用还是额外计算。

Method: 提出复用契约，分离解的精度、学习贡献与数值效用；采用配对状态比较、匹配目标信息与预算、成本核算。进行文献审计，从12篇论文提取18条版本化协议记录，记录保留状态、目标时刻资源与报告的控制项。在固定线性系统与残差容差下，构造两个具有相同解误差、能量误差与残差范数但CG迭代次数不同的初始猜测；覆盖240条源训练轨迹、两类线性PDE族、Fourier神经算子与卷积网络，并比较不同校正算法。

Result: 在64个分布内任务（63×63内部网格）中，相对预测误差≤5%的配对里，三种范数的下降均伴随更多CG迭代，两个库中平均任务速率分别为23.5%与23.9%；同一固定预测器的收益在不同校正算法间发生反转。基于功的选择在留出分布内任务上节省2.50-3.33次CG迭代；匹配自适应显示有限预算预训练价值。独立批次确认某物理训练Fourier神经算子相对零初始化Poisson预条件CG有0.73%的完整在线节省。

Conclusion: 复用评估需要匹配的状态比较与下游计算证据，不能仅凭最终精度判断；所提契约可有效揭示复用带来的真实数值效用。

Abstract: Assessing useful reuse in neural PDE solvers is challenging: final accuracy can reflect source learning and target-time computation. Our reuse contract separates solution accuracy, learning contribution, and numerical utility through paired state comparisons, matched target information and budgets, and cost accounting. A literature audit extracts 18 version-specific protocol records from 12 papers, documenting retained states, target-time resources, and reported controls. For a fixed linear system and residual tolerance, we construct two initial guesses with identical solution-error, energy-error, and residual norms, reaching the same solution with different conjugate-gradient (CG) iteration counts. Across 240 source-training trajectories, two linear PDE families, Fourier neural operators and convolutional networks, a fixed predictor's benefit reverses across correction algorithms. Among pairs with both relative prediction errors less than or equal to 5 percent on 64 in-distribution tasks (63 by 63 interior grids), reductions in all three norms accompany more CG iterations, at mean taskwise rates of 23.5 percent and 23.9 percent in two libraries. Work-based selection saves 2.50-3.33 CG iterations on held-out in-distribution tasks; matched adaptation demonstrates finite-budget pretraining value. Independent batches confirm a 0.73 percent complete online saving for one physics-trained Fourier neural operator against zero-initialized Poisson-preconditioned CG. Reuse requires matched state comparisons and downstream computational evidence.

</details>


### [82] [Budgeted Multi-Source Counterfactual Annotation for Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10974)
*Biao Xiang,Ali Eshragh,Yuexing Li,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究上下文bandit离策略评估（OPE）中反事实标注的预算化获取问题，在给定来源成本与误差画像下，构建整数分配优化问题以最小化估计量方差中依赖标注方案的分量，并提出单调改进的majorization-minimization算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略评估依赖日志数据，但行为策略覆盖不足会导致高方差重加权或奖励模型外推。反事实标注虽能提供未观测动作的证据，但专家与LLM等来源存在成本高、有偏、噪声大等问题，因此需要在预算约束下优化标注分配。

Method: 将预算化标注获取建模为在上下文-动作对与标注来源上的整数分配问题，最小化依赖标注方案的估计量方差分量；刻画首次标注阈值与局部标注价值区间；针对耦合多源问题，提出结合动态规划子程序的majorization-minimization算法。

Result: 在合成临床bandit与LLM标注的教育bandit实验中，与不做标注相比，所提分配方法分别将固定画像下的均方误差（MSE）降低20.58%和10.77%。

Conclusion: 预算化、来源异构的反事实标注分配可有效降低上下文bandit OPE的估计方差与MSE，所提算法具备理论单调性与实证有效性。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value of a target policy from logged data, but limited behavior-policy coverage can force high-variance reweighting or reward-model extrapolation. Counterfactual annotations can add evidence about unobserved actions, yet practical sources, including domain experts and large language models (LLMs), may be costly, biased, or noisy. We study budgeted acquisition of such annotations for contextual-bandit OPE. Given source-specific costs and error profiles, we formulate an integer allocation problem over context-action pairs and annotation sources to minimize the component of estimator variance that depends on the annotation plan. We characterize when annotations are valuable through a first-annotation threshold and local annotation-value regimes. For the coupled multi-source problem, we develop a majorization-minimization algorithm with dynamic-programming subroutines that monotonically improves the objective. Experiments in synthetic clinical and LLM-annotated education bandits show that our allocation method reduces fixed-profile mean squared error (MSE) by 20.58% and 10.77%, respectively, relative to no annotation.

</details>


### [83] [Optimizing Large Language Models with Chained LMOs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10975)
*Sungyoon Kim,Kaan Ozkara,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出链式线性最小化预言机（chained LMOs）框架统一多种Muon类优化器，解释链式组合的增益来源，并提出TensorChain优化器，在Qwen3预训练中优于Muon等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon催生了一系列组合多种矩阵归一化的优化器，但这些方法彼此割裂、缺乏统一视角；同时许多链式方法超出标准LMO框架，在光滑凸目标上可能发散，需要解释组合为何仍能带来收益。

Method: 提出chained LMOs，将相关优化器刻画为LMO的复合；借助线性联想记忆（linear associative memory）从理论上分析链式组合在各向异性嵌入下相对Muon的改进；基于该框架提出TensorChain，跨不同层堆叠兼容权重矩阵，并对三维张量沿各轴归一化。

Result: 在Qwen3 0.6B与1.7B预训练中，TensorChain在平均token效率上优于所有链式基线，在匹配验证损失下相较Muon平均节省9.6%的token。

Conclusion: chained LMOs提供了统一视角来理解Muon类优化器，链式组合的收益可通过线性联想记忆在各向异性嵌入下得到解释；TensorChain验证了该框架的有效性，显著提升预训练token效率。

Abstract: Muon has motivated a growing family of optimizers that compose multiple matrix normalizations, but these methods remain fragmented and lack a unified perspective. We introduce chained linear minimization oracles (chained LMOs), which cast these methods as compositions of LMOs. Despite their empirical success, many chains fall outside the standard LMO framework and can diverge on smooth convex objectives. To explain why composition can nevertheless help, we turn to linear associative memory and show that chaining can improve over Muon under anisotropic embeddings. Empirically, we propose TensorChain, a novel optimizer within the framework that stacks compatible weight matrices across different layers and normalizes the 3d tensor across its axes. In Qwen3 0.6B and 1.7B pretraining, TensorChain outperforms all chained baselines in average token efficiency, with average token savings of 9.6% over Muon at matched validation loss.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-Bandwidth Distribution Matching Distillation: On the Equivalence of Distribution Matching Distillation and Drifting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10989)
*Jialin Zhu,Xing Liu,Feixiang He,He Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 Drifting Model 与 DMD/DMD2 建立精确等价关系，并据此从 Drifting Model 视角提出改进方法 MBDMD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Drifting Model 在一步生成中潜力巨大，且已有工作揭示其与 DFSGM 的联系，但其与广泛使用的蒸馏方法 DMD/DMD2 之间尚无精确对应关系，尽管二者优化目标公式几乎相同。

Method: 将预训练 DFSGM 的速度场/噪声场转换为 Drifting Model 中的吸引力场，并从生成分布中估计排斥力场，从而证明训练 Drifting Model 与分布匹配蒸馏自然等价。基于该等价概念，从 Drifting Model 视角提出多带宽分布匹配蒸馏（MBDMD）方法。

Result: 理论上证明了 Drifting Model 训练与 DMD 的等价性；并据此提出改进的 MBDMD 方法。

Conclusion: 通过建立 Drifting Model 与 DMD 的精确对应，为一步生成模型与蒸馏方法的统一理解提供理论基础，并催生了 MBDMD 这一改进方法。

Abstract: Researchers are exploring effective one-step generative model continuously, and, Drifting Models (Deng et al., 2026), demonstrate great potential in one-step generation recently. There are works that reveal the connection between Diffusion & Flow Style Generative Models (DFSGMs) (Ho et al., 2020; Song et al., 2020a;b; Lipman et al., 2022; Liu et al., 2022) and Drifting Models (Li & Zhu, 2026; Lai et al., 2026; Turan et al., 2026). But no one has yet established a precise correspondence between the Drifting Model and the widely used distillation method- Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD/DMD2) (Yin et al., 2024b;a) to the best of our knowledge, even though their optimization objective formulas are virtually identical. In this paper, we prove that by converting the velocity-field / noise-field from the pre-trained DFSGMs into the attraction force field in Drifting Models and estimating the repulsion force field from the generative distribution, training the Drifting Model is naturally equivalent to the Distribution Matching Distillation. With this equivalent concept, we propose an improved method based on DMD from the Drifting Model's perspective- Multi-Bandwidth Distribution Matching Distillation (MBDMD).

</details>


### [85] [Invariant-Measure Reasoners: Stable Representations for Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10996)
*Yuto Inui,Takuya Konishi,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出不变测度推理器（ImR），用循环块更新下的不变测度（刻画潜在状态长期分布）作为稳定表征，通过预测头输出的期望进行预测，从而缓解潜在推理模型在循环深度增加时的预测不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在推理模型反复用同一循环块更新潜在状态，随着循环深度增加，状态序列可能收敛到状态空间的紧致子集，但未必收敛到不动点。现有模型通常仅对单个潜在状态施加预测头进行预测，而该状态在多次更新后仍可能变化，导致预测在不同循环深度间不稳定。因此需要一种稳定表征来解决预测不稳定问题。

Method: 提出不变测度推理器（ImR）框架：利用不变测度作为稳定表征，该测度描述潜在状态在紧致子集上的长期分布，且对循环块的更新保持不变。ImR 在该测度下对预测头输出取期望进行预测。采用两种使用方式：仅微调现有模型的预测头，以及从头训练模型。

Result: 两种方式均降低了预测不稳定性，并在迷宫与数独任务的多种设置中提升了准确率。某些设置下，用 ImR 训练的模型比现有模型更频繁地表现出非不动点行为，但仍能在此类行为下取得高准确率，而现有模型则不行。

Conclusion: 结果表明，ImR 能够利用原本会导致不稳定的动力学来服务于潜在推理，即把潜在的持续变化从缺陷转化为可利用的特性。

Abstract: Latent reasoning models repeatedly update a latent state using the same recurrent block. As the recurrent depth increases, the sequence of latent states may converge to a compact subset of the state space without necessarily converging to a fixed point. Existing models typically predict by applying a prediction head to a single latent state. However, the latent state can continue to change even after many updates, potentially making predictions unstable across recurrent depths. To address this instability, we introduce invariant-measure reasoners (ImR), a framework that uses an invariant measure as a stable representation. This measure describes the long-run distribution of latent states on the compact subset and is invariant under updates by the recurrent block. ImR predicts from the expectation of the prediction head's output under this measure. We use ImR in two ways: fine-tuning only the prediction head of existing models and training models from scratch. Both approaches reduce prediction instability and improve accuracy in many settings on maze and Sudoku tasks. In some settings, models trained with ImR exhibit non-fixed-point behavior more frequently than existing models yet achieve high accuracy even with such behavior, unlike existing models. These results suggest that ImR can leverage otherwise destabilizing dynamics for latent reasoning.

</details>


### [86] [F$^3$NO: Frequency-Decomposed Finite-Time Flow-map Neural Operators with Cross-Scale Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10998)
*Fan Wu,Cheng Jing,Kookjin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出频率分解有限时间流映射神经算子F³NO，用低频特征引导高频非线性细化，直接预测指定时刻状态并分段递归实现长时预测，在五个PDE基准上优于自回归与直接预测基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子虽能快速进行PDE预测，但重复（自回归）预测会累积误差，且细尺度结构难以准确解析，因此需要兼顾长时稳定性与高频细节分辨能力的预测方法。

Method: 采用频率分解的有限时间流映射神经算子：每层内通过跨尺度条件耦合将全局谱处理（低频）与局部细节细化（高频）相连，用更新的低频特征引导高频分支的非线性细化；模型直接预测指定未来时刻状态，并依据预测区间调节两分支贡献；对长轨迹，在短时段内并行预测、段间递归传播。

Result: 在五个PDE基准上，F³NO的预测精度优于自回归与直接预测基线。消融实验表明频率分解细化可在更少参数下提升精度，而分段策略的收益取决于空间分辨率与动力学机制。

Conclusion: 频率分解与跨尺度条件机制能有效提升神经算子PDE预测的精度与参数效率，有限时间流映射与分段递归的组合为长时轨迹预测提供了可行方案，但分段收益具有条件依赖性。

Abstract: Neural operators enable fast PDE forecasting, but repeated predictions accumulate errors and fine-scale structures remain difficult to resolve. We introduce a frequency-decomposed finite-time flow-map neural operator (F$^3$NO) that leverages updated low-frequency features to guide nonlinear refinement of high-frequency information. Within each layer, this cross-scale conditioning connects global spectral processing with local detail refinement. The model directly predicts states at specified future times and adjusts the contributions of the two branches according to the prediction interval. For longer trajectories, it combines parallel predictions within short temporal segments with recursive propagation between segments. Experiments on five PDE benchmarks demonstrate improved forecasting accuracy over autoregressive and direct-prediction baselines. Ablations show that frequency-decomposed refinement can improve accuracy with fewer parameters, while the benefits of segmentation depend on spatial resolution and dynamical regime.

</details>


### [87] [Low-rank tensor structure of precipitation and its application to satellite-reference merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11000)
*Ryan Solgi,Rohan Shankar,Hugo A. Loaiciga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将美国本土日降水建模为时空张量并采用CP分解，发现保留原生时空模态的重建精度更高；据此提出TMerge张量框架，将卫星降水与稀疏参考观测通过共享低秩时空因子融合，显著改进了IMERG产品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降水具有间歇性与变率大的特点，在大范围区域上精确估计困难；但其时空结构可能具备低秩表示潜力，而现有融合/订正方法（线性偏差订正、分位数映射、神经网络）在稀疏参考观测条件下改进有限。

Method: 将CONUS日降水表示为时空张量，应用CANDECOMP/PARAFAC（CP）分解，比较直接分解时空张量与分别分解独立日场或展开的时空矩阵的效果；在此基础上提出TMerge，通过共享低秩空间因子与时间因子融合卫星降水与稀疏参考观测，并用CPC参考观测订正IMERG Final Run。

Result: 2019—2022年期间，TMerge将相关系数由0.53提升至0.85，RMSE和MAE分别降低48.2%和29.3%；在各季节、降水强度区间和区域上均优于线性偏差订正、分位数映射和神经网络；改进在空间上连贯，且在IMERG误差最大的沿海地区最显著。

Conclusion: 低秩张量结构能够简洁地近似降水主导时空变率，为在大范围区域和有限参考观测条件下改进卫星降水估计提供了实用机制。

Abstract: The intermittent and variable nature of precipitation makes its accurate estimation over extended domains difficult, yet its spatiotemporal structure suggests that a low-rank representation may be possible. This work represents daily precipitation over the contiguous United States (CONUS) as spatiotemporal tensors and applies CANDECOMP/PARAFAC factorization, showing that preserving the native spatial and temporal modes yields more accurate reconstruction than factorizing independent daily fields or unfolded space--time matrices. Building on this finding, this work presents TMerge, a tensor-based framework that integrates satellite precipitation with sparse reference observations through shared low-rank spatial and temporal factors. TMerge was applied to correct the IMERG Final Run product with climate prediction center reference observations over CONUS. During 2019-2022, TMerge increased correlation from 0.53 to 0.85 and reduced root-mean-square error and mean absolute error by 48.2% and 29.3%, respectively. TMerge consistently outperformed linear bias correction, quantile mapping, and neural networks across seasons, precipitation-intensity regimes, and regions. Improvements were spatially coherent and largest in coastal regions where IMERG errors were greatest. These results demonstrate that low-rank tensor structure parsimoniously approximates the dominant spatiotemporal variability of precipitation and provides a practical mechanism for improving satellite estimates under limited reference observations over extended domains.

</details>


### [88] [Emergent Inverse-Depth Scaling From Nonlinearity In Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11063)
*Zirui Peng,Yizhou Liu,Ziming Liu,Jeff Gore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出非线性注意力导致损失随深度呈反比衰减，而非线性注意力模型的幂律深度缩放机制不同，深度缩放可能源于注意力的非线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论以幂律深度缩放解释参数量缩放，在线性注意力模型中这被归因于幂律数据谱：模型无法选择性关注相关token，只能按全局谱强度学习。但大语言模型具有强非线性，现有理论难以解释其深度缩放机制。

Method: 作者在多种测试的数据谱上考察非线性注意力的损失随深度的变化规律，分析非线性使注意力选择性聚焦相关token、强弱谱方向并行学习的效应，并借助与中心极限定理的类比，将跨层共享误差与层间差异的聚合联系起来。

Result: 在所有测试的数据谱上，非线性注意力均产生损失随深度反比衰减；非线性使注意力可选择性聚焦相关token，强弱谱方向并行学习；跨层共享误差决定损失平台，层间差异的聚合带来随深度持续的收益。

Conclusion: 深度缩放可能源于注意力的非线性，使大语言模型能够局部聚焦，从而可能降低全局协方差结构的相关性。

Abstract: Scaling laws describe power-law improvements in model performance with dataset size and parameter count, yet their underlying mechanisms are not fully understood. To explain the parameter count scaling, existing theory posits power-law scaling with model depth. In linear-attention models, this scaling is tied to a power-law data spectrum: unable to selectively attend to relevant tokens, these models learn according to global spectral strength, with stronger directions learned before weaker ones. Large language models, however, can be strongly nonlinear. Here, we show that nonlinear attention yields inverse-depth decay of loss across all tested data spectra. Nonlinearity enables attention to focus selectively on relevant tokens, allowing strong and weak spectral directions to be learned in parallel. Similar focusing across layers motivates a connection to the central limit theorem: shared error across layers sets the loss plateau, while aggregation turns layer-specific differences into continued gains with depth. Our findings suggest that depth scaling may arise from nonlinearity in attention, which allows large language models to focus locally and may make the global covariance structure less relevant.

</details>


### [89] [Measuring and Mitigating Solution Mode Collapse in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11064)
*Liv G. d'Aliberti,Marwa Abdulhai,Sofiia Druchyna,Peter Henderson,Manoel Horta Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ModeBench 基准与 Re:Max 方法，揭示 RLVR 后训练会压缩正确解法的多样性，并通过按“模式”重放来同时提升成功率与解法多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR（可验证奖励强化学习）对任一正确解给予相同奖励，不区分解法模式，导致模型可能将概率集中到少数熟悉解法上；而保留多种正确解法对用户选择与整体性能具有潜在价值，这种多样性损失此前缺乏量化手段。

Method: 提出 ModeBench：包含多解法任务，其验证器同时返回正确性与所发现的模式（mode）。用其度量 RLVR 后训练中解法多样性的变化。针对集中化问题提出 Re:Max：将每个已发现模式中的一个已验证样例存入回放缓冲区，并对这些存储模式均匀训练，使“只出现过一次”的解法与“反复出现”的解法获得同等练习频率。在三种模型规模、两种 RL 目标及更难任务构造上验证。

Result: RLVR 后训练在准确率持平或提升的同时，将概率集中到更少的正确模式上；前沿模型本身已高度集中。Re:Max 在三种模型规模、两种 RL 目标和更难任务构造上，均提升了策略成功频率以及成功方式的多样性。

Conclusion: RLVR 存在解法多样性退化问题，且该问题在前沿模型中已显著存在；通过基于模式的均匀重放（Re:Max）可在不牺牲成功率的前提下恢复并提升解法多样性，为兼顾准确率与多样性的后训练提供了可行方案。

Abstract: A language model (LM) can usually answer the same question in more than one way, but reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is indifferent to which correct answer a model produces. A solution will earn the same reward whether it is the thousandth copy of a familiar answer or one the model has never produced before. Yet, there is potential value in having the model retain multiple correct solutions as it is trained. For instance, multiple modes may give users a choice and provide problem-solving strategies that improve overall model performance. Here, we introduce ModeBench, a benchmark of multi-solution tasks in which the verifier returns both correctness and mode discovered. We then use ModeBench to measure how solution diversity changes under RLVR post-training. We find that RLVR post-training concentrates probability onto fewer correct modes even as accuracy holds or improves, and moreover, that frontier models are already highly concentrated. We then introduce our solution, Re:Max, which stores one verified example per discovered mode in a replay buffer and trains on those stored modes uniformly. A solution found once is, therefore, practiced as often as one found repeatedly. Across three model scales, two RL objectives, and harder task constructions, replay improves both how often a policy succeeds and how many different ways it can succeed.

</details>


### [90] [CityDeploy-Bench: Benchmarking Physics-Grounded Spatial Set Planning for Multi-Transmitter Network Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11065)
*Chenyang Yuan,Xiaoyuan Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CityDeploy-Bench 基准，将城市级多发射机部署重构为物理约束下的空间集合规划问题，用统一射线追踪验证器分离效用表示与规划动态，揭示随物理耦合增强，部署质量依赖能否学到集体交互关系，而更强搜索无法弥补缺失的关系结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级无线部署自动化在复杂城市传播与全网干扰下仍具挑战，已有方法难以在统一、可控条件下比较不同效用表示与规划算法的贡献。

Method: 提出 CityDeploy-Bench，将多发射机部署建模为物理驱动的空间集合规划；在统一射线追踪验证器下，将效用表示与规划动态解耦，并对直接标量奖励、关系模型和高阶交互结构在不同规划器间进行受控对比。

Result: 实验显示，随物理耦合增强，规划行为出现明显转变：部署质量越来越依赖学习到的效用是否刻画发射机之间的集体交互；仅增强搜索能力无法补偿关系结构的缺失。

Conclusion: 多发射机部署应被视为物理交互集合上的协调问题，而非独立空间决策的集合；作者发布 CityDeploy-Data 与基准框架，为连接决策学习与物理驱动无线网络设计提供可复现测试平台。

Abstract: Automating city-scale wireless deployment remains challenging under complex urban propagation and network-wide interference. We introduce \textbf{CityDeploy-Bench}, a benchmark that reframes multi-transmitter deployment as \emph{physics-grounded spatial set planning} under a unified ray-tracing verifier. The benchmark separates utility representation from planning dynamics, enabling controlled comparison between direct scalar rewards, relational models, and higher-order interaction structures across diverse planners. Our experiments reveal a clear transition in planning behavior as physical coupling grows. Deployment quality becomes increasingly dependent on whether the learned utility captures collective transmitter interactions, whereas stronger search alone cannot compensate for missing relational structure. This establishes multi-transmitter deployment as a coordination problem over physically interacting sets rather than a collection of independent spatial decisions. We release CityDeploy-Data and the benchmark framework as a reproducible testbed for research linking decision learning with physically grounded wireless network design.

</details>


### [91] [Optimally Pacing Budget Spending and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11074)
*Mark Braverman,Jingyi Liu,Jieming Mao,Jon Schneider,Eric Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在对抗性设定下，针对任意预算配速专家类，给出了近最优遗憾界：全信息算法达到 O(D√log F + √(T log F))，匹配已有下界；并将技术扩展到在线资源配置，分数分配下达到 O(D√log F)，首次实现 o(√T) 保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预算约束在线学习在广告投放等场景中需要与预算配速专家竞争，但针对任意专家类的最优遗憾界尚不明确，且在在线资源配置任务中能否突破 √T 障碍仍待解决。

Method: 提出全信息在线学习算法，在给定预算配速计划下，对累积支出与该计划距离不超过 D 的专家取得遗憾界；并将该技术推广到在线资源配置问题，在允许分数分配的设定下给出相应算法。

Result: 全信息设定下遗憾为 O(D√log F + √(T log F))，与 Braverman 等（2025）的下界匹配；在线资源配置分数分配设定下遗憾为 O(D√log F)，为该类任务首个 o(√T) 算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为预算约束在线学习提供了近最优遗憾界，并展示了方法在在线资源配置中的有效性，首次在相关任务中超越 √T 量级保证。

Abstract: We establish near-optimal regret bounds for budget-constrained online learning against arbitrary classes of budget-pacing experts in the adversarial setting. In particular, given any class of $F$ experts and a candidate budget pacing schedule, we provide a full-information algorithm which obtains regret $O(D \sqrt{\log F}+ \sqrt{T\log F})$ against all experts whose cumulative spending stays within distance $D$ of this schedule, matching lower bounds established by Braverman et al. (2025).
  We additionally show that our technique extends to various problems in online resource allocation, where the learner gets to see the rewards and costs of the current options available to them, and establish $O(D\sqrt{\log F})$ regret bounds when fractional allocation is allowed. This is the first algorithm we are aware of which can achieve $o(\sqrt{T})$ guarantees for such tasks.

</details>


### [92] [Stability-Plasticity Balance via Singular-Vector Selection in LLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11076)
*Lingxiang Wang,Hainan Zhang,Liang Pang,Hongwei Zheng,Zhiming Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以奇异向量通道为可塑性与稳定性权衡的基本单元，并据此设计参数高效持续学习方法 SVC，在多个模型与任务上兼顾预训练能力保持与下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域持续适配大模型易导致灾难性遗忘，需在获取新能力与保留预训练能力之间权衡；现有 PEFT 虽减少可训练参数，但缺乏决定"何处可塑、何处需稳定"的原则性单元。

Method: 将奇异向量通道视为输入-输出变换单元，可更新以获取新知识或固定以保留预训练能力；微调前用领域数据估计各通道的适配收益，并以固定的公共通用语料作为历史激活代理估计遗忘代价；再通过膝点代价筛选、帕累托前沿过滤与 Otsu 阈值自适应选择可训练通道。

Result: 在四个模型系列与八个下游任务上，SVC 相较现有 PEFT 基线更好地保持预训练能力，同时取得较强的下游性能；通道评分与选择的分析验证了在奇异向量通道层面进行选择性可塑的有效性。

Conclusion: 在奇异向量通道层面进行选择性可塑，可有效实现持续大模型适配，为可塑性与稳定性的分配提供原则化单元。

Abstract: Domain-specific continual adaptation of LLMs risks catastrophic forgetting, creating a fundamental tension between acquiring new capabilities and preserving those learned during pretraining. PEFT mitigates this problem by restricting the number of trainable parameters, but existing methods lack a principled unit for deciding where plasticity should be allocated and stability should be preserved. We identify the singular-vector channel as a natural unit for managing this trade-off. Each channel represents an input-output transformation, which can be updated to acquire new knowledge or fixed to preserve pretrained capabilities. Based on this perspective, we introduce SVC, a parameter-efficient continual-learning method that selectively updates Singular-Vector Channels. Before fine-tuning, SVC uses domain-specific data to estimate each channel's adaptation benefit and a fixed public general-domain corpus only as a history activation proxy for estimating forgetting cost. It then adaptively selects trainable channels based on these scores via knee-based cost screening, Pareto-front filtering, and Otsu thresholding. Experimental results across four LLM families and eight downstream tasks show that SVC better preserves pretrained capabilities while achieving strong downstream performance relative to existing PEFT baselines. Further analysis of channel scoring and selection demonstrates that selective plasticity at the singular-vector-channel level enables effective continual LLM adaptation.

</details>


### [93] [DaCe-DT: Data-Centric Offline Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning via Adaptive Prompts and Trajectory Correction for Heterogeneous Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11085)
*Xinfei Wang,Shanchen Pang,Chenhao Zhang,Shudong Wang,Wenhao Ji,Haiyuan Gui,Meng Han,Xiaojian Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从数据视角分析离线多任务强化学习的三大瓶颈，提出 DaCe-DT 框架，通过长度门控提示掩码、检索增强提示构建与值自适应回报校准，在 Meta-World 上平均提升 11.73%（最优数据）和 13.34%（次优数据）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线多任务强化学习（Offline MTRL）研究主要集中于算法层面优化，对数据层面的改进关注不足，因而难以提升学习能力与泛化性能。作者认为数据质量与分布是性能的关键制约因素。

Method: 提出 DaCe-DT 框架，由三个机制组成：(1) 长度门控提示掩码（LGPM），(2) 检索增强提示构建（RAPC），(3) 值自适应回报校准（VARC）。三者共同实现以数据为中心的提示自适应与轨迹精炼。

Result: 在 Meta-World 基准上，DaCe-DT 持续优于当前最优方法：最优数据集平均提升 11.73%，次优数据集提升 13.34%，表明其能从不完美数据中稳定学习并提升整体多任务性能。

Conclusion: DaCe-DT 通过对提示长度、提示语义相关性与轨迹连续性的数据级处理，缓解异质任务复杂度与数据质量问题，实现了稳健的多任务泛化与稳定的策略学习。

Abstract: Offline multi-task reinforcement learning (Offline MTRL) heavily depends on the quality and distribution of pre-collected data. However, existing methods mainly focus on algorithmic optimization, with less emphasis on data-level improvements to enhance learning ability and generalization performance. This paper, from a data perspective, reveals three key bottlenecks that limit Offline MTRL performance:(i) ineffective utilization of prompts length under diverse task complexities, and (ii) semantic irrelevance of randomly sampled prompt segments, (iii) misleading supervision induced by fragmented and discontinuous trajectories. To address these challenges, we propose DaCe-DT, a robust offline MTRL framework designed to be insensitive to heterogeneous task complexities and data quality, featuring length-gated prompt masking (LGPM), retrieval-augmented prompt construction (RAPC), and value-adaptive return calibration (VARC). Together, these mechanisms enable DaCe-DT to deliver data-centric prompt adaptation and trajectory refinement, resulting in robust multi-task generalization and stable policy learning amid heterogeneous offline data and tasks. Experimental results on Meta-World show that DaCe-DT consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving an average improvement of 11.73% on optimal datasets and an improvement of 13.34% on suboptimal datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness in learning stably from imperfect data and improving overall multi-task performance.

</details>


### [94] [Cova-PINN: Cross-Domain Conservation Physics-Informed Neural Network for Fluid-Solid Conjugate Heat Transfer in Complex Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11108)
*Weizheng Zhang,Xunjie Xie,Hao Pan,Lin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Cova-PINN：在多域PINN中将守恒约束对齐到热交互路径，联合优化局部跨域复合控制体积平衡与全局配对壁面闭合，显著降低TPMS换热器出口温度与器件级闭合误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域PINN可灵活建模介质特定表征以求解流体-固体共轭传热（CHT），但标准多域PINN在各自独立采样的域支撑上施加控制方程与界面条件，导致温度场看似合理，端到端能量传递与出口温度却不准确。

Method: 提出Cova-PINN多域PINN框架，将守恒支撑与复杂几何中的热交互路径对齐：局部尺度上联合优化跨域复合控制体积平衡，全局换热器尺度上优化配对壁面闭合（paired-wall closure）。在四个三周期极小曲面（TPMS）换热器及几何不同的DualMS设计上，与CHT专用、优化导向及复杂几何PINN基线在统一协议下对比。

Result: 相对最接近的基线MUSA-PINN-CHT，四个TPMS拓扑上平均出口温度误差降低37.7%，器件级闭合误差降低60.2%，同时提升全场温度与热负荷精度，并在DualMS上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 将守恒约束与热交互路径对齐可有效修正多域PINN在共轭传热中的能量传递偏差，在多种复杂拓扑上一致提升端到端预测精度。

Abstract: Multi-domain physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) flexibly model medium-specific representations to solve fluid--solid conjugate heat transfer (CHT). However, standard multi-domain PINNs enforce governing equations and interface conditions on separately sampled domain supports, which can yield plausible temperature fields but inaccurate end-to-end energy transfer and outlet temperatures. We propose Cova-PINN, a multi-domain PINN framework that aligns conservation support with thermal interaction paths in complex geometries. Cova-PINN jointly optimizes cross-domain composite control-volume balances at the local scale and paired-wall closure at the global exchanger scale. We evaluate Cova-PINN on four triply periodic minimal surface (TPMS) heat exchangers and a geometrically distinct DualMS design against CHT-specific, optimization-oriented, and complex-geometry PINN baselines under a common protocol. Relative to the closest baseline, MUSA-PINN-CHT, Cova-PINN reduces average outlet-temperature and device-level closure errors across the four TPMS topologies by $37.7\%$ and $60.2\%$, respectively, while also improving full-field and heat-duty accuracy, with consistent gains on DualMS.

</details>


### [95] [Dynamics as Code: On Model Compression via Dynamic System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11115)
*Fan Gao,Wei Su,Juntong Fan,Renfeng Peng,Hongyu Liu,Jinqiao Duan,Feng-Lei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于动态系统（DS）的模型压缩，证明无理缠绕轨迹在Diophantine条件下构成ε-网，并提出统一四类DS家族的通用压缩框架，在ResNet-18和Qwen系列模型上验证了无需后训练的高压缩比与可控误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练神经网络规模不断增大，在严格的内存与算力约束下部署时，模型压缩成为必要前提。已有动态系统范式将压缩重构为紧凑权重表示，即将高维参数用动态系统轨迹的索引编码，解压时再恢复向量；该机制与剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏和低秩分解本质不同，但此前工作缺乏理论保证和通用框架。

Method: 在无理缠绕这一具体动态系统下，作者证明在Diophantine条件下，包含M=O(ε^{-(d+ν)})个状态的有限轨迹构成d维权重空间上的ε-网，从而将状态分辨率、解压误差与压缩比建立起可预测的联系。进一步提出广义DS压缩框架，统一四类动态系统家族：空间填充曲线（Hilbert、Peano、Morton/Z-order、Snake）、混沌系统（Lorenz）、同余与伪随机生成器（LCG、PCG）以及低差异序列（Halton）。为扩展到大规模模型，引入KD-tree和坐标模板加速，并通过离群点识别控制误差。

Result: 在ResNet-18以及Qwen2.5-1.5B和Qwen1.5-7B上的实验表明，基于DS的压缩能够在无需后处理再训练的情况下达到有竞争力的压缩比，解压误差可控，且状态空间设计灵活。

Conclusion: 该工作为动态系统压缩提供了理论支撑（ε-网保证）和通用化框架，证明其是一种有原则且实用的模型压缩方法，兼具可控误差、灵活状态空间设计与免再训练的优势。

Abstract: The escalating size of pretrained neural networks has rendered model compression a prerequisite for deployment under stringent memory and compute constraints. With the irrational winding as an example, earlier work introduced a dynamic system (DS) paradigm that reconceptualizes compression as compact weight representation: high-dimensional parameters are encoded by the index of a trajectory produced by a dynamic system, from which the vector is recovered during decompression. This mechanism is fundamentally distinct from pruning, quantization, knowledge distillation, and low-rank decomposition. Along this direction, we prove that under a Diophantine condition, a finite trajectory of \(M = O(ε^{-(d+ν)})\) states in the irrational winding constitutes an \(ε\)-net over the \(d\)-dimensional weight space, thereby linking state resolution, decompression error, and compression ratio in a predictable manner. Furthermore, we propose a generalized DS-based model compression framework by unifying four DS families---space-filling curves (Hilbert, Peano, Morton/Z-order, Snake), chaotic systems (Lorenz), congruential and pseudo-random generators (LCG, PCG), and low-discrepancy sequences (Halton). Also, we introduce the KD-tree and coordinate-template acceleration to scale to large models as well as outlier identification to control the error. Experiments on ResNet-18 and Qwen2.5-1.5B/Qwen1.5-7B validate that DS-based compression achieves competitive compression ratios without post-hoc retraining, with controllable decompression error and flexible state-space design, establishing it as a principled and practical compression approach.

</details>


### [96] [Machine Learning Optimization for Enhanced OS Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11133)
*Jae Sung Kim,Spencer Ekeroth,Jeremy Neale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究被动操作系统指纹识别在CIC-IDS2017数据集上的有效性，并提出命令行工具OsirisML（基于nPrint预处理与XGBoost建模），在采样Friday子集上达97.66%准确率，全量Friday捕获达84.69%，Monday捕获达73.83%准确率、79.38% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作系统指纹识别可通过分析TCP/IP流量被动识别网络中的操作系统，但需要验证其在大规模真实流量数据集上的有效性，并提供可复用的自动化工具。

Method: 基于CIC-IDS2017（超47GB pcap文件及对应操作系统标签）；提出命令行工具OsirisML，使用nPrint将流量预处理为表格数据，采用XGBoost训练、重训练与测试机器学习模型；评估随机划分训练/测试集的被动识别效果。

Result: 随机划分下：Friday捕获降采样子集准确率97.66%；Friday全量捕获84.69%；无攻击的Monday全量捕获准确率73.83%、F1为79.38%。

Conclusion: 被动OS指纹识别在该数据集上可行，工具链（nPrint+XGBoost）具备实用价值；但全量、无攻击流量场景下性能下降，说明类分布与流量混杂度显著影响识别效果。

Abstract: Operating System (OS) Fingerprinting is a technique that can be used to identify a network's operating systems by evaluating network traffic in the form of TCP/IP packets. This research will explore the effectiveness of passively identifying operating systems on the CIC-IDS2017 dataset, a collection of over 47 gigabytes of pcap files with their corresponding operating systems. This research also proposes a new command line interface, OsirisML, which uses nPrint to preprocess the data into tabular data and XGBoost to apply ML to the data to generate, retrain, and test ML models. When packets are split randomly between training and testing, OsirisML models reach an accuracy of 97.66% on a down-sampled subset of the Friday capture and 84.69% on the entire capture. On the entire Monday capture, which contains no attacks, OsirisML reaches an accuracy of 73.83% and an F-1 score of 79.38%.

</details>


### [97] [QUILT: Rethinking Sparse-Attention Prefill through Shared Query Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11134)
*Zhenduo Zhao,Qihui Zhou,Mingcong Song,Zhiyi Chen,Chuangguan Ye,Fengfan Hou,Zequn Gong,Jing Li,Hongjie Si,Guoping Long*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QUILT：面向稀疏注意力工作负载的跨查询KV复用执行机制，通过SCSD集合分解、级联共享与tile感知执行，降低长上下文注意力延迟与KV数据量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力kernel以查询为单位独立处理，导致相邻查询共享的KV条目被重复加载与反量化，产生冗余内存流量与计算，而相邻查询所选KV存在大量重叠，跨查询复用机会未被利用。

Method: 提出QUILT：联合处理相邻查询并复用共享KV条目；设计Shift-and-Compare Set Decomposition（SCSD），将不规则集合运算转化为规整的数据并行原语以适配现代加速器，并与注意力计算流水线化以隐藏开销；采用级联共享在多粒度层次上捕获复用；引入tile感知执行策略，在共享粒度与硬件tile利用率间取得平衡，并选择性移除低重要性的查询特有尾部以消除低利用率tile。

Result: 在LongBench上基于GLM-5.3与DeepSeek-3.2，在张量并行与序列并行下评测：相比SOTA稀疏注意力kernel，平均kernel延迟降低最多55.1%，处理的KV数据减少最多55.9%，首token时延（TTFT）降低最多36.8%，精度退化可忽略。

Conclusion: 利用相邻查询间的KV复用可有效缓解稀疏注意力的冗余访存瓶颈；QUILT通过集合分解、级联共享与tile感知调度，在保持精度的同时显著提升长上下文注意力执行效率。

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces the cost of long-context attention, but existing kernels typically process queries independently, repeatedly loading and dequantizing KV entries shared across queries. We observe substantial overlap in the KV entries selected by neighboring queries, creating opportunities for cross-query reuse. We present QUILT, a workload-aware sparse-attention execution mechanism that jointly processes neighboring queries and reuses shared KV entries to reduce redundant memory traffic and computation. QUILT introduces Shift-and-Compare Set Decomposition (SCSD), which transforms irregular set operations into regular data-parallel primitives suitable for modern accelerators, and pipelines SCSD with attention computation to hide its overhead. Cascaded sharing captures reuse hierarchically at multiple granularities. A tile-aware execution strategy balances sharing granularity with hardware tile utilization and selectively removes low-importance query-specific tails to eliminate underutilized tiles. We evaluate QUILT on LongBench using GLM-5.3 and DeepSeek-3.2 under both tensor and sequence parallelism. Compared with the state-of-the-art sparse-attention kernel, QUILT reduces average kernel latency by up to 55.1% and processed KV data by up to 55.9%, while reducing time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency by up to 36.8% with negligible accuracy degradation.

</details>


### [98] [ActiveMedAgent: Cost-Aware Trajectory Learning for Multimodal Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11140)
*Weiwei Ma,Xiaobing Yu,Peijie Qiu,Jin Yang,Zhaoqi An,Xuanzhao Dong,Xiaoqi Zhao,Xiaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 ActiveMedAgent 框架，将临床诊断中的"由廉至贵、按需取证"的序列化决策逻辑引入多模态医学AI：基于冻结的API式视觉语言模型，追踪候选诊断概率分布，按"每步诊断效用减成本"为每种证据获取打分，再用轻量MLP控制器在离线评分轨迹上学习"何时继续取证、何时收敛下诊断"。在三个常用基准上，基于轨迹的策略学习稳定优于无引导式获取与全模态基线；并发现"信息过载"效应：175个案例中，智能体用更少通道即给出正确诊断，而全模态基线失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床诊断本质上是序列化过程：医生只在新增证据有望消解诊断不确定性时，才从廉价检查升级到昂贵检查。现有多模态医学AI多采用一次性、全模态输入的静态范式，未建模"获取成本"与"证据增益"的权衡，也缺乏"何时停止检查、何时下结论"的决策机制，导致成本浪费与信息过载。作者希望把这种成本感知的序列决策逻辑引入多模态医学AI。

Method: 方法上，作者在冻结的、仅通过API访问的视觉语言模型之上构建 ActiveMedAgent：1）维护候选诊断上的概率分布，作为诊断不确定性的显式表征；2）将每次证据（模态/通道）的获取按"该步诊断效用增量减去成本"进行评分，形成带评分的决策轨迹；3）离线训练一个轻量MLP控制器，在这些已评分的轨迹上学习策略，输出"继续请求额外证据"或"提交诊断"的动作。该设计无需微调底层VLM，仅学习外层的成本感知获取与停止策略。

Result: 在三个常用基准上，基于轨迹的策略学习在诊断表现上一致优于"无引导式获取"（unguided acquisition）与"全模态输入"（full-modality）两类基线。此外，作者观察到明显的信息过载效应：在175个案例中，ActiveMedAgent 仅使用更少的模态通道即得出正确诊断，而全模态基线反而失败，说明"学会省略哪些信息"与"学会获取哪些信息"同样重要。

Conclusion: 结论是：成本感知的序列化证据获取可有效迁移到多模态医学AI，通过在冻结VLM之上学习"获取—停止"策略，可在降低模态/成本开销的同时提升诊断准确率；"信息过载"现象表明，选择性省略冗余信息是提升诊断性能的关键，即"学什么该弃"与"学什么该取"同等重要。该工作为成本敏感、可解释的交互式医学诊断智能体提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Clinical diagnosis is inherently sequential: clinicians escalate from cheap to costly tests only when additional evidence is expected to resolve diagnostic uncertainty. We present ActiveMedAgent, a framework that brings this cost-aware sequential logic to multimodal medical AI. Given a frozen, API-accessed vision-language model, ActiveMedAgent tracks probability distributions over candidate diagnoses and scores each acquisition by its per-step diagnostic utility minus cost. A lightweight MLP controller is then trained offline on these scored trajectories, learning when to request additional evidence and when to commit. Across three commonly used benchmarks, trajectory-based policy learning consistently outperforms both unguided acquisition and full-modality baselines. Notably, we identify an information overload effect. In 175 cases, the agent produces a correct diagnosis with fewer channels while the full-modality baseline fails, showing that learning what to omit can be as important as learning what to acquire.

</details>


### [99] [Ranking Prior Alignment for Credit Risk Modeling: When Do External Priors Matter?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11146)
*Qiye Lu,Jiang Ji,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Ranking Prior Alignment，将外部排序先验通过温度缩放 KL 散度蒸馏进任意评分模型，用于冷启动信用评分；在商户数据集与 Amex 上均取得正向提升，且增益随数据、容量、特征质量下降而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷启动信用评分场景（新信贷产品上线）面临标注违约数据稀缺、特征管线不成熟、模型需以极小容量部署以防过拟合等问题。常规正则化手段仍依赖同一受限数据，因此需要一种基于领域知识的外部正则化来源。

Method: 提出 Ranking Prior Alignment：模型无关框架，把来自领域专家、教师模型或 LLM 的外部排序先验，通过温度缩放的 KL 散度损失蒸馏到任意评分模型中。统一神经网络（MIL 注意力）与树模型（XGBoost 自定义目标）两种实现，损失形式为 L = L_task + gamma(t) * KL(P_agent || P_model)，其中 gamma(t) 按指数衰减调度。推理阶段无需外部模型，树模型实现可容忍至 eta = 0.5 的标注噪声。

Result: 在超 150 万商户的工业数据集上，MIL 对齐在 3K bags 时 7/7 个评估单元为正（1 个 ID + 3 个 OOT + 3 个退化指标；OOT-1 上峰值 Delta AUC = +0.020），XGBoost 消融在 300 bags 时 9/9 指标为正。公开 Amex 跨数据集验证 5/5 折为正（平均 Delta AUC = +0.041）。四种模型族（MIL、XGBoost、LightGBM、逻辑回归）与四种教师架构表明该框架兼具模型无关性与先验来源无关性。

Conclusion: 对齐增益呈逆缩放规律：当数据量 N、模型容量 C 与特征质量 Q 降低时收益增大，可帮助从业者决策何时投入先验标注。框架不依赖推理期外部模型，具有实用落地价值。

Abstract: Cold-start credit scoring -- deploying models with scarce labeled data, weak features, or minimal capacity -- is a recurring problem in financial machine learning. When a new lending product launches, labeled default data is scarce, feature pipelines are immature, and models must be deployed with minimal capacity to avoid overfitting. Standard defenses operate on the same limited data; what is needed is a source of external regularization grounded in domain knowledge.
  We propose Ranking Prior Alignment, a model-agnostic framework that distills external ranking priors (from domain experts, teacher models, or LLMs) into any scoring model via a temperature-scaled KL divergence loss. The framework unifies neural (MIL attention) and tree-based (XGBoost custom objective) architectures through a single formulation: L = L_task + gamma(t) * KL(P_agent || P_model), where gamma(t) follows an exponential decay schedule. The method requires no external model at inference, and its tree-based instantiation tolerates annotation noise up to eta = 0.5.
  On an industrial dataset of over 1.5M merchants, MIL alignment achieves 7/7 positive evaluation cells at 3K bags (1 ID + 3 OOT + 3 degradation metrics; peak Delta AUC = +0.020 on OOT-1), and XGBoost ablation achieves 9/9 positive metrics at 300 bags. Cross-dataset validation on public Amex shows 5/5 positive folds (avg Delta AUC = +0.041). Four model families (MIL, XGBoost, LightGBM, Logistic Regression) and four teacher architectures show that the framework is both model-agnostic and prior-source-independent.
  We further observe that alignment gains exhibit an inverse-scaling pattern: benefits grow as data abundance N, model capacity C, and feature quality Q decrease, helping practitioners decide when to invest in prior annotation.

</details>


### [100] [Do LLMs Learn from Rewards in Context? : Rethinking the role of reward in In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11152)
*Minchan Kwon,Seunghee Koh,Sunghyun Baek,Minsung Bae,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过受控实验研究"直接上下文强化学习"（direct ICRL），发现奖励信号对模型提升作用微弱，轨迹本身而非其语义内容驱动改进，表明直接ICRL更应被理解为ICL的特例而非推理时RL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体越来越多通过在上下文中积累经验（而非更新参数）来提升推理时表现，这一过程常被描述为上下文强化学习（ICRL）。但ICL是否真能扮演RL的角色尚未被检验。作者希望在最简单形式——直接ICRL（模型直接以原始轨迹-奖励对为条件）中探究奖励是否真的作为学习信号发挥作用。

Method: 在四个基准、六个模型上开展受控实验。通过翻转、随机化或移除奖励，观察改进曲线是否变化；并在明确指示模型探索、利用或对奖励进行推理的元提示下重复实验。此外，测试打乱或损坏的轨迹相对于真实轨迹的效果。

Result: 发现奖励确实被读取，但其效应很小：翻转、随机化或移除奖励几乎不改变改进曲线，即使在明确指示探索/利用/推理奖励的元提示下也如此。轨迹驱动了改进，但并非通过其语义内容——打乱或损坏的轨迹与真实轨迹效果相当。

Conclusion: 这些模式与ICL中已知现象高度相似，表明直接ICRL应被更好地理解为ICL的一个特例，而非推理时RL。这一重构对智能体记忆设计有启示：输入分布和示范等ICL因素可能比奖励塑造和探索等RL元素更重要。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly improve at inference time by accumulating experience in context rather than by updating parameters. This process is often described as in-context reinforcement learning (ICRL). Whether in-context learning (ICL) can actually play the role of RL, however, has not been tested. We study this question in its simplest form, direct ICRL, where the model conditions directly on raw trajectory-reward pairs, and ask whether the reward acts as a learning signal. Through controlled experiments on four benchmarks across six models, we find that the reward is read, but its effect is small: flipping, randomizing, or removing the reward leaves the improvement curve almost unchanged, and this holds even under meta-prompts that explicitly instruct the model to explore, exploit, or reason over rewards. Trajectories drive improvement, but not through their semantic content: shuffled or corrupted trajectories work as well as real ones. These patterns closely mirror those known in ICL, suggesting that direct ICRL is better understood as a special case of ICL than as inference-time RL. This reframing has implications for agent memory design: ICL factors such as input distribution and demonstrations may matter more than RL elements such as reward shaping and exploration.

</details>
