### [101] [RideBench: A Large-Scale Exogenous-Aware Benchmark for Ride-Hailing Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11164)
*Shengsheng Lin,Jing Hu,Zhengyang Hu,Jiazheng Sun,Zichun Cao,Siwei Sun,Zhichao Zou,Enyun Yu,Dongdong Li,Xinyi Hu,Weiwei Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文发布了大规模网约车时间序列数据集Ride-Hailing，并基于此构建了面向外生变量感知预测的基准RideBench，评测30余种方法，揭示现有模型与真实网约车预测需求之间的明显不匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实网约车预测面临外生扰动（天气、节假日、大型活动）与长时程规划需求，但缺乏覆盖多空间区域、多年跨度且包含外生情景的大规模公开数据集与统一评测基准，导致现有模型能力与实际问题需求之间存在差距。

Method: 构建Ride-Hailing数据集：来自滴滴市场数据，覆盖200个空间区域、连续四年、半小时粒度，包含天气扰动、节假日效应、大型活动影响三类外生情景。在其上提出RideBench基准，涵盖常规周级预测与最长8周（最多2,688步）长时程预测，评测30余种方法，包括仅内生模型、外生感知模型及时间序列基础模型。

Result: 在常规周级预测中，未来已知外生变量带来明显收益，尤其在天气、节假日与大型活动情景下；但现有外生感知模型仍难以充分捕捉复杂外部情境下扰动引起的模式变化。长时程预测中，现有模型无法同时实现低点误差、准确整体趋势与可靠近期预测。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了现有预测模型与真实网约车需求之间的不匹配，呼吁发展能更好利用未来已知外生信息、跨异质区域扩展并支持长时程规划的模型。通过发布Ride-Hailing与RideBench，推动社区研究真实网约车预测中的这些实际挑战。

Abstract: We release Ride-Hailing, a large-scale ride-hailing time series dataset synthesized from DiDi's marketplace data across 200 spatial areas. Ride-Hailing spans four consecutive years at half-hourly granularity and covers three representative exogenous scenarios: Weather Disturbance, Holiday Effect, and Large-scale Event Impact. Built upon Ride-Hailing, we introduce RideBench, a comprehensive benchmark for exogenous-aware ride-hailing forecasting, covering both regular week-ahead forecasting and long-horizon 8-week-ahead forecasting with up to 2,688 prediction steps. RideBench evaluates over 30 representative forecasting methods, including endogenous-only models, exogenous-aware models, and time series foundation models. Our results show that future-known exogenous variables provide clear benefits in regular week-ahead forecasting, especially under weather, holiday, and large-scale event (e.g., major sporting events and concerts) scenarios. However, current exogenous-aware models still struggle to fully capture disturbance-induced pattern changes under complex external contexts. For long-horizon forecasting, existing models cannot simultaneously achieve low pointwise errors, accurate broad trends, and reliable near-term forecasts. These findings reveal a clear mismatch between existing forecasting models and real-world ride-hailing requirements, highlighting the need for models that can better exploit future-known exogenous information, scale across heterogeneous areas, and support long-horizon planning. By introducing Ride-Hailing and RideBench, we aim to encourage the community to study these practical challenges in real-world ride-hailing forecasting.

</details>


### [102] [CARE: A Lightweight Plug-in Gated Correction and Uncertainty-aware Module for Long-term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11165)
*Guo Cheng,Changlong Lv,Jingyi Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出即插即用模块 CARE，在不改动原架构的前提下增强任意确定性多变量长时序预测器，通过历史对齐重采样、残差修正与风险门控同时提升精度并提供可解释的逐步可靠性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量长时序预测在电力负荷调度、交通流管理与金融风控中至关重要，但现有确定性主干只输出单一轨迹，掩盖了不同步长与通道间预测难度的异质性，且不提供局部可靠性信号。

Method: CARE（Corrective branch with Aligned context and Relative-error Estimation）与基础模型并行运行：重采样历史上下文以对齐预测时域；从对齐后的历史中学习残差修正模式；通过逐坐标 sigmoid 风险门控施加尺度感知的有界更新。采用多目标损失联合优化预测精度、残差跟踪、风险对齐与基础模型锚定。

Result: 在 8 个基准数据集与 3 个代表性主干上，CARE 以极小的参数量与时延开销提升精度；风险门控能可靠识别高误差区域，在 Weather 数据集上最高门控三分位组的误差约为最低组近 4 倍，为规划者提供可解释的逐步信任信号。

Conclusion: CARE 是一种轻量、通用、无需重构架构的增强插件，兼顾精度提升与逐坐标可靠性估计，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multivariate long-horizon forecasting is critical to electricity load scheduling and traffic flow management, and to financial risk control. Existing deterministic backbones output a single trajectory, masking heterogeneous prediction difficulty across horizons and channels and providing no localized reliability signal. We present CARE (Corrective branch with Aligned context and Relative-error Estimation), a lightweight plug-in that enhances any deterministic forecaster without architectural redesign. Operating in parallel with the base model, CARE resamples historical context to match the forecast horizon, learns residual correction patterns from this aligned history, and applies scale-aware bounded updates modulated by per-coordinate sigmoid risk gates. A multi-objective loss jointly optimizes forecast accuracy, residual tracking, risk alignment, and base-model anchoring. Across eight benchmarks with three representative backbones, CARE improves accuracy with marginal parameter and latency overhead. Its risk gates reliably identify high-error regions: on Weather, the highest-gate tertile exhibits nearly four times the error of the lowest-gate tertile, offering planners an interpretable per-step trust signal. Code is available at https://github.com/CG-BNYC/CARE.

</details>


### [103] [PIVOT: Perplexity-Informed KD-to-RL Transition Scheduling for Vertical-Domain Few-Shot Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11167)
*Heng Li,Yong Zhang,Ning Cheng,Zhigen Li,Yun Zhu,Yanmeng Wang,Shaojun Wang,Jing Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PIVOT（Perplexity-Informed Transition Optimization），一种基于教师评估的序列困惑度动态路由样本的框架，在 OPD 与 GRPO 之间切换，以解决垂直领域小模型少样本分类问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 垂直领域少样本分类对小语言模型困难，监督信号有限导致难以获取领域特定决策知识。现有 KD-to-RL 流程采用全局固定的转换调度，忽略了不同样本在进入奖励驱动精炼前所需的教师引导获取量差异。

Method: PIVOT 是一种动态转换框架：依据教师评估的序列困惑度在 On-Policy Distillation (OPD) 与 GRPO 之间路由样本。低困惑度样本转入 GRPO 进行奖励驱动精炼，高困惑度样本保留在 OPD 下继续领域知识获取。

Result: 在 Banking77 和 HWU64 数据集上，在相同的预热后学生优化步数条件下，PIVOT 持续优于持续 OPD 与全局同步的 OPD→GRPO 基线，取得更强的下游性能与更稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: 动态、基于困惑度的样本级转换策略优于全局固定调度，能更有效地结合教师引导知识获取与强化学习精炼，提升垂直领域少样本分类表现与训练稳定性。

Abstract: Vertical-domain few-shot classification remains challenging for small language models, as limited supervision makes it difficult to acquire domain-specific decision knowledge. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) can improve teacher-guided adaptation by supervising student-generated rollouts, while GRPO-based reinforcement learning can further refine downstream predictions. However, existing KD-to-RL pipelines typically rely on globally fixed transition schedules, ignoring that different samples may require different amounts of teacher-guided acquisition before reward-driven refinement. We propose PIVOT (Perplexity-Informed Transition Optimization), a dynamic transition framework that routes samples between OPD and GRPO according to teacher-evaluated sequence perplexity. PIVOT moves low-perplexity samples to GRPO for reward-driven refinement while keeping high-perplexity samples under OPD for continued domain knowledge acquisition. Experiments on Banking77 and HWU64 show that PIVOT consistently outperforms continued OPD and globally synchronized OPD$\rightarrow$GRPO baselines under the same number of post-warm-up student optimization steps, achieving stronger downstream performance and more stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [104] [SACQ: Structured Decoding with Memory-Conditioned Refinement for Long-Horizon Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11170)
*Guo Cheng,Zhengzhuo Xu,Chenchen Jing,Jingyi Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SACQ是一种可插拔的结构化预测头，用两阶段解码加批自适应scaled log-cosh损失替代flatten readout，在多种骨干网络上提升LTSF精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LTSF模型多采用patch编码器加flatten readout头，用单一共享投影将全部历史记忆映射到所有未来步，导致未来位置隐式耦合、位置特异的历史-未来对齐被掩盖，并对受损输入和极端监督噪声敏感。

Method: SACQ保持编码器不变，仅替换读出头：第一阶段建立粗粒度patch-grid预测脚手架；第二阶段通过历史记忆上的交叉注意力逐未来位置细化，并用学习的逐patch门控将注意力修正与粗脚手架融合。同时提出批自适应scaled log-cosh损失，自动校准对当前残差尺度的鲁棒性，抑制离群梯度并保留对典型误差的类MSE敏感性。

Result: 在PatchTST、DLinear和patch-Mamba骨干上，SACQ取得顶尖的测试MSE/MAE，仅增加少量参数与延迟。在推理期输入损坏和训练集标签噪声压力测试下，SACQ显著优于flatten readout；消融实验验证了各架构组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化预测头结合自适应鲁棒损失可作为通用即插即用模块，改善长期时间序列预测的精度与抗噪能力，且计算开销可控。

Abstract: Long-term time series forecasting (LTSF) models predominantly employ patch-based encoders terminated by a flatten readout head that maps the entire encoded historical memory to all future steps through a single shared projection. This implicit coupling of future positions obscures position-specific historical-to-future alignment and amplifies sensitivity to corrupted inputs and extreme supervision noise. We present SACQ, a plug-in structured prediction head that replaces flatten readout while keeping the encoder unchanged. SACQ adopts a two-stage decoding pipeline: it first establishes a coarse patch-grid forecast scaffold, then refines each future position through cross-attention over historical memory and merges the attention-derived correction with the coarse scaffold via a learned per-patch gate. To stabilize optimization under long horizons and noisy labels, we further propose a batch-adaptive scaled log-cosh loss that automatically calibrates robustness to the current residual scale, suppressing outlier gradients while preserving MSE-like sensitivity for typical errors. SACQ attains top-tier test MSE/MAE across PatchTST, DLinear, and patch-Mamba backbones with only modest incremental overhead in parameters and latency. Under inference-time input corruption and training-set label-noise stress tests, SACQ substantially outperforms flatten readouts, with ablation studies validating each architectural component.

</details>


### [105] [Predictive Multiplicity in Cell-Fate Assignment: Label-Free Rashomon Sets and the Limits of Per-Cell Certification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11185)
*Arjun Bhupatiraju,Abhiram Bhupatiraju*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FateMultiplicity 提出一个无标签 Rashomon 集框架，用于量化单细胞轨迹推断中的命运多重性，并检验每细胞认证能否提升命运判定可靠性——结论是多重性值得测量，但认证并非通往更可靠命运判定的路径，仅有两种构造存活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞轨迹推断将转录组测量映射到发育连续统，但拟合效果相同的多种模型配置可能给出互相冲突的细胞命运分配。这种"命运多重性"此前缺乏系统化的度量与统计刻画，作者希望衡量并报告多重性，并检验基于模型集合的每细胞认证是否能带来更可靠的命运判定。

Method: 提出 FateMultiplicity，一个无标签框架，在不使用谱系标签的情况下构建统计可容许的模型集（Rashomon 集）：通过在交叉拟合的留出基因上评估模型差异，并以随机种子变异进行校准的非劣性检验来纳入模型。随后检验每细胞认证的命运余量 FM 是否比拟合模型自身提供的判定更可靠，并与基线决策余量、种子离散度基线、q-分位数松紧变体以及上/下确界构造进行比较。

Result: 多重性规模大，且更依赖于模型空间的多样性而非其规模：第二算法的 12 种配置暴露 20.0% 的细胞存在多重性，而第一算法的 24 种仅暴露 3.8%。FM 认证并未优于拟合模型自身：在模拟金标准上，FM 判别错误分配的 AUC 为 0.682，显著低于基线配置自身决策余量的 0.965（p = 0.003）和种子离散度基线的 0.854。信息量由可容许集的广度而非基数决定：基数为四时，种子重拟合集 AUC 0.933，超参数扰动集 0.701。将下确界松弛为 q-分位数可恢复判别力，但收敛于单模型自身置信度；上确界达 0.973，因为 theta* 的隶属关系为其提供下界，而下确界无锚定。两种存活构造：余量侵蚀比可区分真实与虚假分支点（模拟中 AUC 0.890，未在真实数据测试）；对抗克隆观测命运时，未认证细胞与其克隆结果不一致的频率比认证细胞高 16.4 个百分点（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 轨迹推断中的多重性值得测量和报告，但在无标签 Rashomon 集上进行每细胞认证并非获得更可靠命运判定的途径。仅余量侵蚀比和克隆一致性检验两种构造存活。

Abstract: Single-cell trajectory inference maps transcriptomic measurements onto developmental continua, yet configurations that fit the data equally well can assign conflicting cell fates. FateMultiplicity is a label-free framework that constructs a statistically admissible model set, or Rashomon set, without lineage labels, by evaluating model discrepancy on cross-fitted held-out genes under non-inferiority testing calibrated against random-seed variation. Multiplicity is large and depends more on the diversity of the model space than its size: twelve configurations of a second algorithm expose 20.0% of cells where twenty-four of the first expose 3.8%. Whether the per-cell certified fate margin FM yields more reliable assignments than the fitted model already provides is then tested, and it does not. On simulation ground truth, on the same cells, FM discriminates misassignment at AUC 0.682, against 0.965 for the baseline configuration's own decision margin (p = 0.003) and 0.854 for a seed-dispersion baseline. Informativeness is governed by the breadth of the admitted set, not its cardinality: at cardinality four, seed refits give 0.933 and hyperparameter-perturbed sets 0.701. Relaxing the infimum to a q-quantile recovers discrimination but converges toward the single model's own confidence; the supremum reaches 0.973 because theta*'s membership bounds it from below, while the infimum is unanchored. Multiplicity in trajectory inference is worth measuring and reporting, but per-cell certification over a label-free Rashomon set is not a route to more reliable fate calls. Two constructions survive: a margin-erosion ratio separates real from spurious branch points in simulation (AUC 0.890, untested on real data), and against clonally observed fate, uncertified cells disagree with their clone's outcome 16.4 percentage points more often than certified cells (p < 0.001).

</details>


### [106] [Do Flatter Minima Drive Better Generalization? An Algorithmic Separation in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11206)
*Mohnish Harwani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 平坦损失景观并非驱动泛化的充分机制；但SAM与权重衰减协同可加速grokking转变，其机理被一个两层ReLU插值模型的理论分析所解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦损失景观长期被认为与更好的泛化相关，但其作为泛化的因果机制尚不明确。Grokking现象（模型长期用非泛化结构拟合训练数据，仅在特定条件下才转向泛化）为区分"相关性"与"因果性"提供了理想试验台。作者希望探究平坦性是否是驱动这一泛化转变的机制。

Method: 1. 使用锐度感知最小化（SAM）将训练偏向平坦解，检验其能否可靠地诱发grokking转变；2. 将SAM与权重衰减等驱动泛化的机制结合，观察对转变速度的影响；3. 构建一个最小的两层ReLU插值模型（同时包含记忆解与泛化解），从理论上解析SAM与权重衰减的关系；4. 对记忆插值解进行局部稳定性分析。

Result: 仅使用SAM虽能产生更平坦的解，却不足以可靠诱发向泛化的转变；当SAM与权重衰减结合时，SAM可将向泛化解的转变加速最多4倍（以epoch计）。理论分析表明：即便在最小模型中，平坦性本身也无法区分记忆解与泛化解，而权重衰减则偏好泛化解；在局部稳定性分析下存在一个窗口，其中记忆插值解在梯度下降下局部稳定，但在低范数区域下对SAM不稳定。

Conclusion: 平坦性本身不足以作为驱动泛化的因果机制；SAM的加速作用源于其与权重衰减的协同——即SAM破坏了低范数区域中记忆插值解的局部稳定性。研究为平坦性在驱动泛化中的作用（尤其在模型倾向以非泛化结构最小化损失的场景下）提供了更具可解释性的解释。

Abstract: Flat loss landscapes have long been linked to better generalization in neural networks. However, its role as a causal mechanism for generalization is less established. Grokking provides an unique testbed to understand this distinction: models are prone to fit observed data using non-generalizing structure and remain in that regime for prolonged periods, transitioning to generalization only under particular training conditions. In this work, we study whether flat loss landscapes can act as a driving mechanism in this transition. While recent work has argued for flatness as a necessary geometric condition for this transition, we find that biasing training toward flatter solutions using sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) is insufficient to reliably induce this transition, despite producing flatter solutions. However, when SAM is paired with mechanisms that drive generalization such as weight decay, an interesting property emerges: SAM can accelerate the transition to generalizing solutions by up to 4x at the epoch-level. We theoretically untangle this relationship between SAM and weight decay using a minimal interpolating two-layer ReLU model with both memorizing and generalizing solutions. We show that even in this simple setup, flatness alone cannot distinguish a memorizing solution from a generalizing one, while weight decay favors generalizing solutions. However, under a local stability analysis, there exists a window where a memorizing interpolant is locally stable under gradient descent but unstable under SAM in the low-norm regime, which can explain SAM's ability to accelerate this transition. Overall, our results provide a more interpretable account of the role of flatness in driving generalization, especially in settings where models are vulnerable to minimizing loss through learning non-generalizing structure.

</details>


### [107] [Bridging KV-Cache Quantization and Linear Attention: From Theory to Pretrained Weight Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11214)
*Kaicheng Xiao,Liran Dong,Haotian Li,Guoliang Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出 RAM-Net，通过离散地址空间上的软分配统一了 per-KV 压缩与 multi-KV 聚合，实现高效注意力，并支持 Transformer 权重迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV-cache 量化压缩单个 KV 条目但保留全部条目，线性注意力递归聚合多个历史 KV 但会引入干扰。二者各自的局限促使作者探究能否在单一机制中统一 per-KV 压缩与 multi-KV 聚合，以同时降低存储与计算开销。

Method: 将 RAM-Net 视为二者的桥梁：通过离散地址空间上的软分配决定对每个地址对应连续 slot 状态的递归更新。在受限 RAM-Net 构造下，证明软地址分配可将硬量化匹配扩展为可分离的读写重叠，局部近似 full-attention 相似度并支持递归聚合。基于软量化中间构造，提出新的 Transformer-to-RAM-Net 权重迁移路径。

Result: 在 0.3B 至 7B 参数的九个预训练 Transformer 模型上，每个模型仅用 500M token 预算，在六项常识与知识任务中，RAM-Net 平均恢复了教师模型相对随机猜测准确率增益的 87.1%。

Conclusion: RAM-Net 从理论与实证上桥接了 KV-cache 量化与线性注意力，既保留了可解释的离散地址结构，又实现了递归聚合的高效注意力，并通过权重迁移高效复用预训练 Transformer。

Abstract: KV-cache quantization and linear attention are two representative approaches to tackling the storage and computational costs of Transformers. KV-cache quantization compresses individual KV entries into discrete codes but retains all entries, whereas linear attention recurrently aggregates multiple historical KV contributions into a fixed-size continuous state but can introduce interference. This contrast raises the question of whether per-KV compression and multi-KV aggregation can be bridged within a single mechanism for efficient attention. We identify RAM-Net as such a bridge through soft assignments over a discrete address space. These assignments determine recurrent updates to the continuous slot state associated with each address. Under a restricted RAM-Net construction, we prove that soft address assignments extend hard quantized matching to a separable read-write overlap that locally approximates full-attention similarity and supports recurrent aggregation. These connections further enable Transformer-to-RAM-Net weight migration through a new path based on a soft-quantized intermediate construction. Across nine pretrained Transformer models from 0.3B to 7B parameters, RAM-Net recovers an average of 87.1% of the teachers' accuracy gains over random guessing across six commonsense and knowledge tasks using only a 500M-token budget per model.

</details>


### [108] [The Lattice of Transition Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11216)
*T. Y. Tsui,Jiatao Gu,Lingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将扩散模型、自回归模型及其中间态统一刻画为"污染格"（corruption lattice）上的路径，以并行步丢弃的依赖性度量调度代价，证明零代价调度的最少步数由数据几何决定（马尔可夫图数据等于图的树深），并可在解码前预测不同调度的性能排序，在文本、图像、视频生成任务上得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型擅长连续场、自回归模型擅长离散词元，已有混合模型各自固定解码调度，缺乏统一理论来判断调度是否最优、能否在解码前预测其性能。本文旨在建立统一的调度分析框架，回答"在固定步数下，能否在解码前预测不同解码调度的性能"。

Method: 将扩散、自回归及中间模型统一表示为污染格上的路径；定义调度代价为其并行步所丢弃的依赖关系；分析零代价调度的最少步数与数据几何的关系；对图上马尔可夫且沿路径相依的数据，证明最少步数等于图的树深；步数不足时用基于预训练权重估计的成对依赖核，在解码前预测各调度的代价排序。

Result: 零代价调度的最少步数由数据几何决定，对词元和连续场一致；对图上马尔可夫数据最少步数等于树深，即随序列长度对数增长、随网格边长线性增长；步数低于树深时任何调度都有正代价，其排序可由预训练权重估计的成对依赖核预测。在文本、图像、视频生成上，多数关于不同指标与基准下调度排序的预测得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来自回归模型、扩散模型及各类混合模型的解码提供了统一的设计原则：通过污染格、调度代价与树深刻画最少步数，并可在解码前预测调度性能，从而指导解码调度设计。

Abstract: Diffusion and autoregression (AR) have long been seen as different categories of generative models, with diffusion specialising in continuous fields and AR specialising in discrete tokens. Recent work seeks to combine the advantages of the two models, and each hybrid fixes its decoding schedule by design. In this paper, we ask whether the performance of decoding schedules of one model can be predicted before decoding at a fixed number of steps. We describe diffusion, AR, and models in between as paths on one corruption lattice, and define the cost of a schedule as the dependence its parallel steps discard. The cost shows that the fewest steps of a zero-cost schedule are set by the geometry of the data, in the same way for tokens and for continuous fields. In particular, for data that are Markov on a graph and dependent along its paths, the fewest steps equal the graph's treedepth, which is logarithmic in the length of a sequence and linear in the side length of a grid. With fewer steps than the treedepth, every schedule pays a positive cost, whose ranking we predict before decoding with a kernel of pairwise dependence estimated from pretrained weights. Across text generation, image generation, and video generation, we verify most of the predictions about the rankings of different schedules under different metrics and benchmarks. This work therefore provides a design principle for decoding for future AR models, diffusion models, and anything in between. Our code is available at https://github.com/TSUITUENYUE/The-Lattice-of-Transition-Laws.

</details>


### [109] [Multimodal Graph Retrieval-Augmented Sequential Recommendation via Collaborative Filtering Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11228)
*Jason Marcell Setiadi,Xin Cao,Lina Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MGRASRec：多模态图检索增强的序列推荐框架，注入协同信号并将推理压缩为单次前向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的序列推荐方法存在两个问题：一是仅依赖目标用户自身交互历史，忽略了邻居用户的协同信号；二是对长历史反复进行MLLM推理导致计算开销巨大。

Method: 提出MGRASRec框架：从用户-物品交互图中检索结构化路径，并通过多模态相似度扩展以提升覆盖范围；将候选物品条件下的协同过滤信号注入MLLM提示；检索同时免费获得与候选最相关的历史物品，避免循环式摘要，实现每个候选仅一次前向传播；各组件统一为增强提示，用于MLLM的参数高效微调。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的广泛评测验证了有效性，所有指标均取得最优性能，尤其在排序质量上增益显著。

Conclusion: MGRASRec通过多模态图检索增强有效融合协同信号，兼顾效率与效果，为MLLM序列推荐提供可行方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated strong potential for sequential recommendation through their ability to reason over complex multimodal data. However, existing approaches either rely solely on the target user's own interaction history, neglecting collaborative signals from neighboring users, or incur substantial computational overhead through repeated MLLM inference over long interaction histories. To address these challenges, we propose MGRASRec, a multimodal graph retrieval-augmented framework for sequential recommendation. MGRASRec injects collaborative filtering signals conditioned on the candidate item directly into the MLLM prompt by retrieving structured paths from a user-item interaction graph, extended via multimodal similarity to increase coverage beyond exact co-interaction overlap. This retrieval also surfaces the history items most relevant to the candidate at no additional cost, removing the need for recurrent summarization and keeping inference to a single forward pass per candidate. All components are unified into an augmented prompt for parameter-efficient fine-tuning of an MLLM. Extensive evaluations across three publicly available datasets validate the effectiveness of MGRASRec, achieving the best performance on all metrics with particularly strong gains in ranking quality.

</details>


### [110] [SteerCast: Retrieval-Based Latent Steering for Decoder-Only Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11229)
*Van Dai Do,Huu Hiep Nguyen,Minh Hoang Nguyen,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SteerCast——一种基于检索的隐空间引导方法，在推理阶段提升decoder-only预测器，无需更新参数即可提高多变量时间序列预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列预测需从历史观测与辅助特征预测未来值，现有预测器在推理时受限于固定参数，难以利用训练集中相似历史案例的信息来纠偏。

Method: 构建检索库：对训练集每个历史窗口存储其表示及在预测器隐空间计算的"引导向量"（真实续接诱导表示与模型自身预测诱导表示之差）。测试时检索查询历史的最近邻，聚合其引导向量，并在自回归生成每一步注入预测器隐状态，引导预测朝向与相似训练样本一致的轨迹。

Result: 在多种多变量基准和多个预测步长上，SteerCast持续优于微调骨干模型和基于检索的基线，且除原始微调外无需额外训练，仅以训练集作为检索语料。

Conclusion: 推理阶段的检索式隐空间引导可在不更新参数的前提下有效提升预测精度，为时间序列预测提供了一种高效、低成本的增强范式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting aims to predict future values from historical observations and auxiliary features. We propose \textbf{SteerCast}, a retrieval-based latent steering method that improves decoder-only forecaster at inference time, without updating its parameters. SteerCast constructs a database from the training set by storing a representation of each history window together with a \emph{steering vector} computed in the forecaster's latent space, defined as the difference between representations induced by the ground-truth continuation and by the model's own prediction. At test time, SteerCast retrieves nearest neighbors for a query history, aggregates their steering vectors, and injects the resulting signal into the forecaster's hidden states at every step of autoregressive generation, guiding predictions toward trajectories consistent with similar training cases. Experiments across diverse multivariate benchmarks and multiple horizons show that SteerCast consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the fine-tuned backbone and retrieval-based baselines, while requiring no additional training beyond the original fine-tuning and using only the training set as a retrieval corpus.

</details>


### [111] [Low-Cost Sensor Calibration for Indoor Air Quality Monitoring: A Dataset, Evaluation Scenarios, and a Lightweight Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11236)
*Jinyong Yun,Seokho Ahn,Hyungjin Kim,Sungbok Shin,Young-Duk Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个为期六个月、涵盖五个地点的低成本与参考传感器配对的室内空气质量数据集，并定义四种评估场景以检验校准模型的空间泛化与时间鲁棒性，进而提出一种结合输入窗口压缩与残差时序/特征融合的轻量时序模型，在四类场景下均取得良好校准性能且边缘推理成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本传感器可实现规模化室内空气质量监测，但存在非线性失真、噪声和时间漂移，需要校准。传统严格成对校准设置要求每个部署位置都共置参考传感器，无法应对空间与时间异质性，难以规模化。

Method: 构建六个月、五地点的多元室内空气质量测量数据集（含低成本与参考传感器及上下文元数据）；基于该数据集定义四种评估场景：参考高效场景与位置迁移场景评估空间泛化，长期漂移场景与事件条件场景评估对渐变和突变分布偏移的鲁棒性；据此推导设计需求，并提出一种轻量时序模型，将输入窗口压缩与残差时序及特征融合相结合。

Result: 在全部四种评估场景下均表现出较强的校准性能，同时保持较低的边缘推理成本。

Conclusion: 该工作通过新数据集与多场景评测框架揭示了低成本传感器校准中的空间与时间异质性挑战，并验证了轻量时序模型在空间泛化与时间鲁棒性方面的有效性，为可扩展的室内空气质量监测校准提供了方案。

Abstract: Low-cost sensors enable scalable indoor air quality monitoring but require calibration because of nonlinear distortions, noise, and temporal drift. The conventional strict pairwise calibration setting requires a co-located reference sensor at each deployment location and does not account for spatial and temporal heterogeneity. To address these limitations, we introduce a six-month dataset comprising multivariate indoor air-quality measurements from low-cost and reference sensors with contextual metadata collected at five locations. Using this dataset, we define four evaluation scenarios. The reference-efficient and location-transfer scenarios evaluate spatial generalization, whereas the long-term drift and event-conditioned scenarios assess robustness to gradual and abrupt distribution shifts. Based on these scenarios, we derive design requirements and propose a lightweight temporal model that combines input-window compression with residual temporal and feature fusion. Experiments show strong calibration performance across all four scenarios with low edge-inference cost.

</details>


### [112] [Read What Matters: Query-Adaptive Quantization for KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11245)
*Siddharth Bhandari,Lucas Gretta,Krishna Balasubramanian,Shiva Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReadKV针对KV-cache存储时未知未来查询、而每个解码查询需不同精度位置的错配问题，提出以"保留位预算"和"每查询读取位预算"分离的渐进式编码方案，实现查询相关的位分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV-cache条目在存入时其未来查询尚不可知，而每次解码查询所需的精度位置各不相同。现有方法多用查询无关的固定精度读取，无法在相同读取预算下匹配查询相关访问的精度需求，尤其在长上下文解码中，每步搬移的缓存字节（而非权重）成为主要成本。

Method: 将保留位与每查询读取位设为独立预算。ReadKV把每个key和value存入渐进式码，其前缀支持不同重构精度。对每个查询：先用查询分配key通道前缀，由重构key计算注意力，再用该注意力分配value token前缀；存储条目保持不变。各阶段在固定预算下优化经校准的失真目标，并证明在细化增益递减条件下的精确分配，同时将目标与注意力输出误差关联。此外构造有限维注意力族，证明查询相关访问在相同读取预算下严格优于任何查询无关读取器。

Result: 在六个基模型上，从八位缓存中平均读取四位，C4困惑度增幅不超过0.66%，逻辑读取量约为16位缓存的四分之一、保留容量约为其一半；在相同payload读取预算下持续优于存储并完整读取四位。在两个指令微调模型上的长上下文问答与检索提供额外质量证据。在NVIDIA A10G上测试的8K token、batch-one、单层工作负载中，限制为八位读取器、平均二位payload读取预算时，延迟比TurboQuant编解码器低39%。

Conclusion: 通过保留位与读取位的分离预算及渐进式编码，可在保持存储不变的同时实现查询相关的精度分配，在显著降低读取量与容量的条件下维持质量，并降低长上下文解码延迟；理论上查询相关访问严格优于查询无关读取。

Abstract: KV-cache entries are stored before their future queries are known, but each decoding query needs precision in different places. We study this mismatch using separate budgets for retained bits and bits fetched per query. ReadKV stores each key and value in a progressive code whose prefixes support different reconstruction precisions. For each query, it allocates key-channel prefixes using the query, computes attention from the reconstructed keys, and then allocates value-token prefixes using that attention. Stored entries remain unchanged. Each stage optimizes a calibrated distortion objective under a fixed budget; we prove exact allocation under diminishing refinement gains and relate these objectives to attention-output error. We also exhibit a finite-dimensional attention family where query-dependent access strictly outperforms every query-independent reader at the same read budget, even with unrestricted competing encoders and decoders.
  Across six base models, reading four bits on average from an eight-bit cache increases C4 perplexity by at most 0.66%, using about one quarter of the logical reads and half the retained capacity of a 16-bit cache. It is consistently more accurate than storing and fully reading four bits at the same payload-read budget. Retaining more bits than each query fetches is aimed at long-context decoding, where the cache bytes moved per step, rather than the weights, dominate cost. Long-context question answering and retrieval on two instruction-tuned models provide additional quality evidence. On the tested 8K-token, batch-one, single-layer workload on an NVIDIA A10G, a restricted eight-bit ReadKV reader with a two-bit mean payload-read budget has 39% lower latency than the tested TurboQuant codec.

</details>


### [113] [Why On-Policy Distillation Sometimes Fails: Vanishing Learning Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11247)
*Lei Zhao,Qichao Zhao,Bowen Zuo,Qishi Zhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的失败机制：发现大规模教师模型会导致早期损失平台期，而自强化学习（self-RL）教师则能持续下降；作者将其建模为小学习率极限下的连续时间动力系统，并提出表征适应受限可能是学习信号崩塌的原因（尚待验证）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD 能有效实现语言模型间的能力迁移，但其失败机制尚未被充分理解。实验观察到：在代码生成与数学推理任务中，使用更大规模教师时 OPD 出现早期损失平台期（200 步后平均最终损失仅下降 25.1%），而自 RL 教师可达 96.2%。作者希望解释这一差异的成因。

Method: 1) 将 OPD 建模为小学习率极限下的理想化连续时间动力系统；2) 利用训练日志诊断，将平台期与基于梯度的学习信号代理指标的早期下降相关联；3) 在共享参数化与正则条件下，对与初始学生足够接近的教师证明局部恢复保证；4) 通过参数变化幅度与表征相似度（线性 CKA）进行实证测量。

Result: 平台期与学习信号代理指标的早期下降同时出现，但损失仍显著存在；测量本身无法确定底层梯度减弱的原因。对于与初始学生足够接近的教师，在共享参数化和正则条件下存在局部恢复保证，为 self-RL 教师的成功提供了条件性解释。所有运行（含平台期与不含平台期）中参数相对变化仅 0.025–0.098%，OPD 前后学生表征高度相似（各层线性 CKA > 0.98）。

Conclusion: 有限的表征适应可能是学习信号崩塌的一个 contributing 因素，但这仍是待验证的假设，需要进一步实验检验。代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) enables effective capability transfer between language models, yet the mechanisms underlying its failures are not fully understood. Across code generation and mathematical reasoning, OPD with larger-scale teachers exhibits early loss plateaus, with an average final loss reduction of 25.1% after 200 updates, compared with 96.2% for self-RL teachers, obtained by further reinforcement learning (RL) training of the initial student. To understand this difference, we analyze OPD as an idealized continuous-time dynamical system in the small-learning-rate limit. Our training-log diagnostics associate these plateaus with an early decline in a gradient-based learning-signal proxy while substantial loss remains; these measurements do not establish why the underlying gradient weakens. We further prove a local recovery guarantee for teachers sufficiently close to the initial student in a shared parameterization under regularity conditions, offering a conditional explanation for the success of self-RL teachers in our experiments. Across runs with and without loss plateaus, we observe small relative parameter changes (0.025-0.098%) and high similarity between the student's representations before and after OPD (linear CKA $>0.98$ across layers). These observations suggest that limited representation adaptation may contribute to learning-signal collapse, a hypothesis that remains to be tested. Code is available at https://github.com/leizhao7/opd-learning-signals.

</details>


### [114] [Neuro-Memory Fuzzy Inference System for Mimicking Human-like Car Following Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11252)
*Nazmul Haque,Md Asif Raihan. Md. Hadiuzzaman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经-记忆模糊推理系统NeMeFIS，通过整合五类人类记忆，非对称建模跟驰行为中的加速与减速过程，在多种道路与车型上验证其优于传统统计与机器学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计模型与常规机器学习方法难以刻画跟驰行为中加速与减速的非对称性及驾驶员认知机制，缺乏对人类记忆系统影响驾驶决策的建模能力。

Method: 构建分层机器学习架构NeMeFIS，整合程序性、工作、情景、语义与陈述性五类记忆；通过元启发式算法将外部变量映射至记忆函数，并采用因子分析与p值分析进行验证；针对Arterial、Collector与Rural Highway三类走廊及不同车型训练54个模型；同时与线性回归、ANFIS和LSTM进行对比，并通过模糊规则分析揭示各记忆类型的作用。

Result: 54个训练模型揭示了由驾驶员感知极限与认知负荷形成的认知阈值；NeMeFIS在复现真实驾驶行为上优于线性回归、ANFIS和LSTM；模糊规则分析表明陈述性记忆需求最高，尤其在减速阶段，程序性记忆驱动加速，语义与陈述性记忆引导减速；风险感知在城市道路上尤为关键；模型在异质与同质数据集上均得到验证。

Conclusion: NeMeFIS为驾驶员认知建模提供了稳健框架，结果可支持心理治疗应用及网联自动驾驶汽车（CAVs）中自适应、类人决策系统的开发，以提升交通安全。

Abstract: This study presents the Neuro-Memory Fuzzy Inference System (NeMeFIS), a hierarchical machine learning architecture that asymmetrically models acceleration and deceleration in car following behavior by integrating five human memory types procedural, working, episodic, semantic, and declarative. By linking external variables to memory functions via metaheuristics and validating them through factor and p-value analyses, NeMeFIS uncovers latent cognitive influences across Arterial, Collector, and Rural Highway corridors for different types of vehicles. Results from 54 different trained models emphasize cognitive thresholds shaped by driver perception limits and cognitive load. The trained NeMeFIS models outperform traditional statistical and conventional machine learning models in replicating realistic driving behavior, including comparisons with Linear Regression, ANFIS, and LSTM architectures. Fuzzy rule analysis reveals that declarative memory demands the highest rule, especially during deceleration, indicating complex braking decisions. Procedural memory drives acceleration, while semantic and declarative memory guide deceleration. Risk perception also emerges as a key factor, particularly on urban roads. Validated on both heterogeneous and homogeneous datasets, NeMeFIS offers a robust framework for modeling driver cognition. The findings support psychotherapeutic applications and the development of adaptive, human-like decision systems in Connected and Autonomous Vehicles (CAVs) to enhance traffic safety.

</details>


### [115] [Residual spectral instabilities in representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11257)
*Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将VAE中的逐维后验坍缩表述为部分坍缩态附近的涨落理论，以负ELBO为有效自由能，通过其二阶展开的Hessian给出潜在涨落的质量矩阵，导出坍缩方向的不变涨落扇区及其精确质量谱，并给出再激活边界判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的表示会逐次丢失潜在自由度，呈现一连串转变，但其背后的稳定性原理尚不清楚。作者希望为VAE中逐维后验坍缩提供统一的稳定性判据。

Method: 将负证据下界（ELBO）解释为有效自由能，对其作二次展开得到高斯理论，Hessian充当潜在涨落的质量矩阵；证明坍缩方向构成不变涨落扇区，并用条件残差算子导出其精确质量谱；推导再激活判据：当解码器方差低于残差谱上边缘时，局部再激活方向降低自由能，相等即为临界（marginality）；在线性高斯VAE极限下该判据退化为主成分阈值。

Result: 解析得到坍缩扇区的精确质量谱；再激活边界由解码器方差与残差谱上边缘的比较决定，等号对应临界；在线性高斯VAE极限下判据恢复主成分阈值；沿连续连通分支反向看，该边界给出逐次坍缩的局部判据；数值延拓实验显示潜在维度在这些谱临界点附近逐次丢失。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种谱级联解释：坍缩过程由残存表示未能解释的残差信息所支配。

Abstract: Learned representations can lose latent degrees of freedom successively, suggesting a cascade of transitions whose underlying stability principle remains unclear. Here we formulate dimension-wise posterior collapse in variational autoencoder (VAE) as a fluctuation theory around partially collapsed states. Interpreting the negative evidence lower bound as an effective free energy, its quadratic expansion defines a Gaussian theory whose Hessian acts as a mass matrix for latent fluctuations. We show that the collapsed directions form an invariant fluctuation sector and derive its exact mass spectrum in terms of a conditional residual operator. A local reactivation direction lowers the free energy when the decoder variance falls below the residual spectral upper edge, with equality marking marginality. The criterion recovers principal component thresholds in the linear Gaussian VAE limit. Viewed in reverse along continuously connected branches, the reactivation boundary provides a local criterion for successive collapse. Numerical continuation experiments show successive loss of latent dimensions near these spectral marginalities. These results support a spectral cascade interpretation governed by residual information left unexplained by the surviving representation.

</details>


### [116] [How to post-train on a surrogate: Envelope sampling mitigates reward hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11281)
*Sanjit Dandapanthula,Shuvom Sadhuka,Samir Khan,Michael Oberst,Aaditya Ramdas,Alexandra Chouldechova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 envelope sampling：在少量真实标注下，通过最小化后训练模型遗憾上界来重新校准 LLM 评审器，从而缓解 reward hacking。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 后训练常用 LLM 评审器等廉价替代奖励，但因人类偏好等真实奖励查询成本高，代理奖励失准会导致 reward hacking。已有评审器重校准方法要么昂贵要么依赖启发式，且当替代模型在稀有输出集上失准时，on-policy 采样会失效。

Method: 提出 envelope sampling，一种有理论依据的评审器重校准方法。假设人类奖励与重校准奖励位于评审器的 L² 球内，最小化后训练模型遗憾的上界。给出两种从 envelope 采样的实用算法：拒绝采样，或针对修改后的奖励进行微调。

Result: 在临床笔记生成任务和受控的 sycophancy 任务上，基于 envelope 样本的重校准能缓解 reward hacking，而基于基座模型样本的重校准则不能。

Conclusion: envelope sampling 为少量真实标注下的评审器重校准提供了理论支撑且实用的方案，可有效抑制 reward hacking。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are commonly post-trained against LLM judges and other cheap surrogates because the true reward, such as human preference, is too expensive to query at scale. This practice often leads to reward hacking, where reinforcement learning against a miscalibrated surrogate leads to undesirable side effects. In this work, we study a setting in which a small number $n$ of model outputs are annotated with ground-truth labels (e.g., from expert review) and used to recalibrate the LLM judge before optimizing against it. Prior approaches to judge recalibration are costly or heuristic, and it is known that on-policy sampling fails when the surrogate is miscalibrated on a rare set of outputs. In this work, we propose envelope sampling, a theoretically-grounded method for judge recalibration that seeks to minimize an upper bound on the regret of the post-trained model under the assumption that the human reward and re-calibrated reward lie in an $L^2$ ball around the judge. We give practical algorithms to sample from the envelope by rejection or by fine-tuning against a modified reward, and experiments on clinical note generation and on a controlled sycophancy task show that recalibrating on envelope samples mitigates reward hacking where recalibrating on base-model samples does not.

</details>


### [117] [Memorization and Malign Generalization in Conditional Diffusion Models with Random Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11288)
*Gwangho Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在高维比例极限下分析了随机特征条件分数模型，推导出训练与测试损失的渐近表达式，揭示了条件扩散模型在过参数化区域出现的“恶性泛化”现象，以及条件信息量越大越易在较小宽度下记忆训练样本的规律，并用U-Net实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 条件扩散模型能生成多样高质量样本，但其记忆与泛化的理论理解有限，现有工作主要在无条件设定下刻画这些行为，条件设定下的理论分析尚属空白。

Method: 构建随机特征条件分数模型，在高维比例极限下推导训练损失和测试损失的渐近表达式，并通过对测试损失的分解，分别分析过参数化区域中模型宽度对条件依赖均值预测与条件内预测方差的影响，同时从训练损失角度分析条件信息量与记忆现象的关系，辅以真实数据上的U-Net实验。

Result: 在过参数化区域，增加模型宽度可改善条件依赖均值的预测，同时降低条件内预测方差，即“恶性泛化”现象；训练损失分析显示，信息量更丰富的条件会导致模型在更小宽度下记忆训练样本；这些理论结论得到U-Net在真实数据上实验的支持。

Conclusion: 该工作为条件扩散模型的记忆与泛化行为提供了理论刻画，揭示了条件信息量与模型宽度在泛化和记忆之间的权衡机制，深化了对条件生成模型理论性质的理解。

Abstract: Conditional diffusion models generate diverse, novel, and high-quality samples under prescribed conditions. However, theoretical understanding of their memorization and generalization remains limited, while recent works have characterized these behaviors primarily in unconditional settings. In this work, we analyze a random-feature conditional score model in the high-dimensional proportional limit, deriving asymptotic expressions for training and test losses. By decomposing the test loss, we show that in the overparameterized regime, increasing model width improves prediction of the condition-dependent mean while reducing within-condition prediction variance, a phenomenon we term "malign generalization." Furthermore, analyzing the training loss reveals that more informative conditions lead to memorization of training samples at smaller widths. These theoretical findings are supported by experiments with U-Net architectures on realistic data.

</details>


### [118] [From Geometry to Generalization: Why Row Normalization Can Beat Adam and Muon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11309)
*Jihwan Kim,Dogyoon Song,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在高维多分类中，行归一化优化器可比全批量Adam、随机重排Adam代理及精确SVD Muon获得严格更高的总体精度，其根源在于不同优化器诱导的几何结构（行欧氏、逐坐标、谱）对总体决策边界方向的保真度不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同优化器能在拟合同一训练数据的同时选出几何结构显著不同的分类器，但这种差异是否会可证明地影响总体（population）性能此前尚不明确。作者希望从理论上刻画优化器几何结构如何决定泛化精度。

Method: 在等各向同性高斯云数据模型下，对比行归一化、全批量Adam（作为随机重排Adam的代理）与精确SVD Muon三种优化器所诱导的几何：分别为类间欧氏几何、逐坐标几何与谱几何。进一步推广到类均值具有独立取向协方差、测试噪声协方差不同的全批量训练情形，以及幂律谱（类均值谱指数小于1）与强各向异性测试噪声；并分析两协方差为对角且接近时优势反转、以及施加相同随机旋转后优势恢复的现象。辅以合成实验和最后一层语言模型实验验证。

Result: 行归一化在高维多分类中取得严格高于全批量Adam、随机重排Adam代理与精确SVD Muon的总体精度。其优势源于行归一化的类级欧氏几何渐近保持总体决策边界方向，而Adam的逐坐标几何与Muon的谱几何带来不消失的扭曲。该优势在各向异性类均值、幂律谱（指数小于1）及强各向异性测试噪声下依然成立；当两协方差均为对角且足够接近时，相对Adam的优势可反转，而施加相同随机旋转会因改变其与Adam坐标轴的对齐而恢复优势。

Conclusion: 优化器的几何归纳偏置可证明地影响总体精度：行归一化的类级欧氏几何在保持总体决策边界方向上优于Adam的坐标几何与Muon的谱几何。该理论预测得到合成实验与最后一层语言模型实验支持。

Abstract: Different optimizers can fit the same training data while selecting classifiers with substantially different geometries, but whether this difference provably affects population performance remains unclear. We show that row-wise normalization can achieve strictly higher population accuracy than full-batch Adam, a proxy for random-reshuffling Adam, and exact-SVD Muon in high-dimensional multiclass classification. Under an isotropic Gaussian-cloud data model, this advantage arises because row normalization's class-wise Euclidean geometry asymptotically preserves the population decision-boundary directions, whereas Adam's coordinate-wise geometry and Muon's spectral geometry introduce nonvanishing distortions. Beyond isotropy, the advantage persists for full-batch training on class means with independently oriented class-mean and test-noise covariances. It holds for power-law spectra with class-mean exponent below one, even under heavily anisotropic test noise. When both covariances are diagonal and sufficiently close, the advantage over Adam can reverse, while applying the same random rotation to both restores it by changing only their alignment with Adam's coordinate axes. Synthetic and last-layer language-model experiments support the predicted advantage.

</details>


### [119] [NP-Hardness of Minimizing Neurons in Two-Hidden-Layer ReLU Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11313)
*Sangrock Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在 $L^p(\mathbb{R}^d,\mathbb{R}^m)$ 逼近约束下，精确计算两层隐藏层 ReLU 网络逼近给定目标函数所需的最小隐藏神经元数目是 NP-hard 的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络架构优化的一个基本问题：能否高效计算在给定容差内逼近目标函数所需的最小隐藏神经元数量。本文针对两层隐藏层 ReLU 网络在 $L^p$ 逼近约束下回答该问题。

Method: 对任意固定 $d\ge1$、$m\ge1$、$1\le p<\infty$，通过从 3-SAT 出发的多项式时间归约证明精确求解该最优化问题是 NP-hard；构造了由两层隐藏层 ReLU 网络实现、紧支撑的多面体 frustum 函数，并证明 box-frustum 函数有限线性组合在 $L^p$ 中稠密。

Result: 归约产生架构间隙：不可满足公式的最优值为零，可满足公式的最优值至少为 $d+2$。该结论即便在目标由有理 ReLU 网络表示且其实现非零、逐分量非负、紧支撑、全局 Lipschitz、连续分片仿射时仍成立。

Conclusion: 在多项式时间内达到 ReLU 网络的最小配置在计算上不可实现，这为在 ReLU 网络设计中采用启发式逼近方法提供了理论依据。

Abstract: A fundamental question in neural network architecture optimization is whether the minimum hidden-neuron count required to approximate a target function within a prescribed tolerance can be computed efficiently. This paper resolves this question for two-hidden-layer ReLU networks under an $L^p(\mathbb{R}^d,\mathbb{R}^m)$ approximation constraint. For every fixed $d \ge 1$, $m \ge 1$, and $1 \le p < \infty$, we prove that computing the optimum exactly is NP-hard. The result holds even when the target is represented by a rational ReLU network whose realization is nonzero, componentwise nonnegative, compactly supported, globally Lipschitz, and continuous piecewise affine. The polynomial-time reduction from 3-SAT produces an architecture gap in which unsatisfiable formulas yield an optimum of zero, whereas satisfiable formulas yield an optimum of at least $d+2$. The proof constructs compactly supported polyhedral frustum functions realized by two-hidden-layer ReLU networks and establishes the $L^p$-density of finite linear combinations of box-frustum functions. The results offer theoretical justification for employing heuristic approximation methods in the design of ReLU neural networks, illustrating that attaining a minimal configuration within polynomial time is computationally unachievable.

</details>


### [120] [Deflating the Hessian: Rank-4 W4A4 Quantization for Multimodal Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11315)
*Shiwen Wang,Pengxiang Zhao,Xiaoming Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出统一框架，将低秩辅助的W4A4 PTQ建模为耦合校准问题，联合优化低秩补偿与残差量化并抑制激活量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer中，低秩分支可缓解4-bit权重-激活后训练量化损失。但现有方法要么分别优化低秩补偿与残差量化、需要更高秩，要么依赖二阶权重更新而未显式建模激活量化误差，该误差在4-bit下尤为突出。

Method: 将低秩辅助的W4A4 PTQ建模为耦合校准问题，从联合目标导出基于优化的求解器：消去输出侧低秩因子得到“deflated Hessian”，对已被低秩分量捕获的残差误差进行折减；同时引入激活噪声代理项抑制激活量化误差。

Result: 在五个扩散骨干上，rank-4方法在PSNR与LPIPS上持续优于rank-4 SVDQuant；在SANA-1.6B、FLUX.1-schnell、FLUX.1-dev上以8倍更小秩超过rank-32 SVDQuant，量化速度最高快6.25倍；在Qwen3-8B上MMLU准确率由61.50%提升至68.17%。

Conclusion: 该方法以显著更低的秩和量化开销实现了更优的W4A4性能，验证了耦合校准与deflated Hessian、激活噪声代理的有效性。

Abstract: In diffusion transformers, low-rank branches can mitigate 4-bit weight--activation (W4A4) post-training quantization (PTQ) loss by decomposing each weight into a low-bit residual and a high-precision low-rank component. Existing low-rank PTQ approaches, however, either optimize low-rank compensation and residual quantization separately, often requiring higher ranks, or rely on second-order weight updates without explicitly modeling activation quantization error, which becomes particularly pronounced under 4-bit quantization. To address these limitations, we present \method{}, a unified framework modeling low-rank-assisted W4A4 PTQ as a coupled calibration problem and deriving optimization-based solvers from the joint objective. Eliminating the output-side low-rank factor yields a \emph{deflated Hessian} that discounts residual errors already captured by the low-rank component, while an activation-noise surrogate is incorporated to suppress activation quantization error. Across five diffusion backbones, rank-4 \method{} consistently outperforms rank-4 SVDQuant in PSNR and LPIPS. It further surpasses rank-32 SVDQuant on SANA-1.6B, FLUX.1-schnell, and FLUX.1-dev with an $8\times$ smaller rank and up to $6.25\times$ faster quantization. Furthermore, on the Qwen3-8B LLM, rank-4 \method{} improves MMLU accuracy from 61.50\% to 68.17\% over rank-32 SVDQuant. Overall, \method{} achieves better W4A4 performance with substantially lower rank and quantization cost.

</details>


### [121] [Sample-Efficient Generative Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11349)
*Minxing Zheng,Shixiang Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CASA（Conformal Adaptive Sample Allocation），通过刻画额外采样样本的边际价值，在不同输入间自适应分配采样预算，以在满足边际覆盖与平均采样预算约束下最小化期望集合大小，从而缓解固定采样数导致的集合膨胀与欠覆盖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式共形预测依赖条件生成器的样本构建不确定集合，其效率取决于样本是否充分代表响应分布。对于大型扩散模型与科学模拟器等场景，采样成本高昂，必须高效使用采样预算。现有方法对每个输入使用相同采样数，在响应分布简单的输入上浪费样本，在复杂输入上采样不足，导致集合膨胀并使困难输入覆盖不足。

Method: CASA 首先刻画增加一个样本所带来的边际价值，然后以最小化期望集合大小为目标，在边际覆盖与平均采样预算约束下，将样本数自适应地分配 across 不同输入。文中还给出理论分析，说明自适应分配在同一预算下优于固定样本数；若遗漏某个模态，则半径必须跨越模态间间隙，即使使用 oracle 半径也无法弥补。实验在合成与真实任务上验证，并与已有的半径自适应方法形成互补。

Result: 理论分析表明，在相同预算下自适应分配比固定采样数产生更小的集合，且遗漏模态导致的半径膨胀无法由 oracle 半径补偿。实验结果显示，在合成与真实任务上，CASA 在相同预算下产生显著更小的集合，常能改善条件覆盖，并且可与现有半径自适应方法互补。

Conclusion: CASA 通过将采样预算从"每个输入固定样本数"转变为"按边际价值自适应分配"，在同等平均预算下获得更紧的不确定集合，并缓解困难输入的欠覆盖问题，为高成本生成式共形预测提供了一种高效的预算分配方案，且与半径自适应方法兼容。

Abstract: Generative conformal prediction builds uncertainty sets from samples of a conditional generator, which are efficient only when the samples represent the response distribution well. This can require many samples, each of which can be costly, as in large diffusion models and scientific simulators, so the sampling budget must be used efficiently. Existing methods draw the same number of samples at every input, wasting samples where the response distribution is simple and undersampling where it is complex, which inflates sets and leaves those inputs under-covered. We propose CASA (Conformal Adaptive Sample Allocation), which characterizes the marginal value of an additional sample and allocates samples across inputs to minimize the expected set size subject to marginal coverage and an average sampling budget. Theoretical analysis shows that adaptive allocation yields smaller sets than a fixed count at the same budget: a missed mode forces a radius that spans the gap between modes, and even oracle radius cannot compensate for it. On synthetic and real tasks, CASA produces substantially smaller sets at the same budget, often improves conditional coverage, and complements existing radius-adaptive methods.

</details>


### [122] [Bernoulli Flow Models: Self-Consistent Generative Modeling for Binary Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11362)
*Hao Mo,Liying Yang,Shumin Yao,Xinxing Yu,Ajian Liu,Xudong Mao,Yanyan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bernoulli Flow Models（BFM），用连续全局伯努利概率流替代离散扩散链，实现低NFE下的自洽采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二值扩散模型生成高质量样本需大量函数评估（NFE），推理代价高；现有模型在低NFE跨步采样时用单步似然近似真实多步似然，导致质量严重下降，且存在训练-推理结构失配。

Method: 不再依赖序列化单步马尔可夫扩散链，而是在数据分布与纯噪声之间定义统一的连续全局伯努利概率流路径，并据此推导任意时间区间上的解析闭式后验转移；低NFE推理只需在新时间网格上重新计算解析后验，而非跳过离散步骤。

Result: 对NFE削减高度鲁棒。在LSUN Churches 256×256上，256步训练的BFM仅用16步采样即达FID 9.22，而最先进离散基线退化至204.10；在标准全步推理下也与连续与离散生成基线保持竞争力。

Conclusion: BFM是理论严谨、自洽且实用的快速二值数据生成框架，消除了离散链固有的训练-推理失配。

Abstract: Binary diffusion models typically require a large number of function evaluations (NFEs) to generate high-quality samples, making practical inference computationally expensive. Reducing NFEs while preserving sample quality without distillation or additional training remains a significant challenge. Existing binary diffusion models define a discrete one-step forward path and then derive the reverse posterior. In low-NFE settings requiring cross-step sampling, they approximate the true multi-step likelihood with a single-step likelihood transition, which severely degrades sample quality. To address this fundamental limitation and decouple the generative dynamics from fixed discrete time steps, we propose Bernoulli Flow Models (BFM). Rather than relying on sequential one-step Markov diffusion chains, BFM defines a unified continuous global Bernoulli probability flow path between data distributions and pure noise, from which we derive analytical closed-form posterior transitions over arbitrary time intervals. Consequently, reducing the inference NFE is no longer an approximation based on skipping discrete steps; it only requires re-evaluating the analytical posterior over a new time grid. This eliminates the structural training-inference mismatch inherent to discrete chains and yields self-consistent low-NFE sampling. Experiments show that BFM is highly robust to aggressive NFE reduction. On LSUN Churches 256x256, a BFM trained with 256 steps achieves an FID of 9.22 using only 16 sampling steps, whereas the state-of-the-art discrete baseline degrades to 204.10. BFM also remains competitive with continuous and discrete generative baselines under standard full-step inference. These results establish BFM as a theoretically rigorous, self-consistent, and practically effective framework for fast binary data generation.

</details>


### [123] [SpatialOPSD: Self-Distilling Spatial Intelligence from Verified Coding Agent Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11366)
*Rongxue Li,Meng Yang,Yiru Mao,Yongliang Tao,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Bowen Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpatialOPSD是一个on-policy自蒸馏框架，通过将空间编码智能体的验证执行轨迹作为特权信息，令MLLM内化空间推理能力，实现免工具推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间编码智能体依赖外部工具生成执行轨迹来提升多模态大模型的空间推理，但存在推理开销高和外部依赖问题。论文探索能否让MLLM内化这种智能体能力，从而完全无需工具运行。

Method: 首先观察到用总结后的智能体执行轨迹提示MLLM可激发其内部空间Chain-of-Thought。基于此提出SpatialOPSD，一种on-policy自蒸馏框架，将验证过的智能体轨迹作为特权信息来内化空间推理。为缓解蒸馏中的特权信息泄露，提出Repetition-Aware Distillation，结合重复掩码与unlikelihood正则化。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，SpatialOPSD自蒸馏在空间和OOD数据集上的平均准确率均高于SFT和GRPO，表现出更优的性能与泛化能力。

Conclusion: MLLM可内化空间编码智能体的能力以实现免工具推理，SpatialOPSD通过on-policy自蒸馏及泄露缓解策略，在准确率与泛化上优于SFT和GRPO。

Abstract: Spatial coding agents significantly improve spatial reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by using external tools to generate verified execution traces. However, this paradigm inherently suffers from prohibitive inference-time overhead and external dependencies. In this paper, we explore whether an MLLM can internalize this agentic capability to operate entirely tool-free. We begin with a simple observation: prompting an MLLM with summarized execution traces of a spatial coding agent naturally unlocks the model's internal spatial Chain-of-Thought (CoT). Motivated by this, we introduce SpatialOPSD, an on-policy self-distillation framework that internalizes spatial reasoning into a standalone MLLM by formulating verified agent traces as privileged information. To mitigate privileged-information leakage during distillation, we introduce Repetition-Aware Distillation, which combines repetition masking with unlikelihood regularization. Experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that self-distilling SpatialOPSD achieves higher average accuracy than SFT and GRPO on both spatial and OOD datasets, exhibiting superior performance and generalization.

</details>


### [124] [From a Prompt to Repertoires: Evolving Functional REpertoires Enable LLM Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11373)
*Fengyuan Liu,Yue Wang,Hangxi Guo,Fengyuan Liu,Chenxu Wu,Yanguang Liu,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 EFRE（Evolving Functional REpertoires），将单一提示替换为随新任务到来而演化的功能库，以缓解提示优化在持续学习中的灾难性遗忘，实验上显著优于 GRPO 并降低跨任务性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的持续学习通常依赖精心设计的参数更新策略，成本高；提示优化虽能避免昂贵的参数更新并在单任务上取得接近甚至优于 GRPO 的效果，但作者发现其在顺序任务适配下存在灾难性遗忘，且优化后的提示会累积过拟合于局部任务分布的规则，因此需要新的高效适配方案。

Method: 提出 EFRE（Evolving Functional REpertoires）：用一组功能（functions）构成的功能库替代单一提示，并随新任务到来而演化——兼容性更新对现有功能进行精炼（refine），冲突性更新触发新功能产生（emergence）。

Result: 在三任务持续学习流上，EFRE 的最终平均性能比 GRPO 高 7.50 个百分点；适配 Bio 任务后，其在 FinQA 上的性能仅下降 1.56 个百分点，而基础提示优化方法下降 25.10 个百分点。在最小化智能体系统中，跨不同骨干模型均观察到一致性提升。

Conclusion: EFRE 在大语言模型持续学习上表现强劲，并展现出在先进智能体系统中持续学习的重要潜力。

Abstract: Continual learning remains challenging for large language models, which must enable models to acquire new skills and knowledge without degrading existing capabilities. Existing approaches typically address this challenge by carefully designing how model parameters are updated. In contrast, prompt optimization avoids costly parameter updates while achieving competitive or even superior performance to reinforcement learning methods such as GRPO on individual knowledge-intensive and reasoning tasks. This raises a natural question: \textit{Can prompt optimization, as an efficient adaptation approach, be directly applied to continual learning?} Our analysis shows that, under sequential task adaptation, it suffers from catastrophic forgetting, while optimized prompts accumulate rules that overfit to local task distributions. To address these limitations, we propose \emph{Evolving Functional REpertoires} (EFRE), which replaces a single prompt with a repertoire of functions that evolves as new tasks arrive: compatible updates refine existing functions, while conflicting updates trigger the emergence of new ones. On a three-task continual-learning stream, EFRE achieves a final average performance 7.50 percentage points higher than GRPO. Moreover, after adaptation to the Bio task, its performance on FinQA decreases by only 1.56 percentage points, compared with 25.10 percentage points for the base prompt optimization method. We further instantiate EFRE in a minimal agent system and observe consistent improvements across different backbone models. Overall, these results demonstrate EFRE's strong performance in continual learning for large language models and highlight its substantial potential for continual learning in advanced agent systems.

</details>


### [125] [CoPoE: Multimodal Fusion via Decomposable Disease-Coordinate Product-of-Experts for Missing-Modality Alzheimer's Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11394)
*Chihun An,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出疾病坐标专家乘积框架CoPoE，将多模态AD证据映射到R/P/N/S四轴结构化潜空间，用掩码PoE只融合可用模态，无需合成补全即可对任意非空模态子集给出稳健可分解后验，在ADNI缺失模态基准上取得最优性能与校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态AD诊断需整合临床、影像、基因组与生物标志物等异质证据，但临床队列普遍存在不规则的模态缺失。现有融合方法要么合成缺失输入、引入人工替代值，要么把可用信号汇入不可解释的潜空间，损害可靠性与可解释性。

Method: 提出CoPoE（Disease-Coordinate Product-of-Experts）：将多模态证据映射到划分为遗传风险(R)、分子病理(P)、神经退行(N)、临床分期(S)四轴的结构化潜空间；每个观测模态对完整RPNS向量参数化一个对角高斯专家，再用掩码专家乘积(PoE)架构只融合可用模态——缺失模态不向融合路径贡献因子，从而在RPNS路径上无需合成插补即可对任意非空模态子集保持稳健、可分解的后验。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上进行大量缺失模态实验：在共享的非PET ADNI嵌入基准下，CoPoE取得最佳全模态性能，并在全部15种观测子集评估中取得最高平均AUROC，优于标准化缺失模态融合基线；同时显著改善原始概率的ECE、Brier分数与NLL。PET监督探针显示全模态下病理(P)块存在证据富集，且在撤除直接流体生物样本输入时仍保留tau相关信号。

Conclusion: 掩码专家乘积与疾病坐标潜空间的结合，为不规则模态缺失下的多模态AD诊断提供了无需合成插补、可分解且校准良好的融合方案，并通过PET监督探针验证了病理轴的表征特异性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multimodal Alzheimer's disease (AD) diagnosis benefits from integrating heterogeneous clinical, imaging, genomic, and biomarker evidence, but clinical cohorts frequently suffer from irregular modality missingness. Existing fusion methods often synthesize absent inputs, risking the introduction of artificial surrogates, or pool available signals into uninterpretable latent spaces. We present CoPoE (Disease-Coordinate Product-of-Experts), a disease-coordinate framework that maps multimodal evidence into a structured latent space partitioned into four distinct biological and clinical axes: genetic Risk, molecular Pathology, Neurodegeneration, and clinical Stage (R/P/N/S). Each observed modality parameterizes a diagonal Gaussian expert over the full RPNS vector, and a masked Product-of-Experts architecture fuses only the available modalities. Consequently, absent modalities add no factor to the fusion path, allowing the network to preserve a robust, decomposable posterior for any non-empty modality subset without synthetic imputation in the RPNS path. Through extensive missing-modality experiments on the ADNI dataset, CoPoE achieves the best all-modality performance and the highest mean AUROC across all 15 observed-subset evaluations among standardized missing-modality fusion baselines under a shared non-PET ADNI embedding benchmark, while substantially improving raw-probability ECE, Brier score, and NLL. Furthermore, PET-supervised probing shows evidence enrichment within the pathology (P) block under full modalities, with tau-related signal retained even when direct fluid biospecimen inputs are withheld. Our code is available at https://github.com/labhai/CoPoE.

</details>


### [126] [Learning from Hetero Density for Cryo-EM Protein Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11403)
*Xu Han,Chaozhuo Li,Xiaowei Yuan,Yuancheng Sun,Kang Liu,Qiwei Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CryoCue 框架，利用异源组分信息引导 cryo-EM 蛋白质结构重建，提升骨架定位与结构重建精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从 cryo-EM 密度图重建蛋白质结构对理解大分子组装至关重要。现有学习方法虽已改进重建效果，但对异源组分（hetero components）信息的利用仍不充分。分析发现，异源组分附近既存在错误预测，也存在真实蛋白质位点；简单过滤邻近候选会因情况不同而改善或损害链构建，因此需要更精细地利用异源信息。

Method: 提出 CryoCue 框架：采用锚点监督的检测器，在五类组分上学习异源表征；利用多尺度异源特征引导骨架定位；并将预测的异源候选通过其类别、置信度和相对坐标系几何关系来条件化结构精修。

Result: 实验表明，CryoCue 改善了异源组分附近的骨架定位，并实现了更精确的蛋白质结构重建。

Conclusion: 有效利用异源组分信息可显著提升 cryo-EM 蛋白质重建质量，CryoCue 为此提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Reconstructing protein structures from cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) maps is essential for understanding macromolecular assemblies. Although learning-based methods have improved protein reconstruction, information from hetero components remains underused. Our analysis finds both false predictions and reference protein sites near hetero components; filtering nearby candidates can improve or impair chain construction. We introduce CryoCue, a framework that uses hetero information to guide protein reconstruction. An anchor-supervised detector learns hetero representations across five component classes. Multiscale hetero features guide backbone localization, while predicted hetero candidates condition structure refinement through their class, confidence, and frame-relative geometry. Experiments show that CryoCue improves backbone localization near hetero components and achieves more accurate protein structure reconstruction.

</details>


### [127] [MC-TRCM: Observation-Aware Recursive Fusion for Incomplete Mobile and Wearable Mental-Health Feature Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11408)
*Wentao Wang,Lifeng Han,Zining Ren,Hengyu Zhong,Guangyu Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MC-TRCM模型处理移动/可穿戴心理健康数据中以锚点聚合的多源特征表：将各特征来源保留为独立token并将缺失性纳入输入上下文，在DepreST-CAT与PSYCHE-D六个端点上取得最优PHQ-9/GAD-7严重度MAE与具竞争力的分类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开的移动与可穿戴心理健康数据集多提供汇总特征表而非同步原始传感器流，每个锚点对应一次问卷/标签时间，可能混合手机或可穿戴摘要、既往症状评分、人口学、临床变量与来源可用性指示。这类数据存在显著的异质性与系统性缺失，传统将各行拼成单一特征向量的表格方法难以显式刻画来源差异与缺失机制，因此需要一种能按来源建模并显式利用缺失信息的模型。

Method: 提出Modality-Conditioned Temporal Recursive Context Model（MC-TRCM）：(1) 将每个特征来源保留为独立token；(2) 将缺失性作为输入上下文的一部分，观测来源用数值与缺失性摘要编码，缺失来源使用可学习的absence token；(3) 以数据集与任务嵌入对融合过程进行条件化（conditioning）；(4) 递归预测头在验证集选定的步数上对输出逐步精化。评估在DepreST-CAT与PSYCHE-D的六个预定义端点上进行，采用参与者级划分与仅基于验证集的模型选择。文中还进行了消融，验证Feature-wise Linear Modulation（FiLM）、absence token、缺失性投影与递归精化的作用，并开展校准与特征来源控制实验。

Result: 在DepreST-CAT的PHQ-9与GAD-7严重度回归上取得最低MAE，较最佳表格基线分别改善0.181与0.217个量表分。分类端点表现依赖任务：在GAD-7取整类别上与最佳基线平衡准确率持平；在PSYCHE-D多分类上以0.002平衡准确率数值最高；在PHQ-9类别与PSYCHE-D二分类上接近最强基线。消融支持FiLM、absence token、缺失性投影与递归精化各组件的贡献，校准与特征来源控制实验刻画了各端点行为。

Conclusion: 在仅提供锚点级汇总特征表的移动与可穿戴心理健康数据设定下，显式按特征来源分token建模并将缺失性作为条件上下文，结合递归精化与数据集/任务条件化，可在严重度回归上稳定优于表格基线，并在分类任务上保持与强基线相当或略优的表现；各组件消融证实其必要性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Public mobile and wearable mental-health datasets often provide summarized feature tables rather than synchronized raw sensor streams. In these releases, each anchor corresponds to a survey or label time and may combine phone or wearable summaries, prior symptom scores, demographics, clinical variables, and source-availability indicators. We propose the Modality-Conditioned Temporal Recursive Context Model (MC-TRCM), which preserves each feature source as a separate token and incorporates missingness as part of the input context. Observed sources are encoded with values and missingness summaries, absent sources use learned absence tokens, dataset and task embeddings condition fusion, and a recursive prediction head refines each output over validation-selected steps. We evaluated MC-TRCM on six predefined endpoints from DepreST-CAT and Prediction of Severity Change-Depression (PSYCHE-D) using participant-level splits and validation-only model selection. MC-TRCM achieved the lowest mean absolute error on DepreST-CAT Patient Health Questionnaire-9 (PHQ-9) and Generalized Anxiety Disorder-7 (GAD-7) severity, improving over the best tabular reference by 0.181 and 0.217 scale points. Classification endpoints showed task-dependent behavior: MC-TRCM matched the best rounded GAD-7 category balanced accuracy, was numerically highest by 0.002 balanced-accuracy points on PSYCHE-D multiclass prediction, and remained close to the strongest references on PHQ-9 category and PSYCHE-D binary prediction. Ablations support Feature-wise Linear Modulation, absence tokens, missingness projections, and recursive refinement, while calibration and feature-source controls characterize endpoint behavior. Our code is available at https://github.com/Botwwt/MC-TRCM.

</details>


### [128] [Policy Alignment: New Signals for Membership Auditing in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11423)
*Yilong Yang,Wenzhuo Shang,Yule Liu,Jiale Teng,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PAMA，一种面向在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的成员审计框架，通过测量学生更新是否朝向降低教师损失的方向来判定提示词是否为蒸馏成员，在六个数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD在蒸馏时会使用常为私有且昂贵的提示词（prompt），存在提示词级别成员审计需求。现有方法依赖基于似然的置信信号或检查点间的学生策略漂移，未能捕捉教师引导的学生更新方向这一关键信息。

Method: 基于"成员提示词直接贡献于教师引导的策略更新，非成员仅通过跨提示词泛化受间接影响"的观察，PAMA将审计转化为：测量学生更新是否朝着降低候选提示词上教师损失的方向。具体提出Teacher Alignment Gain（TAG）从模型输出估计教师对齐的更新方向，并与学生漂移、不确定性对齐信号结合进行可靠审计。

Result: 在六个数据集、三个教师-学生模型家族上评估。在主要基准MATH上，PAMA取得AUC 0.791–0.941，较最先进基线提升14.6–20.6%。

Conclusion: 利用教师引导的更新方向性痕迹（direction trace）能有效实现OPD场景下的提示词级成员审计，优于仅依赖似然或策略漂移的现有方法。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model by aligning its policy with a teacher model on trajectories generated by the student model itself. Through this process, the student policy moves toward the teacher on the prompts used for distillation. However, these prompts are often private and costly, creating a need for prompt-level membership auditing. Existing methods mainly rely on likelihood-based confidence signals or student policy drift between checkpoints, but they do not capture the teacher-induced direction of the student update. In this paper, we propose Policy Alignment Membership Auditing (PAMA), a new auditing framework tailored for OPD. Our key observation is that a member prompt directly contributes to the teacher-guided policy update, while a non-member prompt only experiences indirect effects through cross-prompt generalization. Based on this directional trace, PAMA measures whether the student update moves toward reducing the teacher loss on a candidate prompt. Specifically, we introduce Teacher Alignment Gain (TAG) to estimate the teacher-aligned update direction from model outputs, and further combine it with student drift and uncertainty alignment signals for reliable membership auditing. We evaluate PAMA on six datasets and three teacher-student model families. On MATH, the primary evaluation benchmark, PAMA achieves AUC values of 0.791--0.941, improving AUC by 14.6--20.6% over state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [129] [MotiveMob: Motivation as Semantic Action for Closed-Loop Human Mobility Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11442)
*Mengkun Gao,Zengqing Wu,Renhe Jiang,Jiawei Wang,Yusong Wang,Chuang Yang,Shuyuan Zheng,Makoto Onizuka,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MotiveMob是一个动机驱动的自回归人类移动性生成框架，先假设"为何移动"再联合生成"去向与时间"，在用户级与时间分布偏移下取得更优的分布保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动性可建模为"为何—何处—何时"的决策过程，但现有方法多将行为意图粗粒度表示（如日计划或轨迹级描述），或直接预测下一步位置而不显式推理每步移动的动机，因而在用户级与时间分布偏移下泛化不足。

Method: 提出MotiveMob自回归框架：每步中，动机预测器基于当前移动状态、长期行为报告与移动历史推断合理解释动机或判定轨迹终止；状态预测器将假设的动机落地为候选下一位置与到达时间；候选再经速度可行性检查与重复性检查后反馈进入下一轮决策。

Result: 在涉及未见用户、未见时间段（含季节变化及疫情造成的显著行为扰动）的分布偏移评估中，MotiveMob在分布保真度上持续优于基于预训练与基于提示的竞争方法，对分布外移动模式展现出稳健泛化能力。

Conclusion: 显式建模"为何—何处—何时"的动机驱动决策结构，有助于提升人类移动性生成在用户级与时间分布偏移下的分布保真度与泛化性。

Abstract: Human mobility generation, an important task in urban research, synthesizes trajectory data for urban planning and transportation management. Human mobility can be characterized as a "why-where-when" decision process: people form an intention to move and then determine where and when the corresponding activity will take place. Trajectory generation under user-level and temporal distribution shifts may benefit from explicitly modeling this decision structure. However, many existing human mobility generation methods either represent behavioral intent at a coarse granularity, such as a daily plan or a trajectory-level description, or directly predict future locations without explicitly reasoning about a possible motivation for each movement step. We introduce MotiveMob, a motivation-driven autoregressive framework for human mobility generation that first forms a hypothesis about why the next movement may occur and then jointly generates where and when it may occur. At each step, a motivation predictor conditions on the current mobility state, a long-term behavioral report, and the mobility history to infer a plausible motivation or determine whether the trajectory should terminate. Given the hypothesized motivation, a state predictor grounds it in a candidate next location and arrival time. The candidate then undergoes speed-feasibility and repetition checks before being fed back for the next decision. We evaluate MotiveMob under distribution shifts involving unseen users and unseen temporal periods, including seasonal changes and the substantial behavioral disruption caused by the COVID-19 pandemic. Experiments show that MotiveMob consistently achieves better distributional fidelity than competitive pretraining-based and prompting-based methods under user-level and temporal distribution shifts, demonstrating robust generalization to out-of-distribution mobility patterns.

</details>


### [130] [Zatom-2: Multitask Pretraining on Atomistic Data for Generative Modeling across Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11454)
*Miruna Cretu,Alex Abrudan,Antonia Panescu,Tynan Perez,Rishabh Anand,N. Benjamin Erichson,Michael W. Mahoney,Samuel Blau,Joseph Jacobson,Rafael Gómez-Bombarelli,Rex Ying,Tuomas Knowles,Pietro Liò,Alex Morehead*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Zatom-2：基于约500万OMol25与OMat24结构预训练的原子级生成模型，采用多尺度Transformer与条件流匹配，统一支持分子与材料生成、结构预测及能量/力预测，并在蛋白质等低数据场景中通过迁移学习提升生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有原子级生成模型局限于特定学科（化学或生物），或未同时利用大规模有机（分子）与无机（材料）数据进行通用预训练，难以在数据稀缺的生物场景中迁移，阻碍跨领域发现。

Method: 构建Zatom-2原子级生成模型，在约500万OMol25与OMat24电子结构数据上预训练；采用多尺度Transformer架构结合条件流匹配，支持力条件化及生成、结构预测、分子与材料能量/力预测等基础任务；通过联合生成-预测预训练与迁移学习进行下游适配。

Result: 相比Zatom-1，Zatom-2在分子分布保真度上更优，并在现有分子与材料生成基准上表现强劲；可跨低力与高力区间控制样本生成；在低数据蛋白质生成中，经2000个蛋白域微调，长度外推设置下的蛋白骨架可设计性由无预训练的67.8%提升至74.8%。

Conclusion: Zatom-2验证了统一原子级生成模型跨化学与生物领域的可行性，借助大规模跨域预训练与条件流匹配，实现了多任务能力与低数据迁移的性能提升，为通用原子级建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Unified atomistic modeling has the potential to accelerate discovery in chemistry, materials science, and biology by bridging data-rich chemical domains and data-scarce biological contexts. However, existing generative approaches to atomistic modeling remain highly specialized to scientific disciplines (chemistry vs. biology) or do not leverage both high-volume organic (molecule) and inorganic (material) data for general-purpose pretraining. To this end, we introduce Zatom-2, an atomistic generative model pretrained on approximately five million structures from the OMol25 and OMat24 electronic structure datasets. Zatom-2 features a multiscale Transformer architecture coupled with conditional flow matching that supports force conditioning and foundational pretraining tasks such as generation, structure prediction, and prediction of molecular and material energies and forces. Empirically, Zatom-2 achieves better molecular distribution fidelity than Zatom-1 and achieves strong performance on existing molecule and material generation benchmarks. Zatom-2 demonstrates the ability to control sample generation across low- and high-force regimes, and enhances protein generation in a low-data setting through joint generative-predictive pretraining and transfer learning, increasing protein backbone designability in a length extrapolation setting from 67.8% without pretraining to 74.8% after finetuning on 2,000 protein domains.

</details>


### [131] [Generative Adversarial Loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11458)
*Kislay Aditya Oj,Nidhi Jain,Sri Surya Varma Datla,Priyanka Jayaswal,Kumar Krishna Agrawal,Aditya Desai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生成对抗循环（GAL）框架，通过判别器代理自动生成对抗数据以暴露当前算法弱点，并由生成器代理发现新算法加以克服，在KV压缩、稀疏视频生成、稀疏注意力与上下文扩展四类任务上实现了自主目标设定与算法改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI研究进展由基准创建与方法发现两过程驱动，过去均依赖人类智能。近年方法发现的自动化加速，但基准创建自动化关注不足，制约了自推进系统的发展，因此需要让系统自主设定目标（寻找自身弱点）并改进算法。

Method: 提出生成对抗循环（GAL），一个生成器-判别器框架，交替进行两种代理搜索：(1) 判别器代理生成对抗数据，暴露当前系统弱点，自动完成目标设定；(2) 生成器代理发现算法以克服这些弱点。该框架应用于高效推理的近似算法，区别于以往仅聚焦算法发现的自动研究系统。

Result: 在四类任务上展示对抗数据生成：KV压缩、稀疏视频生成、稀疏注意力、上下文扩展，判别器均能识别当前最优技术的弱点。GAL实现自主改进：KV压缩中，Qwen3-4B下CompactorPress在4x压缩时判别器数据集性能从0.35提升至0.97，同时超越RULER-HARD（+0.77分）；上下文扩展中，Dual Chunk Attention从0.20提升至0.90，并在标准基准上取得增益（ScienceFiction +6分，PG19 32K -0.33 PPL）。

Conclusion: GAL为自主目标设定与算法改进提供了路径，使AI系统能持续发现自身弱点并开发克服方法，推动自推进系统发展。

Abstract: AI research progress can be viewed as the interaction between two processes: benchmark creation and method discovery. Historically, both were driven by human intelligence. However, recent advances in AI have accelerated automated method discovery, while automated benchmark creation has received comparatively less attention. To enable self-advancing systems, we propose Generative Adversarial Loop (GAL), a generator-discriminator framework alternating between two agentic searches: (1) a discriminator that generates adversarial data to expose weaknesses in current systems, and (2) a generator that discovers algorithms to overcome them. We apply this framework to approximation algorithms for efficient inference. Unlike existing auto research systems, which primarily focus on algorithm discovery, GAL introduces a discriminator agent that automates goalpost setting by continually searching for weaknesses in the current algorithm. We demonstrate adversarial data generation across four tasks: KV compression, sparse video generation, sparse attention, and context extension, where the discriminator identifies weaknesses in state of the art techniques. We further show that GAL enables autonomous improvement, with newly discovered algorithms improving not only on adversarially generated data, but also on established benchmarks. Specifically, GAL improves CompactorPress on KV compression with Qwen3-4B at 4x, raising performance on the discriminator dataset from 0.35 to 0.97, while also outperforming RULER-HARD (+0.77 pts). For context extension, GAL boosts Dual Chunk Attention from 0.20 to 0.90 on the discriminator dataset, while yielding gains on standard benchmarks(ScienceFiction (+6 pts) and PG19 32K (-0.33 PPL)). GAL thus provides a path toward autonomous goalpost setting and algorithmic improvement, where AI systems continually discover their own weaknesses and develop methods to overcome them.

</details>


### [132] [Rare Gate Disagreements Can Limit Plasticity: When Gradient Flow Mispredicts Finite-Batch SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11475)
*Ruoyu Zhao,Mingxuan Zhang,Jianbo Dai,Jiaqi Wu,Chenyu Zhu,Tong Che*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 群体梯度流会定性误判有限批量SGD；在双单元ReLU回归中，源任务使两神经元正比例化、目标任务奖励分离时，小步长SGD在"小步长+长预训练"联合极限下失效，恢复所需样本与批量随预训练时间指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体梯度流常用于分析神经网络（含预训练后）的适应过程，但作者发现它会定性错误预测有限批量随机梯度下降的行为，并试图定位这一差异的具体机制。

Method: 在双单元ReLU回归中设置源任务（驱使两神经元趋向正比例）与目标任务（奖励分离它们）。源训练时间T后，对比群体梯度流与批量b、步长η的在线SGD；在目标克隆处分析群体不稳定性：两ReLU门不一致的输入构成概率为δ/π的楔形区，权重衰减使角度指数收缩，其他输入上两单元接收相同随机线性更新并收缩分离。沿精确在线递推（不采用扩散近似）累积楔形采样概率进行界估计。

Result: 群体梯度流在目标上以线性于T的时间恢复；而在线SGD在T≳log(b/η)后，在整个e^{c/η}阶时间范围内以高概率失效，且在Gaussian概率超过1%的显式初始化集上一致成立。对固定T，小步长SGD仍能恢复，故失效需小步长与长预训练的联合极限。在相同源梯度流检查点、e^{c/η}步内以固定概率恢复，需目标样本Nb≳e^{λT}且批量b≳ηe^{λT}（N为更新次数，λ为权重衰减）。模拟中恢复近似取决于不一致预算bδ/η并在时间范围内饱和。

Conclusion: 群体梯度流与有限批量SGD之间存在定性差异，根源在于楔形区（门不一致输入）采样概率的累积与权重衰减导致的指数收缩；恢复成本随预训练时间指数上升，且近似由bδ/η支配。

Abstract: Population gradient flow is a common tool for reasoning about how neural networks adapt, including after pretraining. We show that it can mispredict finite-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD) qualitatively, and we trace the discrepancy to a specific mechanism. In a two-unit ReLU regression, a source task drives the two neurons toward positive proportionality and a target task rewards separating them. After source training for time $T$, gradient flow recovers on the target in time linear in $T$. Online SGD with batch size $b$ and step size $η$ in both phases instead fails with high probability throughout a horizon of order $e^{c/η}$ once $T \gtrsim \log(b/η)$, uniformly on an explicit set of initializations with Gaussian probability above one percent. For each fixed $T$, small-step SGD still recovers, so the failure requires the joint limit of small steps and long pretraining. At the target clone, the population instability is carried entirely by inputs on which the two ReLU gates disagree. For units at angle $δ$ these inputs form a wedge of probability $δ/π$, and weight decay shrinks the angle exponentially during pretraining. On every other input both units receive the same random linear update, which contracts their separation in conditional expectation. Bounding the cumulative probability of sampling the wedge along the exact online recursion, without a diffusion approximation, shows that recovery with fixed probability from an identical source-gradient-flow checkpoint, within $e^{c/η}$ updates, requires $Nb \gtrsim e^{λT}$ target samples and batch size $b \gtrsim ηe^{λT}$, where $N$ counts updates and $λ$ is the weight decay. In simulations, recovery is approximately a function of the disagreement budget $bδ/η$ and saturates in the horizon.

</details>


### [133] [When to Intervene? State-Aware Sparse Manipulation in Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11523)
*Shutong Zheng,Sijia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出V-BSA，将联邦强化学习中"何时干预"识别为独立的攻击维度，利用局部策略不确定性选择稀疏干预状态并进行包络约束的行为引导，以极少干预量实现显著攻击效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦强化学习投毒攻击主要关注"如何构造恶意更新"，而轨迹级干预时机（何时干预）在很大程度上被隐含处理。由于序贯决策中干预位置会影响后续轨迹与学习信号，干预时机本身可能是影响攻击效力的独立因素。

Method: 通过控制实验验证：在固定恶意更新构造时，改变所选轨迹状态仍显著改变攻击效果，从而将"时机"确立为独立攻击维度。据此提出V-BSA（Viability-constrained Behavioral Steering Attack）：利用局部策略不确定性挑选稀疏干预状态，并施加包络约束的行为引导。

Result: 在离散动作基准上，V-BSA仅用密集投毒所需干预量的一小部分，即可对鲁棒聚合器和集成防御造成显著性能退化，同时揭示任务与聚合方式相关的攻击边界。

Conclusion: 干预时机是联邦强化学习中序贯鲁棒性的一个独立维度，为攻击与防御研究提供了新的分析视角。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning (FRL) enables distributed agents to collaboratively train decision-making policies, but its decentralized training process also exposes global policy learning to Byzantine manipulation. Existing poisoning attacks primarily focus on how to construct malicious updates, while trajectory-level intervention timing remains largely implicit. In sequential decision making, however, where an intervention is applied can alter subsequent trajectories and learning signals. Through controlled experiments, we find that changing the selected trajectory states materially alters attack efficacy even when the malicious-update construction is fixed. We therefore identify when as a distinct attack dimension and introduce the Viability-constrained Behavioral Steering Attack (V-BSA), which uses local policy uncertainty to select sparse intervention states and applies envelope-constrained behavioral steering. Across discrete-action benchmarks, V-BSA achieves substantial degradation against robust aggregators and ensemble defenses with only a fraction of the interventions used by dense poisoning, while revealing task- and aggregation-dependent boundaries. Overall, our results highlight intervention timing as a distinct dimension of sequential robustness in FRL. The code is available at https://github.com/Yodeesy/V-BSA

</details>


### [134] [Conditional Transfer from Controlled Pretraining Mixtures to Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11548)
*Ohad Rubin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究区分了合成任务的三种信号：诊断性、可教性和迁移性，发现可教性与下游迁移给出不同排名，且基于损失的自适应调度会把数据配比推向低迁移区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成任务既被用作语言模型能力探针，又被用作预训练数据，但两种用途常以损失下降作为依据，即把损失下降视为有信息量、把更多采样带来的更快损失下降视为任务值得采样的证据。作者试图厘清这一判据是否可靠。

Method: 在受控预训练中，70%语料固定为通用Python，其余30%为三类来源构成的单纯形：OpenCodeInstruct、含12个代码相邻合成任务的精选套件、以及15个源自文献的探针任务。通过任务预算扫描检测可教性，并在混合单纯形上比较下游迁移表现，同时用基于损失的自适应调度器进行三组60k步自由比例训练。

Result: 在27个任务中检测到14个具有可教性，两类合成家族存在明显不对称（精选10/12，文献衍生4/15）。可教性与下游迁移排序不同：在精选套件与OpenCodeInstruct的混合边缘上，固定微调后HumanEval pass@20从纯精选的15.9升到75% OpenCodeInstruct混合时的最高22.6，再降到纯OpenCodeInstruct的19.5。基于损失的Ado调度器在5k步内将OpenCodeInstruct占比压到5%以下、训练结束时降至1.2–1.4%，并比匹配的固定混合对照低2.4–11.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 精选合成数据具有条件价值：它作为混合中的有限份额有贡献，但目标对齐的来源仍应占主导。可教性与迁移性并不一致，基于损失的调度会优化近期任务损失下降，却使数据混合偏离真正带来迁移的区域。

Abstract: Synthetic tasks are increasingly used both as probes of language-model capability and as pretraining data. Both uses are often justified by loss reduction: falling loss is treated as informative, and faster loss reduction with more sampling as evidence that a task is worth sampling. We separate three signals. A task is diagnostic when its loss tracks global pretraining progress; it is teachable when its loss responds to its own token budget; and a data source transfers when including it improves a downstream target. We study controlled pretraining in which 70% of the corpus is fixed general Python and the remaining 30% is a simplex over three source families: OpenCodeInstruct, a curated suite of 12 code-adjacent synthetic tasks, and 15 literature-derived probe tasks. Across a task-budget sweep we detect teachability for 14 of 27 tasks, with a sharp asymmetry between the two synthetic families (10/12 curated versus 4/15 literature-derived).
  Teachability and downstream transfer give different rankings.
