We present the Marformer, a Transformer trained to directly predict conditional marginals given any set of observed values. Like BERT, which is trained to predict missing words from context, the Marformer constructs a hidden-vector representation for each distribution $p(X_i)$ and iteratively refines it through attention to other distributions $p(X_j)$. Unlike generative approaches, the Marformer does not model the full joint distribution, requires no domain knowledge of the data-generating process, and makes all predictions in a single forward pass. We evaluate across three synthetic domains with missing data---Bayesian networks, discretized multivariate Gaussians, and structured annotation data. The Marformer can match or outperform classical missing-data methods, even when those methods are given the true model family and prior that generated the synthetic data. We also evaluate on a real annotation dataset, where the Marformer outperforms the evaluated baselines at the largest training size. In both cases, the Marformer is substantially faster than the evaluated generative baselines.

</details>


### [207] [Long Text to Predictive Features: LLM-Guided Blockwise Feature Engineering via Executable Program Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12390)
*Ziming Dai,Dabiao Ma,Ziheng Guo,Jack Dong,Zimu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出LLM-BlockFE，一个LLM引导的离线特征构建框架，将长文本转化为可执行特征程序，避免在线推理时调用LLM，通过块级回滚和多轨迹交错搜索提升特征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业风控系统依赖结构化数据模型进行高效预测，但大量有价值信息嵌于非结构化长文本中。人工特征工程成本高，而在线逐条调用LLM难以满足部署要求，因此需要一种兼顾信息提取与在线效率的离线特征构建方案。

Method: 构造特征程序：增量追加不可变代码块；用下游模型评估候选特征。针对贪心搜索易陷入次优解，引入基于深度校准信用分配的块级回滚机制，并以交错方式推进多条独立搜索轨迹，通过共享各轨迹探索方向的固定描述减少冗余探索。搜索完成后冻结程序，部署用于提取结构化特征供下游预测模型使用。

Result: 在2个公开和2个私有数据集的全量对比中，相对最强基线获得0.0069至0.0358的绝对AUC提升；上线后对5个金融风控应用的监控显示，相对现有人工策略取得0.02至1.56个百分点的绝对KS提升。

Conclusion: LLM-BlockFE通过离线生成可执行特征程序，在避免在线LLM调用开销的同时有效利用长文本信息，实验与线上监控验证了其显著优于强基线和人工策略的效果。

Abstract: Industrial risk-control systems typically rely on structured-data models for efficient prediction, yet substantial valuable information remains embedded in unstructured long text. Extracting this information through manual feature engineering is labor-intensive, while requiring a large language model (LLM) to process every real-time input may not meet practical deployment requirements. To address this challenge, we propose LLM-BlockFE, an LLM-guided offline feature construction framework that converts long text into executable feature programs, thereby avoiding LLM calls during online inference. LLM-BlockFE constructs feature programs by incrementally appending immutable code blocks and evaluates candidate features using a downstream model. To address the tendency of conventional greedy search to become trapped in suboptimal solutions, our method introduces a block-level rollback mechanism based on depth-calibrated credit allocation and advances multiple independent search trajectories in an interleaved manner, reducing redundant exploration by sharing fixed descriptions of each trajectory's exploration direction. After the search, the resulting programs are frozen and deployed to extract structured features for downstream prediction models. Across two public and two private datasets, LLM-BlockFE achieves absolute AUC improvements of 0.0069 to 0.0358 over the strongest baseline on each dataset in the full-dataset comparison. Post-launch monitoring across five deployed financial risk-control applications shows absolute KS improvements of 0.02 to 1.56 percentage points over the existing manually designed strategy.

</details>


### [208] [Prospective Prediction of OOD Degradation from Source-Side Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12397)
*Sasha,Monin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文验证了仅用源侧训练动态即可在OOD退化被直接观测前预测其发生，属概念验证性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OOD性能退化通常在部署后才发现，若能在训练阶段仅凭源侧信息提前预警，可降低部署风险。

Method: 构建受控的捷径学习（shortcut learning）实验设置，用简单逻辑回归预测器基于源侧训练动态预测未来OOD退化；对比当前值与时间摘要（temporal summaries）的信息量，并将预测器从CNN迁移至MLP且不追加训练。

Result: 逻辑回归预测器产生明确的前瞻性信号，而仅用训练时间不行；源侧量的时间摘要显著优于其当前值；从CNN迁移到MLP后，置信度与熵动态仍保留大量预测信息。

Conclusion: 源侧训练动态包含未来OOD失败的早期预警信号，为OOD退化的事前预测提供了概念验证。

Abstract: We study whether persistent out-of-distribution (OOD) degradation can be predicted before it is directly observed using only source-side training dynamics. In a controlled shortcut-learning setting, a simple logistic regression predictor develops a clear prospective signal, while training time alone does not. Temporal summaries of the source-side quantities are substantially more informative than their current values. When transferred without additional training from a CNN to an MLP, confidence and entropy dynamics retain substantial predictive information. These results provide a proof of principle that source-side training dynamics can contain an early warning signal for future OOD failure.

</details>


### [209] [Learning Kilometer-Scale Weather Prediction with Global-Regional Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12401)
*Guowen Li,Yang Liu,Yujie Wang,Qiuyan Sun,Haoyuan Liang,Juepeng Zheng,Hong Cheng,Haohuan Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 ScaleCast 区域天气预报框架，通过"全球-区域对齐"机制复用预训练全球模型，实现公里级高分辨率区域预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公里级区域天气预报对本地预警和气象敏感决策至关重要。现有数据驱动方法多依赖数值预报提供大尺度引导，或需额外训练全球预报组件，成本高。预训练全球天气模型可高效提供大尺度预报，但其复用面临两大挑战：跨不同网格对齐全球与区域表征，以及将全球引导与局地相互作用融合以推进区域状态。

Method: 提出 ScaleCast 框架，核心为 Global-Regional Alignment。其中 Global-Regional Conversion 模块将联合的全球与区域表征对齐到区域位置；Global-Regional Alignment and Dynamics 模块将对齐后的引导信息与区域邻域相互作用相结合。

Result: 基于 ERA5 全球分析（0.25°）与 CERRA 区域再分析（5.5 km）实验，在地面及高空变量上均提升区域预报；单一训练模型可支持多种全球预报驱动（Pangu-Weather、GraphCast、HRES）而无需针对性重训。在 HRRR（3 km）上微调，验证了对不同区域域与空间分辨率的适应性。风暴案例显示气旋定位与核心气压估计改善；与 HadISD 站点观测对比，局地温湿变化更吻合。

Conclusion: ScaleCast 验证了复用预训练全球模型引导高分辨率区域预报的可行性，兼具跨驱动泛化性与跨分辨率适应性，为公里级区域预报提供高效方案。

Abstract: Kilometer-scale regional weather forecasting is essential for local weather warnings and weather-sensitive decisions. Existing data-driven approaches often rely on numerical forecasts for large-scale guidance or require additional training of global forecasting components. Pretrained global weather models offer an efficient source of large-scale forecasts, motivating their reuse to guide high-resolution regional prediction. However, this coupling requires aligning global and regional representations across different grids and integrating global guidance with local interactions to advance regional states. We propose ScaleCast, a regional forecasting framework that addresses these challenges through Global-Regional Alignment. Its Global-Regional Conversion module aligns joint global and regional representations with regional locations, while the Global-Regional Alignment and Dynamics block combines aligned guidance with regional neighborhood interactions. Experiments using ERA5 global analyses on a 0.25-degree grid and CERRA regional reanalysis at 5.5 km spacing demonstrate improved regional forecasts across surface and upper-air variables, with a single trained model supporting multiple global forecast drivers (i.e., Pangu-Weather, GraphCast, and HRES) without specific retraining. Fine-tuning on HRRR at 3 km spacing further demonstrates the framework's adaptability to a different regional domain and spatial resolution. Windstorm case studies show improved cyclone positioning and core-pressure estimates, while comparisons with HadISD station observations show closer agreement with local temperature and humidity changes.

</details>


### [210] [A Unified Bellman Operator for Safety-Critical Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12420)
*Nishanth Arun Rao,Royina Karegoudra Jayanth,Benjamin Eysenbach,Jaime Fernández Fisac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种联合贝尔曼算子，将性能与安全目标统一到单一值函数中，并用双时间尺度随机逼近证明其收敛性，从而在无需先验知识的情况下实现全程满足安全约束的最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键领域要求同时最大化任务性能并严格遵守安全约束。现有安全强化学习范式存在两难：一类依赖先验知识（如安全过滤器）以提供严格安全保证，另一类虽可联合学习但仅能在期望意义上满足安全约束，缺乏全过程的安全性保障。

Method: 提出一种新的联合贝尔曼算子，将性能目标与安全目标融合进一个联合值函数；采用双时间尺度随机逼近框架进行时序差分学习，在快时间尺度上估计当前联合策略的安全值，在慢时间尺度上估计联合值；通过将极限动力学刻画为占用平均微分包含来保证收敛。

Result: 理论上证明该联合贝尔曼算子的时序差分学习在双时间尺度框架下收敛，且极限动力学渐近收敛到一组受限最优安全任务值函数；收敛后的最优策略在始终保持安全的同时最大化任务回报。在连续控制任务上的神经近似实验显示收敛稳定，测试阶段安全违规接近零。

Conclusion: 该工作通过联合贝尔曼算子与双时间尺度分析，在无需先验安全知识的前提下兼顾了严格安全约束与性能优化，为安全强化学习提供了一种理论上可证明收敛且实践中安全违规近零的新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in safety-critical domains requires maximizing task performance while strictly adhering to safety constraints. Existing safe reinforcement learning paradigms typically force a trade-off: they either require a priori knowledge to provide strict safety guarantees (e.g., safety filters), or they enable joint learning but only satisfy safety constraints on average. In this work, we propose a novel Bellman operator that unifies performance and safety objectives into a joint value function. We show that temporal difference learning with the joint Bellman operator converges under a two-timescale stochastic approximation framework. On the fast timescale, the safety value of the learning joint policy is estimated, while the joint value is estimated on the slow timescale. Convergence is ensured by formulating the limiting dynamics as an occupation-averaged differential inclusion, and showing that it asymptotically converges to a set of limiting optimal safety-constrained task value functions. Theoretically, once converged, the resulting optimal policy maximizes task return while maintaining safety at all times. Empirical evaluations on continuous control tasks with neural approximations demonstrate stable convergence with near-zero safety violations at test time.

</details>


### [211] [Rounding in Preconditioner Space: Redesigning 4-bit AdamW Optimizer-State Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12444)
*Hanyang Li,Shao Tang,Daniel Thomas Braithwaite,Gregory Dexter,Leonardo Neves,Aman Gupta,Hiroto Udagawa,Abhishek Shivanna,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从"舍入空间"视角重新设计AdamW的4-bit优化器状态量化，提出ZIP-SR（含零、在预条件子空间随机舍入）与ZE-EDEN（排零码本+重缩放）两种方案，在130M–2.7B预训练中将验证损失差距最多缩小70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化AdamW优化器状态可降低持久化存储开销，但量化误差会沿一阶/二阶矩递推传播，扰动后续自适应更新。已有方法多在状态空间进行舍入，忽略了舍入坐标选择对预条件子误差的影响。

Method: 以"舍入空间"为核心：对二阶矩做零附近量化单元的局部分析，证明状态均值误差小并不意味着下一步预条件子均值误差小；并用一维二次型构造说明状态空间与预条件子空间舍入会导致定性不同的优化动力学。据此提出ZIP-SR：二阶矩码本保留零，随机舍入概率在预条件子空间计算；以及ZE-EDEN：使用排零二阶矩码本并对量化后的二阶矩块重缩放，以缓解正量化下限造成的预条件子畸变。两者一阶矩均采用4-bit NF4，并在训练最后10%对LM-head一阶矩做定向随机舍入。

Result: 在130M–2.7B参数的GPT与Llama风格预训练中，两种方法在全部评测规模上均缩小了TorchAO 4-bit AdamW相对32-bit AdamW的平均验证损失差距，最大缩小幅度达70%；全参数监督微调中两者验证损失低于TorchAO，且在下游任务上接近32-bit AdamW。

Conclusion: 舍入空间的选择是低比特优化器状态量化的关键设计维度；在预条件子空间设计二阶矩量化（含零保留或排零重缩放）能更有效抑制误差传播，在预训练与微调中同时兼顾显存节省与精度保持。

Abstract: Quantizing AdamW's optimizer states reduces persistent storage, but quantization errors propagate through the moment recurrences and perturb subsequent adaptive updates. We redesign 4-bit optimizer-state quantization for AdamW from the perspective of \emph{rounding space}: the coordinate in which a quantizer chooses between adjacent reconstruction levels. For the second moment, a local analysis of the quantization cell adjacent to zero shows that small mean state error need not imply small mean preconditioner error at the next step. A one-dimensional quadratic construction further shows qualitatively different optimization dynamics under state-space and preconditioner-space rounding. These results motivate Zero-Inclusive Preconditioner-space Stochastic Rounding (\textbf{ZIP-SR}), which retains zero in the second-moment codebook and computes stochastic-rounding probabilities in preconditioner space. As a complementary route, Zero-Excluding EDEN calibration (\textbf{ZE-EDEN}) uses a zero-excluding second-moment codebook and rescales the quantized second-moment block to mitigate the preconditioner distortion caused by the positive quantization floor. Both configurations use 4-bit NormalFloat (NF4) for the first moment, with targeted stochastic rounding of the LM-head first moment during the final 10\% of training. Across GPT- and Llama-style pretraining experiments ranging from \textbf{130M} to \textbf{2.7B} parameters, both methods reduce TorchAO 4-bit AdamW's mean validation-loss gap to 32-bit AdamW at every evaluated model size, with the largest reported gap reduction reaching \textbf{70\%}. In full-parameter supervised fine-tuning, both recipes achieve lower validation loss than TorchAO while remaining close to 32-bit AdamW on downstream tasks.

</details>


### [212] [Caught in the Act: Probes Effectively Detect Sabotage and Catch Unverbalized Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12445)
*Oskar J. Hollinsworth,Alex F. Spies,Tigist Diriba,Adam Gleave,Chris Cundy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出通过探针进行白盒欺骗检测的可扩展方案：构建迄今最大的欺骗数据集FIBS，并设计可跨多层与多token聚合信息的新型探针架构，在SHADE-Arena上达到98.8% AUC，并能检测"内省性欺骗"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体监控困难，且模型可能欺骗人类，现有监测手段不足，需要可扩展的白盒欺骗检测方法以支持前沿部署场景。

Method: 收集迄今最大的欺骗数据集（FIBS）用于训练探针；提出新型探针架构，能够在多个网络层与多个token间聚合信息；同时开展"内省性欺骗"评测，即仅凭上下文无法判定、需借助审慎诱导或训练数据知识才能确定真值的情形。

Result: 在SHADE-Arena上探针达到98.8% AUC，超越基于文本监控的基线；随底层模型规模扩大，探针效果提升；在内省性欺骗评测中区分含真实隐藏目标的对话记录与其他目标的AUC最高达99.7%；在多个开源权重模型上可检测其就话题及信念受压时的欺骗行为。

Conclusion: 白盒探针方法可扩展至前沿监控场景，并能应对上下文无法判定的欺骗情形；作者公开FIBS数据集，以推动探针的有效部署，并鼓励社区补充更多欺骗与破坏行为样本。

Abstract: Recent incidents have highlighted the challenge of monitoring LLM agents and the danger of models deceiving people. We show that white-box deception detection via probes can be scaled up to frontier monitoring settings by collecting the largest deception dataset to date for training probes and introducing a novel probe architecture which can aggregate information across many layers and tokens. Our probes achieve 98.8% AUC in SHADE-Arena, surpassing an Opus 5.5 text-monitoring baseline, and show improved efficacy as the underlying model is scaled up. To push our probes to their limit, we test them on several cases where deception cannot be determined from the context alone. In these cases, which we refer to as introspective deception, the ground truth can only be determined through careful elicitation or thorough knowledge of a model's training data. In one such evaluation, we show that probes can distinguish transcripts containing a model's true hidden goal from other goals with an AUC of up to 99.7%. Our probes also readily detect deception on prominent open-weight models which lie about politically sensitive topics, and about their beliefs when put under pressure. We release our training dataset, dubbed FIBS, to help drive frontier deployment of effective probes, and encourage the community to expand upon it with further examples of deception and sabotage.

</details>


### [213] [Bi-FORK: Generative Modeling of High-Dimensional Bifurcating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12449)
*Anna Zimmel,Fleur Hendriks,Markus Holzleitner,Florian Sestak,Martin Weichselbaumer,Vlado Menkovski,Johannes Brandstetter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Bi-FORK生成框架，用潜空间流匹配与排斥引导采样学习高维物理系统中的分岔一对多解映射，可一次性摊销生成完整解轨迹并恢复不同解分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分岔在物理系统中普遍存在，如结构屈曲、流体与气候动力学，但在深度学习中研究甚少。在对称破缺分岔处，单一输入对应多个同样有效的解，违背了多数学习型物理代理模型所依赖的一对一假设，因此需要能够刻画一对多解映射的方法。

Method: Bi-FORK是一个生成式框架：通过潜空间流匹配（latent flow matching）生成完整轨迹，以保持空间与时间连贯性；并采用排斥引导采样（repulsion-guided sampling），在一次摊销式前向传播中恢复不同的解分支。

Result: 在屈曲梁、力学超材料与Allen-Cahn相分离三类任务上评估，涵盖连续、离散与场值型分岔，离散化规模最高达260,000点。Bi-FORK成功恢复多模态解结构，且相比既有方法在规模上扩展了数个数量级。

Conclusion: Bi-FORK将生成式建模拓展至高维分岔物理系统，突破了传统代理模型一对一映射的局限，为学习分岔解映射提供了可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Bifurcations are ubiquitous in physical systems, from structural buckling to fluid and climate dynamics, yet they remain largely unexplored in deep learning. At a symmetry-breaking bifurcation, a single input admits multiple equally valid solutions, violating the one-to-one assumption underlying most learned physical surrogates. We introduce Bi-FORK, a generative framework for learning these one-to-many solution maps in high-dimensional systems. Bi-FORK generates complete trajectories through latent flow matching, preserving space and time coherence, and uses repulsion-guided sampling to recover distinct solution branches in a single amortized pass. We evaluate Bi-FORK on buckling beams, mechanical metamaterials, and Allen-Cahn phase separation, spanning continuous, discrete, and field-valued bifurcations with discretizations up to 260,000 points. Bi-FORK recovers the multimodal solution structure while scaling several orders of magnitude beyond prior approaches, opening generative modeling to high-dimensional bifurcating physical systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [LIFT: A Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer for Unified Retrieval and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10556)
*Keji Miao,Enhao Cheng,Yan Li,Qun Li,Qi Zhang,Jie Yuan,Xiaoyong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出LIFT模型，将交互分解为Request、Item、Context、Action四状态并建模为因果序列，使检索与排序在共享历史建模的同时保留阶段特定信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 级联推荐系统中检索与排序使用相同的用户历史，但两个阶段在不同交互时刻可获取的信息不同，这种信息错配限制了联合建模的效果。

Method: LIFT将每次交互分解为有序的Request、Item、Context、Action状态并作为因果序列建模；检索读取Request状态，排序读取Context状态；采用Role-Conditioned Attention与轻量级Pre-LN Bias实现该表示。

Result: 在ML-20M和Taobao数据集上，LIFT在所评估的联合模型中取得最高Joint Score，分别较最强基线提升4.9%和3.6%；损失权重扫描显示良好的检索—排序权衡，消融与扩展性分析进一步验证了生命周期序列构建、模型组件与容量设置。

Conclusion: 通过生命周期感知的交互状态分解，可在共享历史建模框架下为检索与排序提供阶段特定信息，从而提升级联推荐系统的整体性能。

Abstract: Cascaded recommender systems use the same user history for retrieval and ranking, while the two stages have access to different information at different points of an interaction. We propose the Lifecycle-aware Interaction Factorization Transformer (LIFT), which decomposes each interaction into ordered Request, Item, Context, and Action states and models them as a causal sequence. Retrieval reads the Request state, while ranking reads the Context state, allowing both tasks to share history modeling while preserving stage-specific information. LIFT instantiates this representation with Role-Conditioned Attention and a lightweight Pre-LN Bias. On ML-20M and Taobao, LIFT achieves the highest Joint Score among the evaluated joint models, improving over the strongest baselines by 4.9% and 3.6%, respectively. Loss-weight sweeps show favorable retrieval--ranking trade-offs, while ablations and scaling analyses further examine lifecycle sequence construction, model components, and capacity settings.

</details>


### [215] [Learning Multi-Step Query Rewriting via Corpus Feedback for Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10955)
*João Coelho,Hong Wang,Jie Yuan,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Wei Niu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文将对话查询改写（CQR）重新建模为序列化检索问题：智能体在多步交互中改写当前轮次、检索、并基于返回的段落条件化下一次改写，从而在检索证据可用后修正指代与词汇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CQR方法多为单步改写：在检索之前即根据对话历史固定改写结果，导致指代消解与词汇选择无法借助语料证据进行纠正。

Method: 将CQR建模为序列化检索任务；智能体在三种类型化改写操作（消解对话意图为独立查询、生成词汇重述、合成伪文档用于文档到文档匹配）与停止动作构成的动作空间中决策；采用监督微调加强化学习训练策略，仅使用单一检索质量奖励，无需人工改写标注。

Result: 在TopiOCQA与QReCC上优于多个检索对齐基线；在不同检索后端上均保持有效；无需额外训练即可泛化到CAsT基准。

Conclusion: 仅通过检索奖励优化，学习到的策略自发形成以早期步骤检索到的段落为依据生成伪文档的行为，显著提升检索效果，表明将改写与检索交错进行是更优的CQR范式。

Abstract: Conversational Query Rewriting (CQR) turns a context dependent user turn into a standalone query for a retriever, and most methods do this in a single step from the dialogue history before retrieving once. The rewrite is therefore fixed before any corpus evidence is available to correct its reference resolution or its vocabulary. We recast CQR as a sequential retrieval problem: an agent rewrites the current turn, retrieves, and conditions its next rewrite on the returned passages. The agent acts in a typed space of three rewriting operations, resolving conversational intent into a standalone query, generating lexical reformulations, or synthesizing pseudo-documents for document-to-document matching, together with a stop action that ends the episode. We train the policy with supervised fine-tuning followed by reinforcement learning against a single retrieval-quality reward, using no human rewrite annotations. Across TopiOCQA and QReCC, the agent outperforms several retrieval-aligned baselines, while remaining effective across retrieval backends and generalizing to the CAsT benchmarks without additional training. Further analysis shows that, through retrieval-reward optimization alone, the learned policy develops a behavior of grounding pseudo-documents in passages retrieved by earlier steps, substantially improving retrieval.

</details>


### [216] [H2CE: Modeling Geo-Semantic Interactions for POI Reranking with Heterogeneous Two-Stage Cross-Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11277)
*Zhengwei Bai,Moreno D'Incà,Danielle Class,Alessandro Moschitti*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 H2CE——面向本地搜索 POI 重排序的异构两阶段交叉编码器，在时延约束下融合语义、地理与数值信号，NDCG@5 达 67.48%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POI 重排序需建模查询条件下的多目标权衡：词法语义、地理邻近性与评分/评论数等数值质量信号之间存在冲突（近的未必满足意图，远的可能语义与质量证据更强）。现有方法难以在实时服务约束下同时保留数值量级信息与细粒度相对权衡，且全量两两比较成本高达 O(N²)。

Method: H2CE 采用异构两阶段交叉编码器架构。数值属性双通道表示：分桶后的自然语言描述子插入交叉编码器输入以支持语义—数值注意力，精确标量值经专用 MLP 保留量级信息；语义与数值嵌入通过隐空间聚合融合，实现超越标量加权和的非线性交互。两阶段排序：Stage 1 对全部候选项逐点打分以可扩展过滤，Stage 2 对 Top-K 候选项进行成对对比并用 Copeland 聚合，将成对开销从 O(N²) 降至 O(N+K(K-1))。

Result: 在 5,743 条查询的本地搜索测试集上，H2CE 取得 67.48% NDCG@5，较 XGBoost LTR 绝对提升 +22.82%，较零样本 LLM 重排序器提升 +35.89%；成对阶段较纯逐点模型再提升 +1.98% NDCG@5。消融实验验证了数值特征、隐空间聚合、Top-K 成对重排序与对齐训练的有效性。

Conclusion: H2CE 通过在交叉编码器中异构融合数值与语义信号，并结合逐点过滤与 Top-K 成对比较的两阶段设计，在时延约束下显著优于 GBDT LTR 与零样本 LLM 重排序，为实用化 POI 重排序提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Point-of-Interest (POI) reranking in local search must model query-conditioned tradeoffs among lexical semantics, geospatial proximity, and numerical quality signals such as rating and review count, while remaining practical under real-time serving constraints. A close POI may only partially satisfy the query intent, while a farther one may offer stronger semantic and quality evidence. We present H2CE, a Heterogeneous Two-stage Cross-Encoder for latency-bounded POI reranking. H2CE represents numerical attributes in two complementary ways: bucketized natural-language descriptors are inserted into the cross-encoder input to support semantic--numeric attention, while exact scalar values are processed by dedicated MLPs to preserve magnitude information. The resulting semantic and numerical embeddings are fused through latent-space aggregation, enabling nonlinear interactions beyond scalar weighted sums. H2CE then applies a two-stage architecture: Stage 1 scores all candidates pointwise for scalable filtering, and Stage 2 performs head-to-head pairwise comparison among the top-$K$ candidates with Copeland aggregation, making fine-grained relative tradeoffs explicit while reducing pairwise cost from O(N^2) to O(N+K(K-1)). On a 5,743-query local search test set, H2CE achieves 67.48% NDCG@5, improving over XGBoost LTR by +22.82% absolute and over a zero-shot LLM reranker by +35.89%. The pairwise stage adds +1.98% NDCG@5 over the pointwise model alone. Ablations confirm the value of numerical features, latent aggregation, top-K pairwise reranking, and aligned training.

</details>


### [217] [EVIE: Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11553)
*Zifei Wang,Wei Wen,Qiang Ji,Qian-Wen Zhang,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 EVIE 系列原生视觉文档检索器，通过证据引导的数据治理、双向师生学习与层次化索引压缩，在提升精度的同时大幅降低存储开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉文档检索需兼顾细粒度页面理解与高效索引，但现有方法难以两全：基于 OCR 的文本检索有预处理延迟且丢失视觉与结构线索；单向量视觉语言模型压缩整页导致匹配粒度不足；MaxSim 多向量检索精度更好但索引体积庞大且精度仍有提升空间。作者认为需在表示学习与索引构建全过程中保留与查询相关的页面证据。

Method: EVIE 包含三项创新：(1) 证据判定式数据治理，用多模态 judge 识别含答案的正样本并过滤不可靠负样本；(2) 双向师生学习，结合对称 listwise 蒸馏与基于前缀的 Matryoshka 表示学习（Prefix-MRL），使单个学生 checkpoint 支持六种嵌套嵌入维度而无需重编码；(3) 层次凝聚索引压缩（HAC），对页面 token 进行带空间正则的聚类并存储语义质心，实现单阶段 MaxSim 检索。

Result: 在 ViDoRe V1/V2/V3 与 JinaVDR 共 138 项任务上验证：EVIE-8B 在 V3 上达 66.75 nDCG@10，超最佳外部基线 1.43 分，四套件平均 79.51；EVIE-4.5B 结合 HAC 仅需每百万页 3.81 GiB 即保持 59.58 nDCG@10，向量载荷降低 128 倍。

Conclusion: EVIE 在精度—存储权衡上取得改进，验证了在表示学习与索引构建中保留查询相关证据的有效性，为可扩展的视觉文档检索提供了兼顾细粒度匹配与索引效率的方案。

Abstract: Accurate and scalable visual document retrieval (VDR) requires both fine-grained page understanding and efficient indexing, yet existing approaches struggle to achieve both. OCR-based text retrieval adds preprocessing latency and can lose visual and structural cues needed to understand complex pages. Single-vector vision-language models bypass OCR, but compressing an entire page into one vector limits the granularity of query--document matching. Multi-vector retrievers with MaxSim provide finer interactions, yet demand large indexes and still leave room for accuracy improvements. We argue that overcoming these limitations requires preserving query-relevant page evidence throughout representation learning and index construction. To this end, we introduce \textbf{\textit{EVIE}} (Evidence-Vector-Informed Embeddings), a family of native visual document retrievers integrating three key innovations: (1) Evidence-judged data governance, which uses a multimodal judge to identify answer-bearing positives and filter unreliable negatives. (2) Bidirectional teacher--student learning with symmetric listwise distillation and prefix-based Matryoshka representation learning (Prefix-MRL), enabling one student checkpoint to serve six nested embedding dimensions without re-encoding. (3) Hierarchical agglomerative index compression (HAC), which clusters page tokens with spatial regularization and stores semantic centroids for single-stage MaxSim retrieval. Extensive experiments across 138 tasks from ViDoRe V1, V2, V3, and JinaVDR validate EVIE. EVIE-8B achieves 66.75 nDCG@10 on V3, exceeding the best external baseline by 1.43 points, with a four-suite average of 79.51. EVIE-4.5B with HAC retains 59.58 nDCG@10 at only 3.81 GiB per million pages, reducing vector payload by $128\times$. Together, these results improve the accuracy--storage trade-off for visual document retrieval.

</details>


### [218] [Overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11598)
*Junjie Chen,Yuxi Dong,Haitao Li,Yiqun Liu,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文概述了NTCIR-19 AEOLLM-2任务，在NTCIR-18 AEOLLM基础上新增"深度研究评估"子任务，聚焦LLM长文本生成（深度研究报告）的自动评估，共收到10支团队91个run，通过与人标注金标准对比衡量评估方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLM）在长文本生成场景中的广泛应用，如何自动评估其生成内容的质量成为关键问题。NTCIR-18的AEOLLM核心任务已取得成功，为进一步探究LLM自动评估方法，尤其是长文本生成场景下的评估，作者在NTCIR-19提出AEOLLM-2任务。

Method: 在AEOLLM-2中引入新子任务"Deep Research Evaluation"，针对LLM生成的长篇深度研究报告进行自动评估。参赛者开发评估方法自动评判这些报告的质量，各方法性能通过将其评分与人工标注的真值标签进行对比来衡量。

Result: 本年度共收到来自10支团队的91个run。论文描述了任务背景、数据集构建方式、评估指标、参赛者方法及最终评估结果。

Conclusion: AEOLLM-2成功延续并拓展了LLM自动评估的研究，新增的深度研究评估子任务为长文本生成场景下的自动评估提供了新的基准与研究方向。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide an overview of the NTCIR-19 Automatic Evaluation of LLMs 2 (AEOLLM-2) task. Building on the success of the NTCIR-18 core task AEOLLM, we proposed AEOLLM-2 for NTCIR-19 to further investigate automatic evaluation methods for Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in long-form text generation scenarios. In AEOLLM-2, we introduced a new subtask, Deep Research Evaluation, which focuses on the automatic evaluation of long-form deep research reports generated by LLMs. Participants developed evaluation methods to automatically assess the quality of these reports, and the performance of each method was measured by comparing its scores against human-annotated ground-truth labels. This year, we received 91 runs from 10 teams in total. This paper describes the background of the task, the dataset construction, the evaluation measures, the participants' methods, and the final evaluation results.

</details>


### [219] [SkillContrast: Difference-Guided Text Selection for Agent Skill Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11650)
*Jiandong Ding,Honglei Ji,Ming Liu,Tao Duan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SkillContrast 是一种免训练的技能文本选择器，通过对比检索到的相似技能并保留其差异文本及局部上下文，为预训练重排器提供紧凑输入，在相同长度条件下优于 TF-IDF 选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相似智能体技能往往共享指令，但在使用条件上存在差异；基于查询的文本选择可能保留共享指令，却遗漏这些关键区别。

Method: 提出 SkillContrast：免训练选择器，对比检索到的技能，保留差异文本及局部上下文，供预训练重排器使用；并通过长度匹配的组件替换分析各成分贡献。

Result: 在 SameCapRisk-Bench 的 1,235 条请求上，相比 TF-IDF 查询选择，在相同单候选输入长度下多产生 54–72 个 clean hits，覆盖 2 种检索器与 2 种重排器规模。长度匹配的组件替换表明差异文本是主要贡献因素，上下文影响较小且方向不一。相比完整技能正文，SkillContrast 减少 51.1–58.8% 的模型输入 token；在 0.6B 模型下少 10–18 个 clean hits，在 4B 模型下 clean hits 持平或更高。

Conclusion: 候选间相对差异可补充查询相关性，用于选择紧凑的重排输入；差异文本是主要增益来源，且在大模型上能兼顾效率与效果。

Abstract: Similar agent skills can share instructions but differ in their conditions of use. Query-based text selection may retain shared instructions and omit these distinctions. We introduce SkillContrast, a training-free selector that compares retrieved skills and retains their differing text with local context for a pretrained reranker. On 1,235 requests from SameCapRisk-Bench, it yields 54-72 more clean hits (requests that retrieve a helpful skill without its marked risky sibling) than TF-IDF query selection at identical per-candidate input lengths, across 2 retrievers and 2 reranker sizes. Length-matched component replacements identify differing text as the main contributor in the primary setting, with smaller, mixed context effects. Relative to full skill bodies, SkillContrast uses 51.1-58.8% fewer model-input tokens, with 10-18 fewer clean hits at 0.6B and matching or higher observed clean-hit counts at 4B. Candidate-relative differences thus complement query relevance in selecting compact reranking inputs.

</details>


### [220] [Autoregressive Retriever: Improving Query Understanding from Item Feedback for Universal Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11666)
*Jianfei Zhao,Yifan Wang,Feng Zhang,Xin Sun,Chong Feng,Zhixing Tan,Yang Luo,Boyuan Pan,Xu Kai,Yao Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AutoRegressive Retriever（ARR），一种在检索过程中迭代利用反馈item内容更新query表示的多模态检索模型，结合SFT与RL训练，在域内和零样本基准上取得更优的平均检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统通用多模态检索将query编码一次后即固定，按embedding相似度对独立索引item排序。这种设计虽高效，但即使检索到的item有助于澄清信息需求，query表示也不会改变，限制了检索精度的进一步提升。

Method: ARR在检索item与更新query embedding之间交替进行，最终用更新后的embedding对集合排序。训练分两阶段：监督微调通过逐步对比监督使编码器学会利用反馈；强化学习将反馈item视为动作，以相关item的最终倒数排名（reciprocal rank）为奖励优化其选择。采用query-side adapter，使优化可在固定item索引上进行。

Result: ARR在域内与零样本基准上均展现强检索性能，平均优于对比基线。进一步分析表明：推理时引入反馈可提升检索效果；使用反馈进行训练也能改善初始query embedding，即在未观察到任何item之前即已受益。

Conclusion: ARR验证了迭代式反馈驱动query更新在多模态检索中的有效性，SFT与RL相结合、配合query-side adapter的设计，可在不重建item索引的前提下提升检索性能，且反馈训练对初始query表示亦具有正向迁移作用。

Abstract: Universal multimodal retrieval typically encodes a query once and ranks independently indexed items by embedding similarity. This design supports efficient search, but leaves the query representation unchanged even when retrieved items could help clarify the information need. We introduce the AutoRegressive Retriever (ARR), a multimodal retrieval model that learns both to select informative items and to use their content to refine subsequent retrieval. ARR alternates between retrieving an item and updating the query embedding, then uses the final embedding to rank the collection. Supervised fine-tuning teaches the encoder to use feedback through stepwise contrastive supervision. Reinforcement learning treats feedback items as actions and optimizes their selection using the final reciprocal rank of a relevant item. A query-side adapter enables this optimization against a fixed item index. ARR demonstrates strong retrieval performance on both in-domain and zero-shot benchmarks, outperforming the compared baselines on average. Further analyses show that feedback improves retrieval at inference time and that training with feedback also improves the initial query embedding, before any item is observed.

</details>


### [221] [Chaos in the Text: Revealing the Modality Preference in Mixed-Modality Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11816)
*Yubo Sun,Chunyi Peng,Yukun Yan,Zhenghao Liu,Zhipeng Xu,Sen Mei,Linlin Xin,Zheni Zeng,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文系统考察了稠密检索器在混合模态语料上的表现，发现性能对模态组成高度敏感（呈V型曲线），并提出Trident方法通过构建文本、图像、融合视图的等权正样本及Multi-Positive View InfoNCE来缓解模态偏好偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索在纯文本和纯图像语料上已取得显著进展，但其能力能否可靠迁移到包含文本、图像及融合文档的混合语料尚不明确。作者发现检索器性能对模态组成高度敏感，且存在"文本混沌"与模态偏好问题，需要针对性方法加以缓解。

Method: 作者跨架构系统评估检索器在混合语料上的表现；随后提出Trident，为每个文档构建文本、图像、融合三种等权正样本视图，并通过Multi-Positive View InfoNCE联合优化相关性判别与正视图平衡，从而抑制文本表示获得系统性更高相似度的模态偏好偏差。

Result: 实验显示，随着图像文档被语义对应的文本表示逐步替换，检索性能呈明显V型曲线：单模态语料表现强，模态共存时显著退化；无关文本比等量无关图像造成更严重退化（即"文本混沌"，Chaos in the Text）；文本表示获得系统性更高相似度，使无关文本可超越相关图像。在视觉文档与自然图像基准上，Trident在CLIP与VLM架构上均提升混合模态检索性能，降低对模态组成和文本干扰的敏感性，并提升单模态平均检索表现。

Conclusion: 检索器对模态组成敏感且存在文本偏好偏差，Trident通过等权多视图正样本与Multi-Positive View InfoNCE有效缓解该问题，在CLIP和VLM架构上同时改善混合模态与单模态检索，具备跨架构的通用性。

Abstract: Dense retrievers have made significant progress on text and image corpora, but whether these capabilities extend reliably to mixed corpora containing text, image, and fused text-image documents remains unclear. In this paper, we systematically examine retrievers across architectures and find that their performance is highly sensitive to modality composition. As image documents are progressively replaced with semantically corresponding text representations, retrieval performance follows a pronounced V-shaped curve, remaining strong on single-modality corpora but degrading substantially when modalities coexist. In particular, irrelevant text causes more severe degradation than an equal number of irrelevant images, a phenomenon we term Chaos in the Text. Further analysis reveals modality preference, whereby text representations receive systematically higher similarity scores, allowing irrelevant text to outrank relevant images. To mitigate this bias, we introduce Trident, which constructs text, image, and fused text-image views of each document as co-equal positives and jointly optimizes relevance discrimination and positive-view balance through Multi-Positive View InfoNCE. Experiments across visual document and natural image benchmarks show that trident improves mixed-modality retrieval on both CLIP-based and VLM-based architectures, reduces sensitivity to modality composition and text distractors, and increases average single-modality retrieval performance.

</details>


### [222] [Project Greenhouse: Progress Toward Fully Open and Sovereign Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11922)
*Jimmy Lin,Sahel Sharifymoghaddam,Lingwei Gu,Nour Jedidi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Project Greenhouse 探索用有限算力构建完全开放、自主的 agentic search 模型，首个里程碑为从零预训练加监督微调构建有竞争力的 pointwise decoder-only 重排序器，仅用少量 GPU 完成，并公开数据、代码、配置及 Gaggle 系列模型检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流方法依赖第三方开放权重骨干模型，缺乏对训练全流程的掌控；作者希望验证仅凭常见公开数据集和有限算力也能构建完全开放、自主可控的 agentic search 模型。

Method: 采用两步配方：从零预训练，随后进行监督微调，构建 pointwise decoder-only 重排序器；不使用任何第三方开放权重骨干，全程端到端自主训练，仅用少量 GPU。

Result: 成功构建了具有竞争力的 pointwise 重排序器，大部分实验仅用不到数块 GPU 完成；发布数据、代码、配置以及 Gaggle 系列模型检查点，支持透明、独立复现。

Conclusion: 证明了以有限算力构建完全开放、自主 agentic search 模型的可行性，为验证更广泛论点迈出第一步。

Abstract: Project Greenhouse represents our exploration of a simple thesis: We believe that it is possible to build fully open and sovereign models for agentic search with only modest computational resources. As a first milestone, we describe how to build a competitive pointwise decoder-only reranker using a simple two-step recipe comprising pre-training from scratch followed by supervised fine-tuning, starting only from commonly available datasets. Contrary to the dominant approach in the literature, we do not rely on existing open-weight backbones from third parties, and thus we are fully in control of model training, from end to end. We were able to accomplish the bulk of our experiments using no more than a handful of GPUs. This report articulates the importance and benefits of our approach, and we share artifacts that enable transparent, independent reproduction of all aspects of model training. Beyond data, code, and configurations that capture our efforts, we also release checkpoints for our family of Gaggle models, demonstrating the feasibility of our approach and providing a first step toward validating our broader thesis.

</details>


### [223] [NativeScope: Relation-Localized Retrieval over Native Topology with a Correct Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12243)
*Long Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 NativeScope 的"先定界后排序"检索方法，利用数据结构原生关系提升召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密检索仅按语义相似度对文本块排序，忽略了数据系统已存储的结构信息（章节归属、会话边界、原生顺序），导致定位精度受限。

Method: 将查询表示为 q -> (A, r, B)：锚点 A 与关系 r 通过 belonging/before/after 算子选出原生单元，目标词 B 仅对与所选范围重叠的文本块排序。内部变体 NS-FullQ 用完整问题对同一候选集排序。在 QASPER 与 LongMemEval 衍生的 200 条受控文档与记忆记录、1024 token 预算下评测。

Result: NativeScope 原生单元召回率为文档 89.28%、记忆 72.50%，较实例级 Dense RAG 分别提升 42.75 与 22.00 个百分点。NS-FullQ 为 87.78% 与 68.50%，与 NativeScope 差异不显著，说明主要增益来自关系定界而非更短的排序查询。采用自动 Top-1 锚点时记忆召回降至 35.50%。

Conclusion: 当锚点坐标与原生关系可靠时 NativeScope 有效；但硬定界会继承定位接口的错误。

Abstract: Dense retrieval usually ranks text chunks by their semantic similarity to a question. This ignores structure that many data systems already store, including section membership, session boundaries, and native order. We propose NativeScope, a scope-then-rank method for queries with a known anchor and relation. It represents a query as q -> (A, r, B). The anchor A and relation r select native units through belonging, before, or after operators, and the target term B ranks only chunks that overlap the selected scope. An internal variant, NS-FullQ, ranks the same candidates with the full question. We evaluate both methods on 200 controlled document and memory records derived from QASPER and LongMemEval under a 1,024-token budget. NativeScope attains native-unit recall of 89.28 percent for documents and 72.50 percent for memories, improving over instance-wide Dense RAG by 42.75 and 22.00 percentage points. NS-FullQ reaches 87.78 percent and 68.50 percent; its differences from NativeScope are inconclusive, locating the primary gain in relational scoping rather than the shorter ranking query. With automatic Top-1 anchors, memory recall falls to 35.50 percent. NativeScope is therefore effective when anchor coordinates and native relations are reliable, but hard scoping inherits errors from the localization interface.

</details>


### [224] [Compact and Efficient Indexes for Learned Sparse Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12300)
*Franco Maria Nardini,Luca Rizzo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文在SEISMIC基础上，通过medoid替换倒排索引块摘要、重排词表并采用DOTPACKING8位打包与4-bit量化码本压缩前向索引，并引入JUMPDOT分块点积内核，在MS MARCO上显著改善学习稀疏检索的速度-空间权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习稀疏检索索引内存占用大，现有SOTA检索数据结构虽高效但内存代价高，需要在几乎不牺牲检索效率的前提下大幅降低内存。

Method: 分两级优化：倒排索引用现成文档作为块代表（medoid）替代稀疏向量块摘要，将块元数据压缩为单一文档ID；前向索引对分量和取值均压缩——重排词表使共现分量相邻，用SIMD友好的DOTPACKING8对Δ-gap做位打包并融合解压与点积计算，取值用按分量分布拟合的紧凑4-bit码本量化；另提出针对少量非零项查询的JUMPDOT分块点积内核。前向索引压缩独立于SEISMIC，可接入KANNOLO等任意基于前向索引评分的系统。

Result: 在MS MARCO上用三种SOTA学习稀疏编码器评估：同等精度下索引查询速度最高快5.3倍且内存约少3倍；内存最受限场景下仍快最多1.9倍且内存少最多3.9倍。

Conclusion: 通过倒排与前向索引的协同压缩及专用点积内核，可在保持精度的同时显著提升学习稀疏检索的速度-空间权衡，且前向压缩方案具备通用可插拔性。

Abstract: This paper investigates how to substantially reduce the memory footprint of learned sparse retrieval indexes without sacrificing the efficiency of state-of-the-art retrieval data structures. Building on SEISMIC, we revisit both levels of its design: the inverted index used to select candidates and the forward index used to score them. For the inverted index, we replace costly per-block summaries with medoids, namely existing documents elected as block representatives, collapsing the per-block metadata from a sparse vector to a single document identifier. For the forward index, we compress both components and values. We reorder the vocabulary to place co-occurring components closer together and encode the resulting $Δ$-gaps with DOTPACKING8, a SIMD-friendly bit-packing scheme that fuses decompression with dot-product evaluation; values are quantized with compact per-component 4-bit codebooks fitted to each component's distribution. We further introduce JUMPDOT, a blocked dot-product kernel tailored for queries that contain only a few non-zero entries. Our forward-index compression is independent of SEISMIC and can be plugged into any system relying on forward-index-based scoring, as we demonstrate by integrating it into KANNOLO. A comprehensive evaluation on MS MARCO with three state-of-the-art learned sparse encoders shows that our solutions markedly improve the speed-space trade-off of learned sparse retrieval: at equal accuracy, our indexes answer queries up to 5.3x faster than the best competitor while using about 3x less memory, and in the most memory-constrained regime, they remain up to 1.9x faster while using up to 3.9x less memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [225] [An Explainable Header-Centric Framework for Large-Scale Semantic Table Interpretation and Data Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10541)
*Marcelo Valentim Silva,Hannes Herrmann,Valerie Maxville*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种可解释、以表头为中心的框架，在仅依赖元数据（无单元格值）条件下完成列类型标注（CTA）与数据质量评估（DQA），并将检测结果聚合为数据源级质量指标 HeadersIQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱质量不仅取决于下游图验证，还取决于集成前表格元数据的质量。在"仅元数据"的语义表格解释场景中，单元格值缺失、噪声大或不适用，列标题因而成为可追溯知识图谱准备的关键语义证据来源。

Method: 框架以表头为中心：借助人工整理的词汇资源将表头映射到 39 个可解释的 FinalFormat 类型，并通过 SourceKeywords 保留词元级可追溯性；每个被赋予的类型激活基于数据质量问题（DQI）分类体系的校验规则，产出缺失数据、重复、领域违规、错误数据类型、时间不匹配等检测；检测结果聚合为轻量、无权重的数据源级质量指标 HeadersIQ；另设并行的知识图谱映射通路，用于对齐 DBpedia 与 Schema.org。

Result: 在 UCI、Prague、Kaggle、VizNet/Sato、SOTAB、T2Dv2 及 SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG 赛道等异构基准上评测，覆盖约 12 万个表头列，显示对噪声真实元数据具有广泛实用覆盖。在 SemTab 2024 赛道上官方 GT-strict 评测结果一般；但一项盲测诊断审计表明，许多不匹配源自基准粒度、别名与本体选择效应，而非完全不合理的表头中心预测。该审计作为关于分歧模式的诊断证据报告，而非修正后的基准性能。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一套可复用的工作流，用于元数据驱动的语义标注、数据源级质量监控与面向知识图谱的基准诊断。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph (KG) quality depends not only on downstream graph validation, but also on the quality of tabular metadata used before integration. In metadata-only Semantic Table Interpretation (STI), where cell values are unavailable, noisy, or unsuitable, column headers become a critical source of semantic evidence for traceable KG preparation.
  We present an explainable, header-centric framework for metadata-only Column Type Annotation (CTA) and Data Quality Assessment (DQA). The framework maps headers to 39 interpretable FinalFormat types using curated lexical resources and preserves token-level traceability through SourceKeywords. Each assigned type activates validation rules based on a taxonomy of Data Quality Issues (DQIs), producing detections such as missing data, duplicates, domain violations, wrong data type, and temporal mismatch. These detections are aggregated into HeadersIQ, a lightweight, unweighted data source-level quality metric.
  The framework was evaluated across heterogeneous benchmarks, including UCI, Prague, Kaggle, VizNet/Sato, SOTAB, T2Dv2, and the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, comprising around 120,000 header columns. The results show broad practical coverage across noisy real-world metadata, while a parallel KG-mapping pathway supports alignment to DBpedia and Schema.org. On the SemTab 2024 Metadata-to-KG track, the official GT-strict evaluation was modest. However, a blinded diagnostic audit indicates that many mismatches reflect benchmark granularity, aliasing, and ontology-selection effects rather than wholly implausible header-centric predictions. We report this audit as diagnostic evidence on disagreement patterns, not as revised benchmark performance. Overall, the paper presents a reusable workflow for metadata-driven semantic annotation, data source-level quality monitoring, and KG-oriented benchmark diagnosis.

</details>


### [226] [Synthesis Through Simulation: Generating Coherent Enterprise Data via Scalable Agent-System Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10549)
*Yipeng Li,Ashutosh Hathidara,Jane Lo,Harshavardhan Abichandani,Gunraj Singh,Atin Ghosh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"通过模拟合成"(STS)范式的免schema数据合成方法，通过LLM智能体在模拟企业环境中调用策略API生成数据，以Generalist Populator实现高保真、全约束满足的合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级工具调用智能体的训练与评估因业务和法律限制（对企业系统、数据、数据库schema的访问受限）而严重受阻。表格数据合成受限于结构有效性与schema可得性，而基于流程的方法则缺乏分布保真度，且需逐域编写。

Method: 提出Synthesis Through Simulation（STS）免schema数据合成范式：LLM智能体在模拟企业环境中对策略强制API执行操作以生成数据，因数据通过定义有效性的同一环境生成，故结构有效性由构造保证，同时将有效性强制与分布建模解耦。其领域无关智能体Generalist Populator（GP）解决分布保真度与合成可扩展性挑战。

Result: 在全部十个环境中，GP在无DB schema访问权限下达到平均边际保真度0.88与100%约束满足率；统计合成器因必需种子数据要求而无法适用于其中七个；具有schema特权的智能体在airline环境的紧耦合工作流中因任务组合脆弱而82%轨迹失败。

Conclusion: STS通过模拟环境实现免schema、结构有效的企业数据合成，兼顾分布保真度与可扩展性，且已开源完整框架、十个环境及生成数据集。

Abstract: Tool-calling agents have become central to enterprise AI, yet training and evaluating them at scale remains severely constrained due to business and legal restrictions on enterprise systems, data, and database schemas. Tabular data synthesis offers a natural alternative, but its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by structural validity and schema availability, while procedure-based approaches yield the opposite weakness, typically lacking distributional fidelity without per-domain authoring. We introduce **Synthesis Through Simulation** (STS), a **schema--free** data synthesis paradigm in which an LLM agent generates data by executing operations against policy-enforcing APIs within simulated enterprise environments. Because data is generated through the same environment that defines what is valid, STS guarantees structural validity by construction while decoupling validity enforcement from distribution modeling, allowing each to be addressed independently. The **Generalist Populator** (GP), STS's domain-agnostic agent, addresses the remaining challenges of distributional fidelity and synthesis scalability: GP achieves **0.88** average marginal fidelity and **100\% constraint satisfaction** across all ten environments *without access to DB schemas*, while statistical synthesizers are inapplicable to seven due to necessary seed data requirements, and schema-privileged agents fail 82\% of trajectories on airline environment's tightly coupled workflows due to brittle task composition. We open-source the full framework, all ten environments, and generated datasets at https://github.com/SAP/synthesis-through-simulation.

</details>


### [227] [Agent-Controlled Forgetting for Tool-Using Agents: Reversible Context Curation in Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10590)
*Jan-Peter Franke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"智能体控制的遗忘"机制：模型自主选取已观测的工具结果，用简短摘要原位替换，并将原始内容存入可恢复档案；通过Python工具支持批量归档与显式恢复，且不修改用户指令与助手消息。在OpenTelemetry调试案例中显著降低上下文与成本，但存在工作负载依赖性与额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型智能体的历史观测中，有效信息密度远低于原始载荷，长轨迹导致上下文膨胀、token消耗与API成本上升。研究目标是实现可逆的上下文管理，在保留必要信息的同时压缩噪声型工具结果。

Method: 由动作模型在已有观测中选择工具结果，将其原位替换为简短注释，并把原始内容保存至可恢复档案；采用Python harness实现批量归档与显式恢复，无需任务特定训练；同时对用户指令与助手消息进行保护，禁止上述操作。

Result: 在OpenTelemetry调试加无关实现任务的探索性案例中，最终提示token为231,951，对比保留完整历史的912,492；累计输入token减少50%；估算API成本约USD 1.28–1.44，对比约USD 4.38。两臂均通过两案例主行为判别；均未完全满足后续评估。该方法请求次数更多、耗时增加17%。在另一组应用开发任务中未带来上下文或成本节省；更早的续写虽降低上下文，但人工评估质量更差。

Conclusion: 可逆上下文管理在噪声较大的工具使用轨迹中可实现显著资源节省，但其收益高度依赖工作负载类型；需权衡额外请求、时延与质量风险，工作负载依赖性是该方法的核心考量。

Abstract: Tool-using agents repeatedly carry observations whose useful content can be much smaller than their original payload. We study agent-controlled forgetting: the acting model selects previously observed tool results, replaces each with a short note at its original position, and retains the exact original in a recoverable archive. A Python harness exposes batch archival and explicit recovery without task-specific model training, while protecting user instructions and assistant messages from these operations. In an exploratory OpenTelemetry debugging case followed by an unrelated implementation task, the method ended with 231,951 provider-reported prompt tokens versus 912,492 under retained history, used 50% fewer cumulative input tokens, and had an estimated API cost of USD 1.28-1.44 versus approximately USD 4.38. Both arms passed the two-case primary behavioral oracle; neither fully satisfied the follow-up evaluation. The method made more requests and took 17% longer. A contrasting application-development pair produced no context or cost saving, and an earlier continuation exhibited lower manually assessed quality despite reduced context. These observations demonstrate substantial resource savings in noisy tool-use trajectories and identify workload dependence as a central consideration for reversible context management.

</details>


### [228] [Verification and Self-Improvement in Agentic AI: Foundations and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10611)
*Chien-Ping Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的"有界验证"理论框架，用以区分Agenic AI系统性能提升背后的不同机制（更长搜索、额外支持、改变提议与验证方式），并将自我改进主张与正确性、可采纳证据、验证资源和选择误差等义务绑定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一的性能分数无法区分系统改进来自哪里：是搜索更久、获得更多支持，还是修改了输出提议与验证机制。需要一种能刻画这些不同机制的框架。

Method: 引入"阶段"（stage）概念，规定可采纳的转录、多项式界、交替验证协议与终端检查器；定义原生可达（默认支持）与闭包前沿（接口已允许的全部支持）；在一致逐点概率间隙与任务相对可靠性下定义语言；证明独立性多数放大保持语言，而随机带上的存在性接受可引入错误输出；分析精确验证（零随机）情形及其放置与完备性；刻画随机验证类Σ_k^RV的层级关系；进行表示分析以区分不变接受与核心-支持标签；研究递归自我改进下均匀有界自修改的封闭性；引入条件误差预算控制自适应候选的误选；构造配额强制XOR合成族以分离搜索成功与接受语言的变化。

Result: 证明独立性多数放大同时保持原生可达与闭包前沿语言；存在性接受可能接纳错误输出；随机验证类满足Σ_k^P ⊆ Σ_k^RV ⊆ Σ_{k+1}^P；严格扩张与深度分离需显式复杂度假设；当BPP=P时得到具有相同前沿的精确伴随类；表示分析可将不变接受与可能在重构下改变的核心-支持标签分离；在共同可靠解释器与固定验证协议下，均匀有界自我修改仍处于同一验证类；配额强制XOR合成族可实现搜索成功无界比例而接受语言不变。

Conclusion: 该框架将自我改进主张转化为对正确性、可采纳证据、验证资源和选择误差的显式义务，为评估Agentic AI的改进机制提供了比单一性能分数更精细的理论工具。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems can improve by searching longer, receiving additional support, or modifying how they propose and verify outputs. A performance score does not distinguish these mechanisms. We compare these changes through bounded verification with hidden terminal randomness. A stage specifies admissible transcripts, polynomial bounds, an alternating verification protocol, and a terminal checker. Its native reach uses default support; its closure frontier permits all support already admitted by the interface. Under a uniform pointwise probability gap and task-relative soundness, these are well-defined languages. We prove that independent majority amplification preserves both languages, whereas existential acceptance over random tapes can admit incorrect outputs. Exact verification is the zero-randomness case, with placement and completeness results. The randomized-verifier classes satisfy $Σ_k^{\mathrm{P}}\subseteqΣ_k^{\mathrm{RV}}\subseteqΣ_{k+1}^{\mathrm{P}}$; strict enlargement and depth separation require explicit complexity assumptions, while $\mathrm{BPP}=\mathrm{P}$ yields exact companions with the same frontiers. Representation analysis separates invariant acceptance from core-versus-support labels that can change under refactoring. For recursive self-improvement, uniformly bounded self-modification under a common sound interpreter and fixed verification protocol remains within the same verification class. A separate conditional-error budget controls false selection across adaptively chosen candidates. A quota-enforced XOR-synthesis family separates unbounded ratios of search success from changes in the accepted languages; exact and probabilistic audits check the resulting evidence requirements. The framework ties self-improvement claims to obligations on correctness, admissible evidence, verification resources, and selection error.

</details>


### [229] [The Harness as the Only Mutable Surface: Compliance-Bounded Self-Evolution of LLM Agents in Credit Pipelines, with a Measured Admission Gate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10629)
*Ravil Akhtyamov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出：LLM智能体的"自我进化"只有被限制在运行时harness（指令文本、工具调用逻辑、原语组合）而冻结模型权重时，才具备可审查性——每次自适应都是一份带原因和测试的diff。基于此边界构建双环引擎与单一准入闸门，在部署前写入哈希链记录，并在仿真中（模拟智能体+种子搜索提议器，非语言模型）评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我改进的LLM智能体虽能适应规则变化，但一个重写自身的智能体破坏了监督者所审查的产物：具名变更、记录的测试、审批。因此需要论证并界定何种自我演化是可审查的。

Method: 双环引擎（dual-loop engine），受"演化限于运行时harness、权重固定"的约束；设单一准入闸门（admission gate），在部署前写入哈希链（hash-chained）记录。评估为仿真实验：使用模拟智能体与种子搜索（seeded-search）提议器而非真实语言模型；覆盖三类监督性重新解释（supervisory re-interpretation）家族、三种严重程度、每种10个随机种子。

Result: 在7,449个候选变更中，带闸门环路仅准入144个，均未在留出历史上加剧错误；在所有低、中严重度单元中把假阳性率恢复到oracle水平且未提高漏报。若将闸门替换为无界系统所用的检查（仅看近期轨迹中可见的少数错误），同样环路准入309个有害变更，90次运行中有49次漏报率超过10%——假阳性下降源于筛选被放宽。在偏移前标签上评估时，闸门拒绝了每一个候选，说明重新解释必须被编码为重标历史的规则。参数性与范围性偏移可被局部修复，结构性偏移只能靠原语替换修复；在最高结构性严重度下，闸门固定的容差在半数种子中阻止了正确的替换。研究还将机制映射到欧盟《人工智能法案》对高风险信用评分的条款，并指出2026年4月的美国模型风险指引将智能体式AI排除在其范围之外。

Conclusion: 将自我演化约束在运行时harness、冻结权重，是实现可审查自适应的可行前提；哈希链准入闸门能有效阻止有害变更并维持漏报控制，但固定容差在最高结构性严重度下会成为修复瓶颈，且重新解释必须以重标规则形式编码。

Abstract: Self-improving LLM agents can adapt a credit pipeline to a changed rule, but an agent that rewrites itself destroys the artefact a supervisor reviews: a named change, a recorded test, an approval. We argue that self-evolution is reviewable only if it is confined to the runtime harness (instruction text, tool-call logic and primitive composition) while model weights stay fixed, so that every adaptation is a diff with a cause and a test attached. We give a dual-loop engine built on that bound, with one admission gate that writes a hash-chained record before deployment, and we measure the gate in simulation, with a simulated agent and a seeded-search proposer rather than language models. Across three families of supervisory re-interpretation at three severities, 10 seeds each, the gated loop admitted 144 of 7,449 candidate changes, none of which worsened error on held-out history, and restored the false-positive rate to the oracle level without raising missed flags in every low- and mid-severity cell. With the gate replaced by the check an unbounded system applies (fewer errors visible in recent traces), the same loops admitted 309 harmful changes and left missed flags above 10% in 49 of 90 runs: false positives fell because the screen was loosened. Evaluated on pre-shift labels, the gate rejected every candidate, so a re-interpretation must be encoded as a rule that relabels history. Parametric and scope shifts were repaired locally, a structural one only by primitive replacement; at the highest structural severity the gate's fixed tolerance blocked the correct replacement in half the seeds. We map the mechanisms to the EU AI Act's provisions for high-risk credit scoring and note that the April 2026 US model-risk guidance excludes agentic AI from its scope.

</details>


### [230] [Speaking the Navigator's Language: Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translation for Frozen Aerial VLN Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10635)
*Xi Chen,Zhe Liu,Xiaogang Xu,Jiafei Xu,Chunyi Zhou,Yuan Su,Rui Zeng,Tianyu Du,Kelu Yao,Chao Li,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出TGIT（轨迹锚定指令翻译器），在不微调导航器的前提下，将用户简短意图指令翻译为导航器可执行的详细指令，从而弥合航空视觉语言导航中的"指令鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中视觉语言导航（Aerial VLN）智能体通常在细节丰富、与轨迹对齐的指令上训练，而真实用户发出的是简短、意图驱动的指令。这种"指令鸿沟"导致在冻结的OpenFly导航器上成功率（SR）从31.03%骤降至11.33%，严重限制实际部署。

Method: 1) 用风格示例提示语言模型，将原始指令转换为成对的、以意图为中心的Weak指令，以规模化生成翻译器训练数据；2) 提出轨迹锚定指令翻译器（TGIT），作为前端模块保持导航器冻结，通过从导航器的轨迹结果中学习，将Weak输入翻译为可执行指令。

Result: Weak指令训练的翻译器将Weak输入SR提升至37.93%；零样本迁移到真实人类指令（11.33%→32.51%）；在留出的OpenFly上提升（4.95%→20.79%）；并在CityNav与AirVLN上均取得性能恢复。

Conclusion: TGIT以冻结导航器+可训练前端翻译器的解耦范式，有效弥合指令鸿沟，兼具对弱指令、真实人类指令及跨数据集的可迁移性。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically trained on detail-rich, trajectory-aligned commands, whereas users issue short, intent-driven instructions; on a frozen OpenFly navigator, this \emph{instruction gap} drops success rate (SR) from $31.03\%$ to $11.33\%$. To scale translator training, we prompt a language model with human-written style examples to convert original commands into paired, intent-centered Weak commands, which yield $15.27\%$ SR. We introduce the \textbf{Trajectory-Grounded Instruction Translator (TGIT)}, a front-end that keeps the navigator frozen and translates Weak inputs into agent-executable commands by learning from its trajectory outcomes. The resulting Weak-trained translator raises Weak-input SR to $37.93\%$ and transfers zero-shot to real human instructions ($11.33\%{\rightarrow}32.51\%$); it also improves held-out OpenFly ($4.95\%{\rightarrow}20.79\%$) and yields recovery on CityNav and AirVLN.

</details>


### [231] [Plan-and-Patch: Diffusion Language Models for Agentic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10786)
*Syamantak Kumar,Jiang Guo,Hassan Hamad,Hideo Kobayashi,Yi Xiang,Yezhou Yang,Yanjun Qi,Daniele Bonadiman,Jiarong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Plan-and-Patch框架，用扩散语言模型生成结构化计划并通过填充受影响区域进行局部修复，在计划修复成功率和生成速度上优于自回归模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程智能体执行需协调子目标、工具使用和中间结果，但环境变化、工具异常或动作失败会使计划假设失效，因此智能体不仅要生成计划还需修正计划；而修正通常只影响局部，整体重新生成会带来不必要的改动。

Method: Plan-and-Patch是一种"规划-执行"框架：扩散语言模型（dLLM）通过并行解掩码生成程序式结构化计划；修复时以保留的前缀和后缀为条件，仅重新生成受影响的区域，保持周围步骤不变。实验对比DreamReasoner-8B（扩散）与Qwen3-8B（自回归）作为规划器。

Result: 在无任务特定训练的Natural Plan任务上，扩散模型计划修复成功率为53.7%，接近自回归模型（27.0%）的两倍；在ALFWorld和TextCraft上进行任务特定训练后，两者计划生成成功率相近，但扩散模型将平均计划生成延迟降低39-46%。

Conclusion: Plan-and-Patch为长时程智能体提供了更快的计划生成和有效的计划修复框架，局部修复机制结合扩散模型的并行生成能力具有明显优势。

Abstract: Planning is increasingly important for long-horizon agents, where successful execution requires coordinating subgoals, tool use, and intermediate outcomes over many steps. Yet assumptions made during planning may be invalidated by the environment, tools may return unexpected results, or actions may fail. Effective agents must therefore not only generate plans, but also revise them. Such revisions often affect only part of a plan, leaving the preceding and subsequent structure intact. Rather than regenerate the entire plan and risk unnecessary changes, repair can regenerate the affected region conditioned on the preserved prefix and suffix. We introduce Plan-and-Patch, a plan-and-act framework in which a diffusion language model (dLLM) generates a structured, program-like plan through parallel unmasking and repairs it by filling in selected regions while keeping the surrounding steps fixed. We compare DreamReasoner-8B and Qwen3-8B as diffusion and autoregressive (AR) planners. On Natural Plan without task-specific training, diffusion (53.7%) achieves nearly twice the plan repair success rate of AR (27.0%). After task-specific training on agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and TextCraft, the planners achieve similar observed success in plan generation, while diffusion reduces mean plan-generation latency by 39-46% relative to AR. Our results show that Plan-and-Patch provides a framework for faster plan generation and effective plan repair in long-horizon agents.

</details>


### [232] [Whose Ground Truth? Embracing Ambiguity in Human-Centered AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10805)
*Jingyao Wu,Mohammad Tariqul Islam,Per Rådberg Nagbøl,Julie Gerlings,Giovanni Leoni,Georgiana Fryza,Lars Pilegaard Thomsen,Jakob Mainz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这是一篇立场论文，主张AI研究应从追求单一"标准答案"转向对人类解释空间的建模，并区分有意义的歧义与标注噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统越来越多地与人类互动并做出关乎人的决策，理解人类的解释方式成为发展"以人为本AI"的重要环节。然而传统机器学习与AI大多假设存在唯一的确定真值，将人类标注的差异性通过聚合消除或视为噪声。对于许多以人为本的任务，人类解释本身固有歧义，同一输入的多种解释可能同时合理有效，将其压缩为单一目标会丢失人类感知、判断与经验多样性的有意义信息。

Method: 作为立场论文，本文通过论证提出研究议程：呼吁转向对"可能人类判断的解释空间"进行建模，同时区分有意义的歧义与标注噪声。论文围绕AI系统的表示、学习、评估、部署与治理等环节，阐述该视角应如何指导研究与实践。

Result: 本文未报告实验或实证结果，属于观点性论述：主张采用能够刻画解释空间（而非单一真值）的建模范式，并在评估与治理中纳入歧义的考量，以支持更能反映人类解释多样性的以人为本AI。

Conclusion: AI研发应从假设唯一确定真值、消除标注差异的范式，转向建模合理解释的空间并区分真实歧义与噪声；这一视角应贯穿AI的表示、学习、评估、部署与治理，从而构建更以人为本、更尊重人类解释多样性的AI系统。

Abstract: As AI systems increasingly interact with people and make decisions about them, understanding human interpretations becomes an important part of developing human-centered AI. Conventional machine learning and AI systems are largely developed under the assumption that a single definitive ground truth exists, with variability in human annotations often resolved through aggregation or treated as noise. However, for many human-centered tasks, human interpretation is inherently ambiguous, and multiple interpretations of the same input may be simultaneously reasonable and valid. Reducing such ambiguity to a single target risks overlooking meaningful information about the diversity of human perception, judgment, and experience. In this position paper, we call for a shift towards modeling the interpretation space of plausible human judgments, while distinguishing meaningful ambiguity from annotation noise. We argue that this perspective should guide how AI systems are represented, learned, evaluated, deployed, and governed, supporting more human-centered AI that better reflects the diversity of human interpretation.

</details>


### [233] [On the Clock: Towards Punctual and Productive Time-Budgeted AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10833)
*Aaron Wang,Neelabh Madan,Vlad Sobal,Matthew Trager,Michael Kleinman,Elman Mansimov,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究考察小型LLM智能体在显式wall-clock时间预算下能否既遵守运行时限制又高效利用时间。通过在MLE-Bench Lite（Qwen3.6-27B）和Zork I（Qwen3-4B）上评估，发现仅靠提示声明预算时智能体无法有效控制时间；引入harness计时反馈与截止强制机制可显著提升预算遵守度，RL（GRPO）在Zork I上实现近乎完美的预算遵守并能泛化到未见预算。但遵守预算后，智能体仍无法将额外时间转化为性能提升，且多预算GRPO训练易坍缩至最短预算策略，揭示"时间遵守"与"生产性时间分配"之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体基准中额外计算时间可实质性提升性能，但智能体能否在明确wall-clock预算下既遵守时限又有效利用时间尚不清楚。作者希望探究小型LLM智能体在时间预算下的行为缺陷及其成因（时间感知缺失、行动时长难以预判、缺乏从可用时间到策略的映射），并寻找可行的干预手段。

Method: 在两个agentic基准上评估：MLE-Bench Lite的五个竞赛任务（Qwen3.6-27B）与Zork I/Jericho（Qwen3-4B）。设置两类互补干预：(1) harness层面机制，暴露计时信息并强制截止；(2) 带预算感知奖励的强化学习（GRPO）。对比仅提示声明预算的基线设置。

Result: 仅提示声明预算时，智能体无法将预算转化为受控的时间使用。harness注入计时信息显著改善Qwen3.6-27B的预算遵守度且无明显性能损失，强制钩子进一步提升遵守度。GRPO在Zork I上实现近乎完美的预算遵守并泛化到未见预算，但在MLE-Bench上未超越未训练harness的任务表现。智能体遵守预算后仍无法利用额外时间提升性能：RL策略学会何时停止，却常以重复动作填充剩余时间；多预算GRPO训练倾向于坍缩到最短预算所学策略。

Conclusion: 时间遵守与生产性时间分配之间存在显著鸿沟，这是预算条件化智能体面临的核心挑战。仅靠harness计时反馈或RL训练可解决遵守问题，但无法保证额外时间被有效利用。

Abstract: We study whether small LLM agents can operate effectively under explicit wall-clock time budgets by both respecting the allocated runtime and using available time productively. We evaluate Qwen3.6-27B on five competitions from MLE-Bench Lite and Qwen3-4B on Zork I (Jericho), two agentic benchmarks where additional computational time can meaningfully improve performance. In the simplest setting, where the budget is stated only in the prompt, agents fail to translate the stated budget into controlled use of time. These failures arise from gaps in time awareness, since the harness provides no timing feedback, but also because they cannot reliably anticipate the duration of actions, and do not have a learned mapping from available time to an appropriate strategy. We investigate two complementary classes of interventions: harness-based mechanisms that expose timing information and enforce deadlines, and reinforcement learning with budget-aware rewards. Injecting timing information through the harness substantially improves budget adherence for Qwen3.6-27B without measurable loss in performance, while enforcement hooks tighten adherence further. RL with GRPO achieves near-perfect budget adherence on Zork I and generalizes to held-out budgets not seen during training, but does not improve task performance over the untrained harness on MLE-Bench. Once agents are made to respect the budget, they still fail to use additional time to improve task performance. RL-trained policies learn when to stop but often fill extra time with repeated actions, and GRPO training on multiple budgets tends to collapse toward the strategy learned for the shortest budget. Our results reveal a gap between time adherence and productive time allocation, which remains a central challenge for budget-conditioned agents.

</details>


### [234] [Self-Supervised Keyframe Discovery for Horizon-Invariant Behavior Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10857)
*Prabin Kumar Rath,Omkar Patil,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Keyframe Mnemonics自监督方法，通过从随机采样历史观察中学习目标并作为奖励筛选关键帧（mnemonics），再训练以关键帧为条件的BC策略，在非马尔可夫长程记忆任务中实现无限视野上下文保持与强泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非马尔可夫环境下的行为克隆（BC）需要策略在长视野上推理上下文信息。现有策略架构依赖循环或注意力机制捕获长期依赖：循环模型存在隐状态塌缩与BPTT梯度不稳定问题，注意力模型受上下文长度根本限制。因此需要一种能在无限视野下保留决策相关上下文、同时保持紧凑工作记忆的方法。

Method: 提出Keyframe Mnemonics，一种自监督方法：先在随机采样的历史观察上学习一个目标函数，将其作为奖励用于关键帧（mnemonics）选择，从而自动"发现"信息量关键的历史观察；随后训练一个以所发现关键帧为条件的BC策略来建模动作分布。在特定任务结构假设下，该形式化方法提供无限视野的上下文保持保证，并使策略工作记忆中仅保留少量决策相关关键帧。

Result: 在合成记忆域上，mnemonic条件化BC策略达到100%成功率（SR），并泛化到比训练长数个数量级的视野而无性能退化。在记忆密集型机器人操作基准上，跨23个任务相对最强基线平均绝对SR提升13.9%；在真实机器人上于20倍更长视野下仍保持80% SR。

Conclusion: Keyframe Mnemonics通过自监督关键帧发现与关键帧条件化BC，有效缓解了循环模型的隐状态塌缩和梯度不稳定及注意力模型的上下文长度限制，在无限视野上下文保持与长程泛化上表现优异，适用于合成记忆域与真实机器人长视野操作任务。

Abstract: Behavior cloning (BC) in non-Markovian environments is a challenging problem because policies have to reason over contextual information over long horizons. Existing policy architectures rely on recurrent or attention-based mechanisms to capture long-term dependencies. However, recurrent models suffer from hidden-state collapse and gradient instability under backpropagation through time, while attention-based models are fundamentally limited by context length. To address these issues, we propose Keyframe Mnemonics, a novel self-supervised method that $\textit{discovers}$ a set of information-critical observations ($\textit{mnemonics}$) by learning an objective from randomly sampled past observations and using it as a reward for keyframe selection. We then train a BC policy that conditions on the discovered keyframes to model the action distribution. Under certain task-structure assumptions, our formulation provides context retention guarantees over an infinite horizon, while maintaining a small set of decision-relevant keyframes in the policy's working memory. We evaluate our method on synthetic memory domains, where mnemonic-conditioned BC policies achieve $100$% success rates (SR) and generalize to horizons orders of magnitude beyond training without performance degradation. Additionally, we evaluate on memory-intensive robot manipulation benchmark, achieving a $13.9$% average absolute SR improvement over the strongest baseline across $23$ tasks and retaining $80$% SR at $20\times$ longer horizons on a real robot. Code and videos are available at https://keyframe-mnemonics.github.io.

</details>


### [235] [Reading the Room: Foundations, Design, and Challenges of Normative Competence in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10906)
*Andrea Wynn,Harsh Satija,Seokhyun,Baek,Anqi Liu,Eric Nalisnick,Gillian K. Hadfield*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出多智能体社区辩论环境以研究LLM的"规范能力"，发现基线LLM即使在学习规范能提升准确率时也无法学会规范，规范模块效果高度依赖规范风格与模型能力，且存在无选择性归因失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社区受规范系统治理，AI对齐需适应数量庞大、变化快且常具任意性的规范，因此需要"规范能力"，即仅从交互中推断社区所执行的规范，而不依赖静态预训练知识。作者希望将规范能力与预训练暴露分离从而单独研究。

Method: 提出多智能体社区辩论设置，辩论准入受合成规范控制；测试基线LLM智能体；实验多种"规范模块"（用于规范推断的架构组件）；并在真实规范伴随特异性非规范性行为时观察归因行为。

Result: 基线LLM智能体无法学会规范，即使遵循规范能提升其准确率；规范遵循对规范风格和规范模块所用模型高度敏感，缺乏泛化性；面对特异性、非规范行为时，LLM表现出无选择性归因失败，会不加区分地模仿噪声行为，即使明确惩罚此类模仿仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在LLM中操作化并评估规范能力，表明当前AI系统擅长行为模仿，但缺乏辨别社会所执行秩序的能力。

Abstract: Human communities are governed by normative systems: shared standards that produce \textit{norms} dictating acceptable behavior, enforced through community sanctioning. Aligning increasingly autonomous AI systems with these norms is a central alignment challenge, complicated by the fact that norms are vast in number, change quickly, and are often arbitrary (e.g., dress or language conventions). Thus, alignment requires \textit{normative competence}: the ability to discern from interaction alone what norms a community enforces without relying on static pretrained knowledge. We introduce a multi-agent community debate setting, where access to debate is governed by synthetic norms, to study normative competence in isolation from pretraining exposure. We show that baseline LLM agents fail to learn norms even when doing so would improve their accuracy. We then experiment with various \textit{normative modules} -- architectural components for norm inference -- finding that norm-following is highly sensitive to both the style of norm and the model powering the normative module, suggesting a lack of generalizability. Furthermore, when idiosyncratic, non-normative behaviors accompany the true norm, LLM agents exhibit an unselective attribution failure: they indiscriminately copy idiosyncratic noise alongside enforced rules, a pattern that persists even when imitating unnecessary behaviors is explicitly penalized. To the best of our knowledge, our work is the first to operationalize and evaluate normative competence in LLMs, demonstrating that current AI systems excel at behavioral mimicry but lack the capacity to discern socially enforced order.

</details>


### [236] [StoreBench: A Live-Commerce Environment for Evaluating and Training Autonomous Operator Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10942)
*Daksh Raghuvanshi,Ved Vedere,Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 StoreBench：一个生产级电商后端支撑的实时商业环境，用于评测 LLM 智能体的长时程规划与经济决策能力，并以脚本策略、人类专家和大规模后训练实验进行多维校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准多为静态：世界仅在智能体行动时变化、奖励仅为终局判定、通过门槛任意设定。这使得 LLM 后训练中的强化学习环境难以真实反映长时程规划与不确定性下的经济判断能力，需要更贴近真实动态世界且可复现、抗奖励攻击的评测环境。

Method: 构建 StoreBench：智能体通过 29 个与人类运营者相同的商户工具经营中型在线服装店；客户全天候下单，供应商调价与违约，市场冲击部分或完全无预警到来。采用窗口化操作预算，使模拟时间由动作数决定而非模型延迟；通过门槛以脚本锚定策略校准，奖励经奖励黑客清单加固，给定动作序列可完全复现。评测 7 个前沿 LLM，覆盖 11 个场景（30–45 天及完整模拟年）、3 个世界种子、匹配推理强度；并开展 GRPO 后训练实验（Qwen3.5-27B，仅 5 个不相交任务）。

Result: 无模型平均达到脚本 smart-triage 策略水平：最佳模型 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 在任务-种子单元通过率 49%，启发式策略为 97%。人类专家使用相同工具与预算超过所有模型（复合均值 0.708 vs. 0.700）。在 Claude Code harness 下完整模拟年中多数模型表现大幅提升。GRPO 后训练中，Qwen3.5-27B 在留出评测任务上的复合均值从 0.136 提升至 0.373。

Conclusion: StoreBench 提供了动态、可复现、抗刷分的实时商业智能体评测环境，揭示当前前沿 LLM 与人类专家及启发式策略的显著差距，同时验证后训练可有效提升表现；公开 5 个训练任务、10 条示例轨迹及评分验证工具，完整环境与评测套件暂不公开以防污染。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning environments are now a primary lever for improving large language model (LLM) capabilities in post-training, yet most agentic benchmarks remain static: the world moves only when the agent acts, the reward is a terminal verdict, and the pass bar is set arbitrarily. We introduce StoreBench, a live-commerce environment in which an agent runs a mid-size online apparel store on a production-grade commerce backend, testing long-horizon planning and economic judgment under uncertainty. Customers order around the clock, suppliers reprice and fail, and market shocks arrive with partial or no warning. The agent acts through the same 29 merchant tools a human operator would use, under a windowed operation budget that makes simulated time a function of actions taken, so model latency cannot influence simulated time. Pass thresholds are calibrated against scripted anchor policies, the reward is hardened against a catalogue of reward hacks, and every episode replays identically given a sequence of actions. We evaluate seven frontier LLMs on 11 scenarios of 30 to 45 days and a full simulated year, over three world seeds at matched reasoning effort. No model matches the scripted smart-triage policy on average: the best, DeepSeek-V4-Pro, passes 49% of task-seed cells against the heuristic's 97%. Human experts working through the same tools and budgets outscore every model (mean composite 0.708 vs. 0.700). Over a full simulated year under the Claude Code harness, most models show dramatic performance improvement. In a GRPO post-training run, Qwen3.5-27B trained on only five disjoint tasks raises its mean composite on the held-out evaluation tasks from 0.136 to 0.373. We release five example training-split tasks, ten sample trajectories, and the scoring and verification tooling; the full environment and evaluation suite are withheld to keep the benchmark uncontaminated.

</details>


### [237] [RIT-RAG: Navigating Document Corpora with Retrieval-Induced Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11370)
*Meghanadh Pulivarthi,Swaraj Kumar Biswal,Kushagra Bhushan,Yatin Nandwani,Sachindra Joshi,Dinesh Raghu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 RIT-RAG，将内容检索与结构导航结合：离线为每个文档建树，查询时检索chunk并诱导子树叶节点，由LLM agent导航读取，在多领域基准上取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agentic RAG 暴露的是无文档结构的孤立 chunk，难以区分真正相关证据与仅表面相似的片段；结构感知方法（如 PageIndex）难以扩展到大语料，且先锁定单一文档，选错后无法恢复。

Method: 离线阶段基于文档目录或站点地图为每篇文档构建树结构；查询阶段先检索一批 chunk，利用其位置诱导出可管理的子树（可跨多文档），再由 LLM agent 导航这些子树、选择性阅读有希望的节点并在需要时重写查询。检索负责“去哪看”，agent 负责“读什么”。

Result: 在金融、科学和客服等基准上，RIT-RAG 在 vanilla、graph-based 与 agentic 基线中取得最高答案准确率。在新构建的 EntQABench（284 万技术文档网页）上，对三个 LLM 相比最强基线提升 6.8 至 11.4 个点。

Conclusion: 将内容检索的广度与文档结构导航的精度相结合，可有效缓解单一文档锁定问题，并在大规模语料上实现可扩展、可恢复的 RAG。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds language models in external corpora. Agentic RAG enables iterative search, yet exposes the model to isolated chunks without document structure, making it difficult to distinguish relevant evidence from chunks that merely resemble the query. Structure-aware methods such as PageIndex navigate document structure but cannot scale to the structures of large corpora, which do not fit in the LLM context. Hence, they first commit to a single document using a document retriever and cannot recover from a wrong choice. We propose RIT-RAG (Retrieval-Induced Tree RAG), which combines content retrieval with structural navigation. Offline, RIT-RAG builds a tree for each document from its table of contents or sitemap. At query time, it retrieves a broad set of chunks and uses their positions to induce manageable sub-trees, potentially across multiple documents. An LLM agent navigates these sub-trees, selectively reads promising nodes, and reformulates queries when needed. Thus, retrieval proposes where to look, while the agent decides what to read. Across financial, scientific, and customer-support benchmarks, RIT-RAG achieves the highest answer accuracy among vanilla, graph-based, and agentic baselines. On EntQABench, our new benchmark of 2.84 million technical-documentation webpages, it improves accuracy by 6.8 to 11.4 points over the strongest baseline across three LLMs.

</details>


### [238] [Learning How to Search for Plans with Exponentially Less Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10954)
*Dominik Drexler,Simon Ståhlberg,Markus Fritzsche,Blai Bonet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出用索引式策略（indexical policy）学习搜索控制，通过引入 choose 规则限制真实选择点，证明结构终止性可将执行长度多项式界于对象数，从而在多项式空间内完成规划；结合语言模型的反例引导学习，在IPC 2023学习赛道和Autoscale Agile套件上解出1,709/1,890任务，超越LAMA、BFWS与Levitron。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 启发式搜索即使启发函数近乎完美，也可能存储指数级状态。作者希望用“学习搜索控制”替代状态搜索，为每个领域学习一份通用策略，避免访问状态列表带来的指数空间开销。

Method: 方法是学习一种索引式策略（每领域一份规范）：策略含寄存器（保存对象）与模式（对规则排序）。新增 choose 规则把对象载入寄存器并标记一个回溯点，该点只需一个候选即可；其余规则必须对其所有结果都成立、无需搜索。用语言模型在反例引导循环中学习这类策略，循环中认证结构终止、验证训练任务并保持选择深度较小。随后用深度优先过程执行策略。

Result: 主要结果为：结构终止性（排除无限执行）同时把每条执行长度界定为对象数的多项式。于是深度优先过程在多项式空间内找到规划，无需访问状态列表，代价是时间仅在选择深度（执行中真实选择数）上指数。因此该策略可解的问题类属于 NP；选择深度为常数时属于 P。实验中，学习到的策略在IPC 2023学习赛道与Autoscale Agile套件的1,890个测试任务中解出1,709个，多于 LAMA、BFWS 和 Levitron，且多数在一秒内、100 MiB 内完成。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过学习具有有界选择深度的索引式策略，可在多项式空间内实现规划并给出 NP/P 的理论刻画，同时实验性能超过经典规划器。

Abstract: Heuristic search for a plan can store exponentially many states, even when its heuristic is almost perfect. We instead learn search control, one specification per domain, written as an indexical policy: a generalized policy with registers that hold objects and modes that sequence its rules. We add the choose rule, which loads an object into a register and marks a backtracking point, where one candidate suffices; every other rule must work for all of its outcomes and needs no search. Our main result is that structural termination, which rules out infinite executions, also bounds every execution by a polynomial in the number of objects. A depth-first procedure then finds a plan in polynomial space, however large the state space, with no list of visited states. The cost is time, exponential only in the choice depth, the number of real choices along an execution. Any class that such a policy solves therefore lies in NP, and in P at constant choice depth. We learn these policies with a language model in a counterexample-guided loop that certifies termination, verifies the training tasks, and keeps the choice depth small. With the learned policies, the procedure solves 1,709 of 1,890 test tasks of the IPC 2023 Learning Track and the Autoscale Agile suite, more than LAMA, BFWS, and Levitron, and most of them within one second and 100 MiB.

</details>


### [239] [How Narrative Wrapping Affects LLM Refusal: A Cross-Language Benchmark and Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11005)
*Zhankai Ye,Yanning Wang,Yukai Jin,Bo Mei,Fangyi Li,Wei Wang,Shangqian Gao,Xin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究角色扮演/叙事包装绕过安全对齐的漏洞，构建GUISE基准并提出AXIS方法，在多模型上取得最佳安全-可用性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的语言模型常拒绝直接的有害请求，但对放在角色扮演或叙事包装中的同一请求却会作答。论文旨在跨语言和语体系统测量这一漏洞，并从表征层面理解其机制。

Method: 构建GUISE基准：包含英语、现代汉语、文言文的平行请求，匹配的有害-良性配对，留出评估用的包装类型，并将"先警告后作答"计为攻击成功的更严格判定标准。进行表征分析，发现语言和语体会使有害请求表征轻微偏离模型的拒绝方向，而叙事包装则使其大幅偏离。据此提出AXIS方法，结合偏好优化、将有害请求表征与拒绝方向对齐的旋转目标，以及训练模型完全拒绝而非先警告后作答的承诺目标。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B上，攻击成功率英语为89.4%，现代汉语为93.0%，文言文达95.7%。在Qwen3-1.7B、Qwen3-4B和GLM-4-9B上，AXIS在所比较方法中取得最高的安全与可用性综合得分。

Conclusion: 语言和语体包装对拒绝方向的偏离较小，叙事包装偏离更大，揭示了漏洞的表征机制；AXIS通过表征旋转与承诺训练有效提升安全对齐，并在多模型上实现最佳安全-可用性权衡。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models often refuse a harmful request stated directly but answer the same request inside a role-play or narrative wrapper. We measure this vulnerability across languages and registers: attack success on Qwen3-1.7B is already 89.4% in English and 93.0% in modern Chinese, and reaches 95.7% in Classical Chinese. We build GUISE, a benchmark for systematically studying this vulnerability. It includes parallel requests in English, modern Chinese, and Classical Chinese, matched harmful and benign pairs, wrapper types held out for evaluation, and a stricter criterion that counts warn-then-answer responses as attack successes. Representation analysis shows that language and register move harmful-request representations only slightly away from the model's refusal direction, whereas narrative wrappers move them much farther away. We propose AXIS, which combines preference optimisation with a rotation objective that aligns harmful-request representations with the refusal direction and a commitment objective that trains the model to refuse completely rather than produce a warn-then-answer response. Across Qwen3-1.7B, Qwen3-4B and GLM-4-9B, AXIS achieves the highest combined safety and usability score among the compared methods.

</details>


### [240] [Curating Always-Loaded Context for LLM Agents: A Capacitated Assortment Model with Censored Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.11007)
*Zexuan Liu,Yuning Yang,Tiancheng Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM代理上下文文件（如AGENTS.md）的策展形式化为带容量约束的选品问题，证明最优文件大小存在上界、盲目追加可任意劣于最优子集选择，并在反馈截断（censored feedback）条件下给出增删指令的决策依据与遗憾界，实证表明无关规则会降低模型合规性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理每轮会话都需重新加载固定的上下文文件，加载的每个token在后续每轮均被重复计费，且文件增大会导致性能退化。然而实践中人工或自动策展者通常采用"追加"方式扩充文件，缺乏对增删取舍的理论指导，因此需要为上下文策展建立形式化框架。

Method: 将上下文策展建模为带容量约束的选品问题（capacitated assortment problem）：指令消耗有限注意力容量下的token，增加一条指令不会提升其他指令的合规性，保留下来的指令产生每会话的固定设置成本。在此框架下推导最优文件大小的上界，比较"全部追加"与最优子集选择的净价值差异，分析token预算与token价格低估情形下的损失界限。进一步研究基于历史会话的学习：反馈具有截断性（censored feedback）——已加载指令的收益与危害可观测，而未加载指令仅在其缺失造成损害时才产生反馈。在此设定下分析删除被代理忽略的指令是否必然移除有用指令、追加指令前需要收集多少证据，并在人类审查者每期仅能检查有限次编辑的约束下给出遗憾界。

Result: 证明了三项理论结果：（1）无论候选指令数量多少，最优文件大小存在上界；（2）对所有具有正独立价值的指令全部追加，其净价值可任意劣于选择最优子集；（3）token预算可限制token价格被低估时的损失。在截断反馈设定下证明删除被忽略指令不可避免会移除有用指令，并刻画了追加指令所需证据量及有限编辑审查下的遗憾界。实证实验显示，从真实上下文文件中抽取的无关规则会降低语言模型的合规性。

Conclusion: 上下文文件不宜盲目追加，而应在注意力容量和token成本约束下做最优子集选择；截断反馈使得删除决策存在固有风险，需在收集充分证据后再增删指令。该工作为LLM代理上下文策展提供了理论边界与可操作的决策准则，并指出无关规则对合规性的实际危害。
